### [1] [Geodesics and Thermodynamics of a Schwarzschild Black Hole with Hernquist Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24046)
*A. R. P. Moreira,A. Bouzenada,F. M. Belchior,F. C. E. Lima*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper studies a Schwarzschild black hole embedded in a Hernquist dark matter halo, analyzing how the halo alters photon trajectories (via null geodesics), thermodynamic properties (mass, Hawking temperature, entropy, etc.), and scalar field perturbations. The results show nontrivial deviations from the standard vacuum Schwarzschild solution due to dark matter effects.
\n\n
本文研究了嵌入在Hernquist暗物质晕中的Schwarzschild黑洞，分析该晕如何通过零测地线改变光子轨迹、影响热力学性质（质量、Hawking温度、熵等）以及标量场微扰。结果表明，暗物质效应导致系统在光学、热力学和微扰性质上显著偏离标准真空Schwarzschild解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand how realistic astrophysical environments—specifically dark matter halos modeled by the Hernquist profile—affect fundamental black hole properties such as photon dynamics, thermodynamics, and stability. Standard Schwarzschild solutions assume vacuum spacetime, but real black holes likely reside in dark matter-rich regions, necessitating a more realistic metric construction.
\n\n
动机在于理解真实的天体物理环境——特别是由Hernquist轮廓建模的暗物质晕——如何影响黑洞的基本性质，如光子动力学、热力学和稳定性。标准Schwarzschild解假设真空时空，但真实黑洞很可能位于富含暗物质的区域，因此需要构建更符合实际的度规。

Method: The authors construct a modified Schwarzschild metric by incorporating the Hernquist dark matter density profile $\\rho(r) = \\frac{M_{\\text{halo}}}{2\\pi} \\frac{a}{r(r+a)^3}$ into the stress-energy tensor, effectively treating dark matter as a static, spherically symmetric fluid. They solve the null geodesic equations to study light bending, derive horizon conditions and thermodynamic quantities (e.g., Hawking temperature $T_H = \\frac{\\kappa}{2\\pi}$ with surface gravity $\\kappa$ modified by dark matter), and analyze scalar perturbations via the Klein-Gordon equation in the modified background.
\n\n
作者通过将Hernquist暗物质密度轮廓 $\\rho(r) = \\frac{M_{\\text{halo}}}{2\\pi} \\frac{a}{r(r+a)^3}$ 引入应力-能量张量，构建了一个修正的Schwarzschild度规，将暗物质视为静态球对称流体。他们求解零测地线方程以研究光线偏折，推导视界条件和热力学量（例如Hawking温度 $T_H = \\frac{\\kappa}{2\\pi}$，其中表面引力 $\\kappa$ 受暗物质修正），并通过Klein-Gordon方程在修正背景中分析标量微扰。

Result: The Hernquist dark matter halo induces measurable corrections: (1) photon trajectories are deformed compared to vacuum geodesics; (2) the event horizon location shifts, altering the black hole mass definition and reducing the Hawking temperature; (3) entropy and heat capacity are modified, affecting thermodynamic stability; (4) scalar perturbations exhibit altered quasinormal mode spectra due to the dark matter potential. All results deviate quantitatively from the pure Schwarzschild case.
\n\n
Hernquist暗物质晕引入了可观测的修正：(1) 光子轨迹相对于真空测地线发生形变；(2) 事件视界位置移动，改变了黑洞质量定义并降低Hawking温度；(3) 熵和热容被修正，影响热力学稳定性；(4) 标量微扰因暗物质势而表现出改变的准正规模谱。所有结果均与纯Schwarzschild情形存在定量偏差。

Conclusion: The presence of a Hernquist dark matter halo nontrivially modifies the geometric, optical, thermodynamic, and perturbative characteristics of a Schwarzschild black hole. This implies that astrophysical black holes in dark matter-dominated regions cannot be accurately described by vacuum solutions, and environmental effects must be incorporated for precision modeling of observables like shadow size, thermal emission, and gravitational wave ringdown.
\n\n
Hernquist暗物质晕的存在非平凡地修正了Schwarzschild黑洞的几何、光学、热力学和微扰特性。这意味着位于暗物质主导区域的天体物理黑洞无法用真空解准确描述，必须纳入环境效应，以精确建模诸如阴影尺寸、热辐射和引力波铃宕等可观测量。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the physical and geometrical properties of a Schwarzschild black hole (BH) immersed in a Hernquist dark matter halo. To accomplish our purpose, one builds the BH metric by incorporating the Hernquist dark matter profile into the Schwarzschild geometry. In addition, we verify the null geodesic solutions and the Halo effect on photon dynamics. Within this framework, one examines the corresponding light trajectories to determine the deformation of photon paths generated by the dark matter distribution. Furthermore, the thermodynamic properties of the system are studied by deriving expressions for the black hole mass, the horizon condition, the Hawking temperature, the entropy, the Gibbs free energy, and the heat capacity. Our results show that the dark matter halo modifies the thermal structure and stability conditions of the black hole configuration. Finally, we investigate the scalar perturbations to examine the influence of the Hernquist halo on the dynamical propagation of scalar fields in the BH background. In this framework, the results obtained demonstrate that the dark matter parameters yield nontrivial corrections to the optical, thermodynamic, and perturbative properties of the Schwarzschild black hole, producing deviations from the standard vacuum solution.

</details>


### [2] [Hawking atmosphere of anti-de Sitter black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24091)
*A. F. Cardona,C. Molina*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper studies the semiclassical dynamics of Hawking radiation from evaporating spherically symmetric anti-de Sitter (adS) black holes, combining a quantum tunneling framework with backreaction and a renormalized energy-momentum tensor calculation in Vaidya-adS spacetime to reveal non-thermal luminosity behavior, especially for small adS black holes.
\n\n
本文研究了球对称反德西特（adS）黑洞蒸发过程中霍金辐射的半经典动力学，结合考虑反作用的量子隧穿框架与Vaidya-adS时空中的重整化能动张量计算，揭示了尤其对于小adS黑洞的非热光度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to understand how quantum effects—particularly Hawking radiation and its backreaction—influence the evaporation dynamics and near-horizon atmosphere of black holes in asymptotically anti-de Sitter spacetimes, where standard thermodynamic intuition may break down due to strong curvature and finite cavity-like boundary conditions.
\n\n
该研究的动机源于需要理解量子效应——特别是霍金辐射及其反作用——如何影响渐近反德西特时空中黑洞的蒸发动力学和近视界“大气”，在这些时空中，由于强曲率和类似有限腔体的边界条件，标准热力学直觉可能失效。

Method: The authors employ two complementary methods: (1) the Parikh-Wilczek quantum tunneling formalism, which accounts for energy conservation and backreaction by relating the emission probability $\\Gamma \\sim e^{\\Delta S_{\\text{BH}}}$ to the change in Bekenstein-Hawking entropy $\\Delta S_{\\text{BH}}$, enabling computation of time-dependent luminosity; and (2) calculation of the renormalized energy-momentum tensor $\\langle T_{\\mu\
u} \\rangle_{\\text{ren}}$ for a quantum field in the dynamical Vaidya-adS geometry that models mass loss due to evaporation.
\n\n
作者采用了两种互补方法：(1) Parikh-Wilczek量子隧穿形式体系，通过将发射概率 $\\Gamma \\sim e^{\\Delta S_{\\text{BH}}}$ 与贝肯斯坦-霍金熵变 $\\Delta S_{\\text{BH}}$ 相关联，从而计入能量守恒与反作用，并计算时间依赖的光度；(2) 在描述因蒸发导致质量损失的动态Vaidya-adS几何中，计算量子场的重整化能动张量 $\\langle T_{\\mu\
u} \\rangle_{\\text{ren}}$。

Result: The time-dependent luminosity derived from the tunneling approach deviates significantly from blackbody predictions: for small adS black holes, luminosity does not increase monotonically with temperature because rapid mass loss suppresses emission. The renormalized $\\langle T_{\\mu\
u} \\rangle_{\\text{ren}}$ in Vaidya-adS geometry corroborates this picture, showing how quantum fields respond self-consistently to the evolving geometry and confirming the breakdown of equilibrium thermodynamics during evaporation.
\n\n
由隧穿方法导出的时间依赖光度显著偏离黑体辐射预言：对于小adS黑洞，由于快速的质量损失抑制了辐射，其光度并不随温度单调增加。Vaidya-adS几何中的重整化 $\\langle T_{\\mu\
u} \\rangle_{\\text{ren}}$ 支持这一图像，表明量子场如何自洽地响应演化几何，并证实了蒸发过程中平衡热力学的失效。

Conclusion: The interplay between semiclassical geometry, quantum field theory, and black hole thermodynamics governs the structure of the Hawking atmosphere in adS spacetimes. Backreaction and non-equilibrium effects are essential for accurately modeling evaporation, particularly for small black holes where adS boundary conditions amplify deviations from ideal thermal behavior.
\n\n
在adS时空中，霍金“大气”的结构由半经典几何、量子场论与黑洞热力学之间的相互作用共同决定。反作用与非平衡效应对于准确建模蒸发过程至关重要，尤其对于小黑洞，adS边界条件会放大其对理想热行为的偏离。

Abstract: This work investigates the semiclassical evolution of the Hawking atmosphere surrounding evaporating, spherically symmetric anti-de Sitter (adS) black holes. We model the evaporation process within a dynamical framework, treating the emission of Hawking radiation as a quantum tunneling process through the black-hole horizon. Using the Parikh-Wilczek tunneling method, we incorporate backreaction effects, with the emission probability being linked to the resulting change in the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy of the black hole. This probability is then used to compute the time-dependent luminosity of the system, revealing significant deviations from ideal blackbody behavior, particularly for small adS black holes. For these objects, the luminosity does not increase with temperature due to strong mass variations during evaporation. To complement this microscopic approach, we compute the renormalized energy-momentum tensor for a quantum field propagating in the Vaidya-adS geometry modelling the evaporation process. Together, these approaches clarify the interplay between geometry, quantum fields, and thermodynamics in shaping the Hawking atmosphere and the evaporation dynamics of black holes in asymptotically adS spacetimes.

</details>


### [3] [Red/Blue shifts of photons in a Hayward black hole immersed in perfect fluid dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24102)
*L. Marlene. López,L. A. López*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper studies gravitational redshift and blueshift of photons from particles in stable circular orbits around a Hayward regular black hole surrounded by perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM). It finds that the PFDM parameter alters key orbital radii (e.g., ISCO, photon sphere) and modifies both frequency shifts and the black hole shadow size, with implications for observational tests using Sgr A*.
\n\n
本文研究了围绕被完美流体暗物质（PFDM）包围的Hayward正则黑洞稳定圆轨道上粒子所发射光子的引力红移与蓝移。研究发现，PFDM参数会改变关键轨道半径（如ISCO、光子球），并调制频率移动和黑洞阴影大小，这对利用Sgr A*进行观测检验具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to explore how the presence of perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) modifies strong-field gravitational effects near a regular black hole, specifically focusing on observable quantities such as photon frequency shifts and black hole shadow. These observables can potentially constrain or detect dark matter contributions in the vicinity of supermassive black holes like Sgr A*.
\n\n
动机在于探索完美流体暗物质（PFDM）如何修改正则黑洞附近的强场引力效应，特别关注光子频率移动和黑洞阴影等可观测量。这些观测量可能用于限制或探测超大质量黑洞（如Sgr A*）附近暗物质的贡献。

Method: The authors analyze geodesic motion of massive and massless particles in the spacetime of a Hayward black hole modified by a PFDM background. They compute the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO), photon sphere radius, and the corresponding gravitational redshift/blueshift using the conserved energy and angular momentum along timelike geodesics. The black hole shadow is calculated via the impact parameter at the photon sphere, and results are compared with Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) constraints on Sgr A*.
\n\n
作者分析了在由PFDM背景修正的Hayward黑洞时空中，有质量与无质量粒子的测地线运动。他们利用类时测地线上守恒的能量和角动量，计算了最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）、光子球半径及相应的引力红移/蓝移。黑洞阴影通过光子球处的撞击参数计算，并将结果与事件视界望远镜（EHT）对Sgr A*的观测限制进行比较。

Result: For nonzero PFDM parameter $\\lambda$, the redshift minimum increases relative to the pure Hayward case ($\\lambda = 0$), while the blueshift decreases. Positive $\\lambda$ shrinks the shadow radius, whereas negative $\\lambda$ enlarges it beyond the Schwarzschild value. The deviations in shadow size remain within current EHT observational bounds for moderate $|\\lambda|$, suggesting PFDM could be probed via high-precision strong-gravity observations.
\n\n
对于非零PFDM参数$\\lambda$，相对于纯Hayward情形（$\\lambda = 0$），红移最小值增大，而蓝移减小。正的$\\lambda$使阴影半径缩小，负的$\\lambda$则使其大于Schwarzschild值。对于中等大小的$|\\lambda|$，阴影尺寸的偏差仍在当前EHT观测限制范围内，表明可通过高精度强引力场观测探测PFDM。

Conclusion: Frequency shifts of photons from orbiting emitters and the angular size of the black hole shadow are sensitive probes of perfect fluid dark matter in the strong-field regime. The interplay between the Hayward regularization and PFDM leads to distinctive observational signatures that may be tested with future EHT-like observations of Sgr A* and other galactic centers.
\n\n
来自轨道辐射体的光子频率移动和黑洞阴影的角直径是探测强场区域中完美流体暗物质（PFDM）的灵敏探针。Hayward正则化与PFDM之间的相互作用产生了独特的观测特征，未来可通过类似EHT对Sgr A*及其他星系中心的观测加以检验。

Abstract: We investigate the red/blue shifts of photons emitted by particles moving along stable circular orbits around a Hayward regular black hole immersed in perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM). The PFDM contribution modifies the horizon structure and shifts the characteristic radii associated with circular motion, including the photon sphere and the ISCO radius. We show that the redshift reaches its minimum in the pure Hayward case and increases for nonzero values of the PFDM parameter, whereas the blueshift exhibits the opposite behavior. We also analyze the black hole shadow and compare the results with the observational constraints from Sgr A*. Positive values of the PFDM parameter reduce the shadow radius, while negative values produce larger deviations from the Schwarzschild case. These results suggest that frequency shifts and black hole shadows may provide observational signatures of PFDM effects in strong gravitational fields.

</details>


### [4] [Probing the limits of the semiclassical Einstein equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24103)
*Gustavo Schranck Habermann,Daniel A. Turolla Vanzella*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper investigates the limits of semiclassical gravity by constructing a controlled scenario where quantum fluctuations of weak-gravitational states—despite individually being small—collectively drive the system into a strong-gravity regime. By analyzing a branch-degenerate observable, it enables a direct comparison between full quantum gravity predictions and those from the semiclassical Einstein equation, thereby probing when the latter breaks down.
\n\n
本文通过构建一个可控的场景来研究半经典引力的适用极限：尽管单个弱引力态的量子涨落很小，但其非相干叠加整体上可驱动系统进入强引力区域。通过分析一个分支简并观测量（branch-degenerate observable），可以直接比较完整量子引力与半经典爱因斯坦方程的预测，从而探明后者的失效边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Semiclassical gravity relies on the semiclassical Einstein equation $G_{\mu\nu} = 8\pi G \langle \hat{T}_{\mu\nu} \rangle$, which assumes that quantum matter backreacts on spacetime only through the expectation value of its stress-energy tensor. However, this framework ignores potentially large quantum fluctuations of $\hat{T}_{\mu\nu}$, which formally diverge and may invalidate the approximation even when individual fluctuations appear small. The authors aim to rigorously test the domain of validity of this approximation using gravitational nonlinearities.
\n\n
半经典引力依赖于半经典爱因斯坦方程 $G_{\mu\nu} = 8\pi G \langle \hat{T}_{\mu\nu} \rangle$，该方程假设量子物质仅通过其应力-能量张量的期望值对时空产生反作用。然而，该框架忽略了 $\hat{T}_{\mu\nu}$ 可能存在的巨大量子涨落——这些涨落在形式上是发散的，即使单个涨落看似微小，也可能使近似失效。作者旨在利用引力的非线性特性，严格检验该近似的有效范围。

Method: The authors construct an analytically solvable model involving an incoherent superposition (statistical mixture) of weak-field gravitational configurations. Due to the nonlinear nature of Einstein gravity, the collective effect of these states can amplify into a strong-field regime. They introduce a “branch-degenerate observable”—an observable whose classical value is identical across different branches of the superposition but whose quantum expectation differs from the semiclassical prediction—enabling a clean diagnostic of the breakdown of semiclassical gravity.
\n\n
作者构建了一个可解析求解的模型，其中包含弱场引力构型的非相干叠加（统计混合）。由于爱因斯坦引力的非线性特性，这些态的集体效应可被放大至强场区域。他们引入了一种“分支简并观测量”（branch-degenerate observable）——该观测量在叠加的不同分支上具有相同的经典值，但其量子期望值与半经典预测不同——从而为半经典引力的失效提供清晰的诊断工具。

Result: The analysis demonstrates that even when each component of the mixture corresponds to a weak-gravity state with small local quantum fluctuations, the nonlinear coupling inherent in general relativity can lead to macroscopic deviations from semiclassical predictions. The branch-degenerate observable exhibits a measurable discrepancy between the full quantum treatment and the semiclassical Einstein equation, signaling a breakdown of the latter despite apparently favorable conditions.
\n\n
分析表明，即使混合中的每个组分都对应于局部量子涨落微小的弱引力态，广义相对论固有的非线性耦合仍可导致与半经典预测的宏观偏差。分支简并观测量在完整量子处理与半经典爱因斯坦方程之间展现出可观测的差异，表明后者在看似有利的条件下已然失效。

Conclusion: The validity of semiclassical gravity cannot be guaranteed solely by the smallness of local quantum fluctuations of the stress-energy tensor. Gravitational nonlinearities can conspire with quantum incoherence to produce strong-field effects that lie beyond the semiclassical approximation. The proposed framework offers a concrete pathway to empirically or theoretically delimit the regime where semiclassical gravity remains reliable.
\n\n
半经典引力的有效性不能仅由应力-能量张量局部量子涨落的微小性来保证。引力非线性可与量子非相干性协同作用，产生超出半经典近似范围的强场效应。所提出的框架为经验或理论上界定半经典引力可靠适用的区域提供了具体路径。

Abstract: In the context of semiclassical gravity, the semiclassical Einstein equation is often invoked when backreaction of quantum matter/fields on the spacetime is at stake. It is expected to hold when quantum fluctuations are small. Yet, it is routinely used to justify the central role of the expectation value of the stress-energy tensor of quantum fields, whose fluctuations formally diverge. Here we propose a new way to probe the limits of this approximation by exploiting peculiar nonlinearities of gravity. As a proof of principle, we construct a controlled, analytically tractable setting where the incoherent mixture of weak-gravity states drives the system into a strong-gravity regime. By selecting a branch-degenerate observable, one can compare predictions of quantum and semiclassical gravity, potentially delimiting the validity of the latter.

</details>


### [5] [Towards a causal effective thermodynamics of scalar-tensor gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24133)
*Narayan Banerjee,Andrea Giusti,Valerio Faraoni,Luca Gallerani,Alain Maltais-Gosselin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper extends the thermal analogy of scalar-tensor gravity to the causal Israel-Stewart thermodynamics by promoting heat flux density to a timelike vector, enabling consistent decoupling of effective temperature $\mathcal{T}$ and thermal conductivity $\mathcal{K}$ while preserving GR as the equilibrium limit.
\n\n
本文将标量-张量引力的热力学类比推广到因果性的Israel-Stewart热力学模型，通过将热流密度提升为类时矢量，实现了有效温度 $\mathcal{T}$ 与有效热导率 $\mathcal{K}$ 的一致解耦，同时保持广义相对论（GR）作为 $\mathcal{KT} \to 0$ 极限下的平衡态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Previous formulations based on Eckart's irreversible thermodynamics suffer from acausality and lack independent control over effective temperature $\mathcal{T}$ and thermal conductivity $\mathcal{K}$ in scalar-tensor gravity. The authors aim to construct a causal thermodynamic framework that resolves these issues while retaining general relativity as a natural equilibrium limit.
\n\n
此前基于Eckart不可逆热力学的表述存在非因果性问题，并且在标量-张量引力中无法独立调控有效温度 $\mathcal{T}$ 与热导率 $\mathcal{K}$。作者旨在构建一个因果性的热力学框架，以解决这些问题，同时保留广义相对论作为自然的平衡极限。

Method: The authors adopt the Israel-Stewart causal thermodynamics model with a minimal ansatz: the heat flux density is promoted to an independent timelike vector field. This yields modified constitutive equations that are analytically tractable and introduce relaxation dynamics for the effective fluid variables. The vanishing-$\mathcal{KT}$ limit is explicitly imposed to recover general relativity as equilibrium.
\n\n
作者采用Israel-Stewart因果热力学模型，并引入一个极小假设：将热流密度提升为一个独立的类时矢量场。由此得到可解析处理的本构方程，并为有效流体变量引入弛豫动力学。通过显式施加 $\mathcal{KT} \to 0$ 极限，确保广义相对论作为平衡态被恢复。

Result: The framework successfully decouples $\mathcal{T}$ and $\mathcal{K}$ for the first time in a consistent manner within scalar-tensor gravity, provides causal evolution equations, and maintains GR as the equilibrium state in the $\mathcal{KT} \to 0$ limit. The formalism is ready for cosmological applications.
\n\n
该框架首次在标量-张量引力中一致地实现了 $\mathcal{T}$ 与 $\mathcal{K}$ 的解耦，提供了因果性的演化方程，并在 $\mathcal{KT} \to 0$ 极限下保持广义相对论为平衡态。该形式体系已可用于宇宙学应用。

Conclusion: By embedding scalar-tensor gravity into the Israel-Stewart causal thermodynamics with a minimal extension, the paper establishes a robust and physically interpretable effective fluid description that overcomes limitations of Eckart theory and naturally incorporates general relativity as its equilibrium configuration.
\n\n
通过将标量-张量引力嵌入到带有极小扩展的Israel-Stewart因果热力学中，本文建立了一个稳健且物理可解释的有效流体描述，克服了Eckart理论的局限性，并自然地将广义相对论纳入为其平衡构型。

Abstract: The thermal analogy between the effective fluid of scalar-tensor gravity and Eckart's irreversible thermodynamics is extended to the causal Israel-Stewart model, adopting the minimal ansatz of promoting the heat flux density to a timelike vector. This choice yields analytically manageable constitutive equations, allowing for the first consistent decoupling of the effective temperature $\mathcal{T}$ and the effective thermal conductivity $\mathcal{K}$ of scalar-tensor gravity. Crucially, this new framework preserves the interpretation of general relativity as the equilibrium state approached via a dynamical relaxation process in the vanishing-$\mathcal {KT}$ limit. This new causal formalism is applied to cosmology.

</details>


### [6] [Inferring Neutron-Star Properties from Post-merger Gravitational-wave Spectra with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24145)
*Dimitrios Pesios,Nikolaos Stergioulas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper demonstrates a proof-of-concept for using artificial neural networks to infer neutron-star properties—mass $M$, quadrupolar tidal deformability $κ_2^τ$, and mass–radius slope $dR/dM$—directly from noise-free post-merger gravitational-wave spectra of equal-mass binary neutron-star mergers. Nonlinear inverse surrogates outperform linear baselines, with ensemble single-task networks achieving the best performance, while also recovering known physical relations.
\n\n

Abstract: We present a proof-of-concept study of the inverse problem of inferring neutron-star properties directly from the post-merger gravitational-wave spectrum of equal-mass binary neutron-star mergers. Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) provide a powerful, theory-independent probe of the dynamical structure of spacetime and the cosmological background. Method: The authors employ the Unruh-DeWitt detector model, coupling one qubit of a four-qubit Dicke state to a detector undergoing uniform proper acceleration in Minkowski spacetime. The dependence of entanglement on the dimensionless acceleration parameter $a/\\Omega$ (with $\\Omega$ the detector energy gap) is analyzed analytically or numerically.

作者采用Unruh-DeWitt探测器模型，将四量子比特Dicke态中的一个量子比特耦合到在Minkowski时空中经历匀加速运动的探测器。他们在对场自由度进行迹运算后计算约化密度矩阵，并使用适当的纠缠度量（如广义concurrence或negativity）来量化四体纠缠。他们分析了纠缠对无量纲加速度参数$a/\\Omega$（其中$\\Omega$为探测器能隙）的依赖关系，采用解析或数值方法。

Result: The key result is that tetrapartite entanglement does not decay monotonically with increasing acceleration. Instead, it first decreases, reaches a minimum, and then increases asymptotically toward a finite non-zero value. This demonstrates that within a finite range of acceleration, the Unruh effect can enhance—rather than suppress—multipartite entanglement, revealing its dual role as both a source of decoherence and a potential entanglement generator.

核心结果是：四体纠缠并不随加速度增加而单调衰减，而是先减小、达到极小值后，再渐近地趋近于一个有限非零值。这表明在有限加速度范围内，Unruh效应可以增强而非抑制多体纠缠，揭示了其双重角色——既是退相干源，又可能是纠缠生成机制。

Conclusion: The study concludes that Dicke states exhibit unexpected resilience to Unruh-induced decoherence, with entanglement even being enhanced at moderate accelerations. This challenges the simplistic view of the Unruh effect as purely detrimental and highlights Dicke states as promising candidates for relativistic quantum information protocols where observers may be in non-inertial frames.

Abstract: We investigate the behavior of tetrapartite entanglement in a four-qubit Dicke state under relativistic motion by employing the Unruh-DeWitt detector model, where one detector undergoes uniform acceleration. We show that the entanglement exhibits a non-monotonic evolution: it first decreases and subsequently increases toward a finite value as the acceleration grows. In contrast to the conventional view that the Unruh effect leads to a monotonic degradation of multipartite entanglement, our results demonstrate that it can instead enhance multipartite entanglement within a finite parameter regime. This behavior reveals a dual role of the Unruh effect in multipartite systems. Our findings therefore provide a refined understanding of relativistic multipartite quantum correlations, indicating that Dicke states constitute more robust multipartite quantum resources against Unruh-induced decoherence and may offer advantages for relativistic quantum information processing tasks.

### [11] [Polarized Anisotropic Stochastic Gravitational Wave Background Search with Ground-Based Detector Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25772)

TL;DR: This paper extends stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) analysis to a full-Stokes formalism ($I$, $V$, $Q$, $U$), analogous to CMB polarimetry, enabling joint reconstruction of intensity and polarization components. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conventional SGWB searches assume unpolarized or intensity-only signals, but many physical mechanisms—such as parity-violating gravity, anisotropic sources, or chiral astrophysical populations—can generate polarized gravitational waves. Motivated by CMB polarimetry techniques, the authors seek to develop a consistent maximum-likelihood framework that jointly reconstructs all four Stokes parameters for SGWB, thereby enabling unbiased estimation and access to new observables.

传统的SGWB搜寻假设信号无偏振或仅含强度分量，但许多物理机制——如宇称破坏引力、各向异性源或手征性天体物理种群——均可产生偏振引力波。标准互相关分析流程未考虑偏振自由度（$V$, $Q$, $U$），可能导致强度图推断出现偏差并遗漏关键信号。受CMB偏振测量技术启发，作者旨在构建一个一致的最大似然框架，联合重构SGWB的全部四个Stokes参数，从而实现无偏估计并获取新的可观测量。

Method: The authors generalize the standard SGWB cross-correlation formalism to a full-Stokes description by defining Stokes responses for ground-based interferometer networks. They construct a joint likelihood function over spherical-harmonic coefficients of $I$, $V$, $Q$, and $U$, incorporating the detector network’s Fisher matrix, which encodes the non-orthogonality of Stokes responses. Maximum-likelihood estimators are derived for the angular power spectra $C^{VV}_\ell$, $C^{EE}_\ell$, $C^{BB}_\ell$, and the cross-spectrum $|C^{IV}_\ell|$. The method treats polarized shot noise from CBC transients on equal footing with intensity noise, without assuming parametric suppression.
\n\n
作者通过为地面干涉仪网络定义Stokes响应，将标准SGWB互相关形式推广至完整的Stokes描述。他们构建了一个关于 $I$、$V$、$Q$ 和 $U$ 的球谐系数的联合似然函数，并纳入探测器网络的Fisher矩阵，该矩阵刻画了Stokes响应的非正交性。由此导出了角功率谱 $C^{VV}_\ell$、$C^{EE}_\ell$、$C^{BB}_\ell$ 以及交叉谱 $|C^{IV}_\ell|$ 的最大似然估计量。该方法将CBC瞬变源产生的偏振散粒噪声与强度噪声同等对待，不假设其存在参数压低。

Result: Applying their full-Stokes pipeline to LVK O3 data, the authors obtain the first observational constraints on $C^{VV}_\ell$, $C^{EE}_\ell$, $C^{BB}_\ell$, and $|C^{IV}_\ell|$. They demonstrate that intensity-only analyses yield biased estimates when polarized components are present, due to the lack of orthogonality in the Fisher inner product between Stokes modes. Furthermore, they show that polarized shot noise from CBC foregrounds scales comparably to intensity shot noise, implying that polarization cannot be neglected even for unresolved transient populations. The full-Stokes framework successfully isolates polarized anisotropies invisible to conventional methods.
\n\n
将完整的Stokes分析流程应用于LVK O3数据，作者首次获得了对 $C^{VV}_\ell$、$C^{EE}_\ell$、$C^{BB}_\ell$ 和 $|C^{IV}_\ell|$ 的观测限制。他们证明，当存在偏振分量时，仅强度分析会因Stokes模式在Fisher内积中缺乏正交性而产生偏差。此外，他们表明CBC前景产生的偏振散粒噪声与强度散粒噪声具有可比拟的标度，意味着即使对于未分辨的瞬变源群体，偏振效应也不可忽略。完整的Stokes框架成功分离出传统方法无法探测的偏振各向异性。

Conclusion: The full-Stokes formalism is essential for unbiased and complete characterization of the SGWB, particularly in the presence of polarized astrophysical or cosmological sources. Standard intensity-only analyses are insufficient and potentially misleading. The developed maximum-likelihood map-making framework enables future high-sensitivity searches for parity violation, chiral gravitational-wave production, and anisotropic source distributions through polarization-sensitive observables.
\n\n
完整的Stokes形式对于无偏且全面地刻画SGWB至关重要，尤其是在存在偏振天体物理或宇宙学源的情况下。传统的仅强度分析是不充分且可能产生误导的。所发展的最大似然成图框架为未来通过偏振敏感可观测量搜寻宇称破坏、手征性引力波产生机制及各向异性源分布提供了基础。

Abstract: Gravitational waves admit a Stokes decomposition into intensity ($I$), circular polarization ($V$), and linear polarization ($Q$, $U$), analogous to Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) polarimetry. We implement a full-Stokes maximum-likelihood SGWB map-making analysis for ground-based detector networks, promoting the standard cross-correlation data products used in existing pipelines to a joint reconstruction of $I$, $V$, $Q$, $U$. Applied to LVK O3 data, we constrain the polarized angular spectra $C^{VV}_\ell$, $C^{EE}_\ell$, $C^{BB}_\ell$ and $|C^{IV}_\ell|$. We show that an intensity-only model is biased when polarized sky components are present, since the detector-network Fisher inner product does not generally make the Stokes responses orthogonal. For transient CBC foregrounds, polarized shot noise is not parametrically suppressed relative to ordinary CBC intensity shot noise. The full Stokes framework separates the Stokes sectors while providing access to polarized anisotropies invisible to conventional intensity-only searches.

The authors demonstrate that black holes act as resonant spectral filters, with excitation amplitudes given by the spatial Fourier transform of initial data at wavenumber $k \sim \omega_n$. They validate this analytically and numerically using tailored perturbations and introduce a new fitting tool, $\mathtt{QNMToolkit}$, for robust time-domain ringdown analysis.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding how different quasinormal modes of a black hole are excited by generic perturbations has remained elusive, with prior treatments being case-specific and lacking a unified physical principle. 理解黑洞的准正规模如何被一般扰动激发一直是个难题，以往的研究多为个案处理，缺乏统一的物理原理。本文旨在揭示一个普适且定量的机制，将初始扰动的谱结构与铃宕信号中的QNM振幅联系起来。

Method: The authors factorize the QNM excitation coefficients and show they equal the weighted spatial Fourier transform of the initial perturbation evaluated at wavenumber $k \sim \omega_n$, where $\omega_n$ is the complex QNM frequency. They construct localized initial data with tunable spectral bandwidth and carrier frequency to isolate mode excitation. Analytical derivations are complemented by numerical time-domain evolutions of perturbed black holes. A new algorithm, $\mathtt{QNMToolkit}$, performs ringdown fits across ensembles of sliding time windows to quantify fitting variance and extract robust amplitudes.

作者对QNM激发系数进行因子分解，证明其等于初始扰动在波数 $k \sim \omega_n$（$\omega_n$ 为复QNM频率）处的加权空间傅里叶变换。他们构造具有可调谱带宽和载频的局域化初值以分离模激发。解析推导辅以黑洞扰动的时间域数值演化。新算法 $\mathtt{QNMToolkit}$ 在大量滑动时间窗上执行铃宕拟合，以量化拟合方差并提取鲁棒的振幅。

Result: The excitation amplitude of each QNM is directly proportional to the Fourier spectrum of the initial perturbation at the mode’s frequency. 每个QNM的激发幅度直接正比于初始扰动在其模频率处的傅里叶谱。数值模拟证实：当模频率落在扰动的谱支撑范围内时，该模被选择性激发；当扰动的主频与 $\mathrm{Re}(\omega_n)$ 对齐时，激发达到最大。这些预测通过基于 $\mathtt{QNMToolkit}$ 对时间域数据的拟合，在百分之一精度水平上得到验证。

Conclusion: Black holes function as resonant spectral filters during ringdown, with QNM excitation governed by a simple and universal spectral matching rule between the perturbation and the mode frequencies. Abstract: The ringdown of a perturbed black hole (BH) can be described as a superposition of quasinormal modes (QNMs), whose frequencies are determined by the spacetime geometry while their amplitudes depend also on the perturbing source. However, the physical mechanism governing mode excitation remains unclear and is typically treated on a case by case basis. In this work, we show that QNM excitation is governed by a simple spectral rule: each mode is excited according to the Fourier content of the perturbation evaluated at its characteristic frequency. This result follows from the factorization of the excitation coefficients and establishes a direct, quantitative connection between the spectral properties of the perturbation and the resulting ringdown amplitudes. We demonstrate analytically and numerically that BHs act as resonant spectral filters. We show analytically that the excitation amplitude of each mode equals the weighted spatial Fourier transform of the initial data evaluated at wavenumber $k\simω_n$ so that the filter selectively excites modes whose frequencies lie within the spectral support of the perturbation while suppressing others. Consequently, the excitation is maximized when the dominant perturbation frequency lies close to the real part of the QNM frequency, and we validate this at the percent level with fits to time-domain numerical evolutions. To robustly perform these fits, we have developed a new fitting algorithm, $\mathtt{QNMToolkit}$, which performs ringdown fits over large ensembles of sliding time-domain windows and quantifies the resulting fitting variance.

Abstract: The physical origin of spacetime discreteness remains a central open problem in quantum gravity, with most existing approaches relying on specific microscopic structures or model-dependent assumptions. The microscopic lengths are further governed by a geometric renormalization-group flow admitting finite-length fixed points. This construction preserves Lorentz invariance and general covariance without ad hoc cutoffs or symmetry breaking. Our results show that the classical continuous-spacetime description corresponds to an unstable limiting regime, whereas a finite microscopic length and a discrete spacetime structure arise naturally from the fundamental requirements of micro-measurement consistency.

Frame-independent analysis confirms interior compliance but reveals Hawking–Ellis Type IV violations in the smoothing tail. Method: The authors introduce two source-first shell ansätze: (1) an S-shell with vanishing shift vector ($\\beta = 0$), where the lapse and spatial metric are solved from the Hamiltonian constraint for a prescribed energy density; and (2) a T-shell where the shift is derived from the momentum constraint given the same energy-momentum source. Both ansätze ensure explicit matter models and satisfy the initial-value constraints by construction. They evaluate eight warp-shell constructions using a five-criterion standard: (i) regularity of the metric, (ii) exact satisfaction of Einstein constraints, (iii) presence of an explicit matter model, (iv) frame-independent verification of energy-condition margins via eigenvalue analysis of the stress-energy tensor (classifying Hawking–Ellis types), and (v) global diagnostics including null-energy line integrals. The comprehensive scan over 600 configurations in both S- and T-shell classes yields no admissible solution. The energy-condition deficit persists in the static limit ($v_0 = 0$), indicating it arises from the geometry of the source–vacuum transition rather than from kinematic effects like the shift vector. Nevertheless, the null-energy condition integrated along representative off-axis null geodesics remains positive for all source-prescribed shells, suggesting that averaged energy conditions may be less restrictive than pointwise ones.

Abstract: An exact gravitational instanton solution on a vacuum Kerr-like warped spacetime in conformal dilaton gravity is found. This suggests that this is the highest possible polynomial in describing the singularities of black holes of Petrov type D axially symmetric manifolds and don't fits the Plebanski-Demianski classification of black holes which is determined by a fourth order polynomial.   <summary>Details</summary>

Additionally, standard ringdown analyses discard pre-merger data, which encode physical information about QNM excitation amplitudes and phases. The authors aim to disentangle spectral measurement from theory interpretation and leverage pre-merger constraints to improve ringdown inference without presupposing a specific spacetime model.

Method: The authors develop \texttt{SPRING} (\textit{Spectral-level Pre-merger-informed RINGdown inference}), a Bayesian inference framework that: (i) models the ringdown signal using an agnostic parametric form—specifically a two-component damped sinusoid (2DS)—without enforcing Kerr QNM frequency-damping relations; (ii) uses pre-merger gravitational-wave data to inform priors on the complex amplitudes (magnitude and phase) of the QNM components via consistency with numerical-relativity-calibrated waveform models; and (iii) performs spectral inference at the level of observable frequencies and damping times, deferring theory-specific interpretation (e.g., mapping to mass and spin) to a later stage. The method is applied to the event GW250114.

在GW250114上的应用表明，相较于忽略并合前信息的纯ringdown分析，采用2DS模型的\texttt{SPRING}显著提升了贝叶斯证据（$Δ\ln B \sim 5$–$10$）。尽管第二个阻尼正弦分量引入了额外自由度（可能模拟超出Kerr的模式），所推断的残余黑洞质量和自旋仍与完整的inspiral-merger-ringdown分析结果高度一致。这表明并合前信息有效正则化了谱推断，并抑制了虚假模式激发。

Conclusion: \texttt{SPRING} establishes a theory-agnostic pathway for black-hole spectroscopy by separating spectral measurement from spacetime interpretation and incorporating pre-merger constraints on QNM amplitudes. This approach enhances the robustness of ringdown analyses, improves model comparison via increased Bayesian evidence, and paves the way for more stringent tests of the no-hair theorem and general relativity in the strong-field regime.

\texttt{SPRING}通过将谱测量与时空解释分离，并引入对QNM振幅的并合前约束，为黑洞谱学建立了一条无理论预设的路径。该方法增强了ringdown分析的鲁棒性，通过提升贝叶斯证据改善了模型比较能力，为在强场区域更严格地检验无毛定理（no-hair theorem）和广义相对论铺平了道路。

Abstract: Black-hole spectroscopy aims to infer properties of the remnant spacetime from the quasinormal-mode (QNM) spectrum of the gravitational-wave ringdown signal. In most implementations, however, this inference is performed with waveform models that already incorporate Kerr or other theory-specific QNM spectral relations, thereby entangling spectral measurement with remnant or beyond-Kerr parameter inference. At the same time, conventional ringdown analyses commonly excise the pre-merger data, which in principle contain information about the excitation amplitudes and phases of the QNMs. We introduce \texttt{SPRING} (\textit{Spectral-level Pre-merger-informed RINGdown inference}), a framework designed to separate ringdown spectral inference from theory-side interpretation while propagating pre-merger information through amplitude-scale estimation. The resulting remnant posterior remains closely consistent with the inspiral-merger-ringdown estimate, despite the extra freedom introduced by the second DS component. Abstract: In this study, we explore the dynamics of particle orbits and their corresponding gravitational wave signatures in the vicinity of a Schwarzschild black hole (BH) surrounded by a cloud of strings and embedded in a perfect fluid dark matter medium. Our findings reveal that while both the orbital radius and angular momentum increase with increasing $a$, they decrease as $α$ increases; notably, the energy exhibits the opposite trend, decreasing with $a$ and increasing with $α$. Furthermore, we examine periodic orbits indexed by rational numbers $q$ and the gravitational waveforms they generate. The results demonstrate that an increase in the string cloud parameter $a$ induces a significant phase delay in the waveform. Specifically, waveforms with lower values of $a$ oscillate over shorter time intervals, whereas those with higher values extend to longer time scales. These distinct features, including noticeable differences in amplitude, allow the waveforms to be clearly distinguished from those in a pure Schwarzschild spacetime.

Result: The study finds that for negative values of the dark matter–scalar coupling $β_d < 0$, spontaneous scalarization occurs across a wide range of dark matter densities $\\rho_d$. Moreover, the relevant $β_d$ and $\\rho_d$ values correspond to those in which self-interacting dark matter successfully addresses discrepancies in galaxy formation (e.g., core–cusp and too-big-to-fail problems), suggesting observational relevance if dark matter stars exist.

Conclusion: If compact objects made of self-interacting dark matter exist, they would be highly susceptible to spontaneous scalarization for a broad class of negative scalar–dark matter couplings. Abstract: We investigated scalarization in dark matter stars. In scalar tensor theories, the coupling between matter and a scalar field generates an effective mass, which is known to trigger the growth of the scalar field in stars and black holes (spontaneous scalarization). On the other hand, models in which the scalar field couples not only to baryonic matter but also to dark matter have been discussed in the literature. Within the framework of scalar tensor theories, we studied the parameter ranges in which the growth of the scalar field occurs in compact stars formed by self interacting dark matter. As a result, we found that scalarization occurs over most of the parameter region with a negative coupling parameter $β_d$. This parameter range is broader than that for scalarization in conventional neutron stars. Since the dark matter parameters considered correspond to those in which self-interacting dark matter resolves problems in galaxy formation, if dark matter stars actually exist, they could serve as probes for investigating the relationship between the scalar field and dark matter.

### [24] [Cesàro convergence of the high-order WKB method and its applications to black-hole overtones and long-lived modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25705)
*Roman A. Konoplya,Jerzy Matyjasek,Alexander Zhidenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: We present a high-order, fully automated Mathematica implementation of the black-hole WKB method using the Bender-Wu algorithm and diagonal Padé approximants. This approach efficiently computes quasinormal modes in traditionally challenging regimes—high overtones ($n > l$) and long-lived modes of massive fields—but reveals limitations for non-moderate black-hole metrics where apparent convergence may be misleading. We propose Cesàro means of Padé-resummed WKB series as a reliable internal convergence criterion.
\n\n
我们提出了一种基于Bender-Wu算法和对角Padé逼近的高阶全自动Mathematica实现的黑洞WKB方法。该方法能高效计算传统上具有挑战性的两类准正规模：高泛音（$n > l$）和大质量场的长寿命模式，但也揭示了对于非适度（non-moderate）黑洞度规的局限性——此时看似收敛的结果可能严重偏离真实频率。我们提出使用Padé重求和WKB级数的Cesàro平均值作为可靠的内部收敛判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard low-order WKB methods struggle with two important regimes: (1) high overtone numbers where $n > l$, and (2) very long-lived quasinormal modes arising from massive fields around black holes. Moreover, there is a need for robust, automated, and systematically improvable computational frameworks that can assess their own reliability without external validation. The paper aims to address these gaps by extending the WKB method to very high orders and analyzing its convergence properties.
\n\n
标准低阶WKB方法在两类重要情形下表现不佳：(1) 高泛音数（$n > l$）的情形；(2) 黑洞周围大质量场所产生的极长寿命准正规模。此外，亟需一种鲁棒、自动化且可系统改进的计算框架，能够无需外部验证即可评估自身结果的可靠性。本文旨在通过将WKB方法拓展至极高阶并分析其收敛性质来填补这些空白。

Method: The authors implement a fully automatic high-order WKB scheme in Mathematica based on the Bender-Wu recursion algorithm. They enhance convergence using diagonal Padé approximants and analyze the behavior of the resulting series for various black-hole spacetimes. To assess reliability, they examine both raw WKB outputs and their Cesàro means, particularly focusing on metrics classified as “non-moderate” where near-horizon expansion coefficients grow large.
\n\n
作者基于Bender-Wu递归算法，在Mathematica中实现了全自动的高阶WKB方案。他们利用对角Padé逼近提升收敛性，并针对不同黑洞时空分析所得级数的行为。为评估可靠性，他们同时考察原始WKB输出及其Cesàro平均值，特别关注“非适度”类度规——其近视界展开系数较大。

Result: The high-order WKB method combined with Padé approximants successfully computes accurate quasinormal frequencies for high overtones ($n > l$) and long-lived massive-field modes. However, for non-moderate black-hole metrics, the method may appear numerically stable yet converge to incorrect values. Crucially, while individual Padé-resummed WKB orders do not converge monotonically, their Cesàro means exhibit monotonic convergence beyond a certain high order, providing a self-consistent convergence diagnostic.

结合Padé逼近的高阶WKB方法成功计算了高泛音（$n > l$）和长寿命大质量场模式的精确准正规模频率。然而，对于非适度黑洞度规，该方法虽看似数值稳定，却可能收敛至错误值。关键发现是：尽管单个Padé重求和的WKB阶次不呈现单调收敛，但其Cesàro平均值在达到足够高阶后表现出单调收敛性，从而提供了一种自洽的收敛性诊断工具。

Conclusion: High-order WKB with Padé resummation is a powerful tool for computing difficult quasinormal modes, but numerical stability alone does not guarantee correctness, especially for non-moderate black-hole geometries. The monotonic convergence of Cesàro means of the Padé-WKB sequence offers a practical internal criterion to validate results, enhancing the method’s reliability in automated computations.

结合Padé重求和的高阶WKB方法是计算困难准正规模的强大工具，但仅凭数值稳定性不足以保证正确性，尤其对于非适度黑洞几何。Padé-WKB序列的Cesàro平均值的单调收敛性提供了一种实用的内部判据，可用于验证结果，从而提升该方法在自动化计算中的可靠性。

Abstract: We develop a fully automatic Mathematica implementation of the black-hole WKB method at very high orders based on the Bender-Wu algorithm, which in principle is limited only by memory and computational time, and show that when pushed to sufficiently high order and improved by diagonal Padé approximants the method becomes efficient for two regimes which are usually regarded as difficult for the standard low-order WKB treatment: the first several overtones with n>l and the very long-lived quasinormal modes of massive fields. At the same time, we show that this efficiency has a nontrivial limitation: for black-hole metrics belonging to the non-moderate class, especially when higher coefficients of the near-horizon parametrization become large, the WKB sequence may exhibit an apparent convergence to values which are nevertheless far from the accurate quasinormal frequencies. Thus, numerical stabilization of the WKB output alone is not always a sufficient criterion of correctness. However, we observe that although the WKB method with diagonal or near-diagonal Padé approximants does not exhibit monotonic convergence order by order, the corresponding Cesàro means become monotonically convergent once a sufficiently high WKB order is reached. This behavior may serve as an internal WKB criterion for the convergence of the method.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from theoretical proposals that quantum gravitational effects could cause black holes to transition into white holes, potentially producing observable high-energy signals. Method: The authors adopt a photospheric emission model adapted to the white hole scenario, assuming that the outflow from the white hole is dominated by photons and leptons rather than baryons. The total emitted energy is estimated using conservation laws and thermodynamic considerations at the photosphere. Finally, they infer the present-day cosmological number density of lunar-mass ($\\sim 10^{-2} M_\\odot$) primordial black holes by requiring their transition rate not to overproduce the observed diffuse gamma-ray background.

Result: The model yields a non-thermal gamma-ray spectrum peaking around MeV–GeV energies, with a total isotropic-equivalent energy of order $10^{48}–10^{50}$ erg. The derived constraint implies an upper bound on the comoving number density of lunar-mass primordial black holes of approximately $n_{\\text{PBH}} \\lesssim 10^{-5} \\, \\text{Mpc}^{-3}$, assuming all such PBHs undergo black-to-white hole transitions today.

该模型得到一个非热伽马射线谱，峰值位于 MeV–GeV 能段，各向同性等效总能量约为 $10^{48}–10^{50}$ 尔格。由此导出的限制表明，在假设所有此类原初黑洞当前均发生黑洞-白洞转变的前提下，其共动数密度上限约为 $n_{\\text{PBH}} \\lesssim 10^{-5} \\, \\text{Mpc}^{-3}$。

A general reconstruction procedure is established, allowing the systematic construction of nonlinear teleparallel \(F(T)\) models for arbitrary coframe ansätze. Focusing on power-law (PL) configurations, we obtain several classes of exact solutions, including constant-radius, black-hole-like (BH-like), and wormhole-like (WH-like) branches, and analyze their horizon structures, torsion singularities, and stability properties. The inclusion of electromagnetic sources leads to new charged solutions that generalize Reissner--Nordström (RN) spacetimes and reveal modified near-horizon and asymptotic behaviors. The results are further organized within an invariant classification framework, highlighting the role of torsion in shaping the solution space. Overall, this work provides a unified and covariant approach to the construction and interpretation of physically relevant compact-object, effective cosmological, and regularized strong-field sectors in nonlinear teleparallel gravity, with potential implications for strong-field tests beyond General Relativity (GR).

### [27] [Generalising gravitationally induced decoherence beyond linear environmental interactions in a microscopic quantum mechanical toy model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25936)
*Max Joseph Fahn,Renata Ferrero,Kristina Giesel,Roman Kemper*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper generalizes a quantum mechanical toy model for gravitationally induced decoherence by replacing the standard linear coupling between system and environment with an interaction based on Weyl elements of environmental position operators. Two analytical methods are developed to compute environmental correlation functions—using Wick’s theorem with ladder operators and via short-time Fourier transform without them—both yielding consistent results. The model recovers prior linear-coupling decoherence in the small Weyl parameter limit, supports Markovian dynamics due to fast-decaying correlations, and provides a solution to the renormalized master equation.
\n\n
本文通过将系统与环境之间的标准线性耦合替换为基于环境位置算符的Weyl元素的相互作用，推广了引力诱导退相干的量子力学玩具模型。作者发展了两种解析计算环境关联函数的方法——一种使用升降算符结合Wick定理，另一种基于短时傅里叶变换且完全避免升降算符——两者结果一致。该模型在小Weyl参数极限下复现了先前的线性耦合退相干模型，由于关联函数快速衰减而支持Markov近似，并给出了重整化主方程的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from limitations of previous gravitationally induced decoherence models that assume a linear coupling between the system Hamiltonian and environmental position operators. The authors aim to explore more general interactions inspired by polymer quantum mechanics, where Weyl elements (exponentials of position operators) are fundamental, and non-linear couplings may arise naturally from truncated expansions of these exponentials. This allows for richer decoherence dynamics beyond the linear regime while maintaining compatibility with established frameworks in specific limits.
\n\n
动机源于先前引力诱导退相干模型的局限性，这些模型假设系统哈密顿量与环境位置算符之间呈线性耦合。作者旨在探索受polymer quantum mechanics启发的更一般相互作用，在该框架中Weyl元素（位置算符的指数）是基本算符，且非线性耦合可通过这些指数的截断展开自然产生。这使得退相干动力学超越线性区域，同时在特定极限下仍与已有框架兼容。

Method: The authors introduce a sinusoidal interaction Hamiltonian based on Weyl elements $e^{i\lambda \hat{x}_k}$ of the environmental oscillators’ position operators $\hat{x}_k$, with coupling strength controlled by a Weyl parameter $\lambda$. They develop two complementary analytical techniques to compute thermal correlation functions of the environment: (1) using Wick’s theorem with annihilation/creation operators in the Schrödinger representation, and (2) employing the short-time Fourier transform without invoking ladder operators—making it suitable for future extension to polymer quantisation. They also generalize the spectral density to accommodate the exponential form of the coupling. The master equation is derived under the Born-Markov approximation, justified by numerical evidence of rapid decay in correlation functions, and solved after renormalization.
\n\n
作者引入基于环境振子位置算符$\hat{x}_k$的Weyl元素$e^{i\lambda \hat{x}_k}$的正弦型相互作用哈密顿量，耦合强度由Weyl参数$\lambda$控制。他们发展了两种互补的解析方法计算环境的热关联函数：(1) 在Schrödinger表象中使用升降算符结合Wick定理；(2) 采用短时傅里叶变换，完全不依赖升降算符——便于未来推广至polymer quantisation。他们还推广了谱密度以适应耦合的指数形式。在Born-Markov近似下推导主方程（数值结果表明关联函数快速衰减，从而验证该近似），并求解了重整化后的主方程。

Result: Both analytical methods yield identical environmental correlation functions, confirming internal consistency. Numerical evaluation shows these correlation functions decay rapidly in time, validating the Markov approximation. A Taylor expansion in the Weyl parameter $\lambda$ demonstrates that the first-order term exactly reproduces the linear-coupling decoherence model of Xu & Blencowe (2022) and Domi et al. (2024). The generalized spectral density accommodates the exponential coupling structure, and the renormalized master equation admits an explicit analytical solution.
\n\n
两种解析方法得到相同的环境关联函数，验证了内部一致性。数值计算表明这些关联函数随时间快速衰减，证实了Markov近似有效。对Weyl参数$\lambda$进行Taylor展开显示，一阶项精确复现了Xu & Blencowe (2022)和Domi等(2024)提出的线性耦合退相干模型。推广后的谱密度可适配指数耦合结构，且重整化主方程具有显式解析解。

Conclusion: The work successfully extends gravitationally induced decoherence models beyond linear system-environment coupling by employing Weyl-element-based interactions. The dual-method approach to correlation functions provides a robust foundation for future applications in polymer quantum mechanics, where standard Fock space techniques fail. The recovery of established results in the $\lambda \\to 0$ limit ensures continuity with prior literature, while the explicit solution of the renormalized master equation opens avenues for studying non-linear decoherence effects potentially relevant to quantum gravity phenomenology.
\n\n
本工作通过采用基于Weyl元素的相互作用，成功将引力诱导退相干模型推广至超越线性系统-环境耦合的情形。关联函数的双重计算方法为未来在polymer quantum mechanics中的应用奠定了坚实基础（在该框架中标准Fock空间技术失效）。在$\lambda \\to 0$极限下复现已有的结果确保了与先前文献的连续性，而重整化主方程的显式解则为研究可能与量子引力现象学相关的非线性退相干效应开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We generalise the quantum mechanical toy model for gravitationally induced decoherence presented in Xu, Blencowe (2022) and Domi et al. (2024). In contrast to earlier formulations, in which the Hamiltonian of the system of interest is linearly coupled to the position operators of the oscillators in the environment, we consider an interaction formulated in terms of Weyl elements of the environment's position operators. This extension is motivated by polymer quantum mechanics, in which Weyl elements are fundamental operators, as well as by the possibility of generating non-linear interactions through suitable truncations of the exponential Weyl elements. Here we focus on a sinus-like coupling that is still quantised using the Schrödinger representation and, in the limit of a small Weyl parameter, reproduces the conventional linear interaction. To derive the corresponding master equation, we developed two complementary methods for the analytical calculation of the environmental correlation functions. The first utilises Wick's theorem for thermal expectation values in conjunction with annihilation and creation operators, while the second is based on the short-time Fourier transform and completely avoids the use of annihilation and creation operators, making it more readily transferable and generalisable to a polymer quantisation. Both approaches yield identical results. We further generalise the spectral density required for the exponential coupling structure. A numerical analysis shows that the environmental correlation functions decay rapidly with time, which supports the validity of the Markov approximation. Using a Taylor expansion in the Weyl parameter, we show that the first-order term reproduces the decoherence model of Xu, Blencowe (2022) and Domi et al. (2024). Finally, we derive the solution to the renormalised master equation.

</details>


### [28] [Gravitational wave interactions with matter: beyond quadrupolar perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25950)
*Ulrich K. Beckering Vinckers,Nigel T. Bishop*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper generalizes the theory of linearized gravitational wave (GW) interactions with matter beyond quadrupolar ($\ell=2$) modes to arbitrary multipole moments $\ell \ge 2$ using the Bondi-Sachs formalism on a Minkowski background, and derives formulas for GW damping and heating. It concludes that higher-$\ell$ modes are generally more strongly damped, making their observational detection in post-merger or supernova signals unlikely.
\n\n
本文将线性引力波（GW）与物质相互作用的理论从四极矩（$\ell=2$）模式推广到任意多极矩 $\ell \ge 2$，在Minkowski背景上使用Bondi-Sachs形式体系，推导了GW阻尼和加热效应的公式。结论是高$\ell$模式通常被更强地阻尼，因此在并合后或超新星信号中观测到这些模式的可能性很低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior studies limited GW-matter interaction analyses to quadrupolar ($\ell=2$) perturbations within the Bondi-Sachs framework. However, realistic astrophysical sources like binary neutron star mergers or core-collapse supernovae can excite higher multipole moments ($\ell > 2$). Understanding how these higher-$\ell$ GWs interact with matter—particularly regarding energy dissipation—is essential for interpreting potential observational signatures.

先前的研究将引力波-物质相互作用分析限制在Bondi-Sachs框架内的四极矩（$\ell=2$）扰动。然而，像双中子星并合或核心坍缩超新星这样的真实天体物理源可以激发更高阶的多极矩（$\ell > 2$）。理解这些高$\ell$引力波如何与物质相互作用——特别是关于能量耗散——对于解释潜在的观测信号至关重要。

Method: The authors extend the Bondi-Sachs formalism for linearized gravitational waves on a Minkowski background to include general spherical harmonic angular dependence with arbitrary $\ell \ge 2$. They compute the energy transfer between GWs and matter by analyzing the stress-energy pseudotensor coupling, leading to explicit expressions for damping (energy loss from GWs) and heating (energy deposition into matter) rates as functions of $\ell$.
\n\n
作者将Minkowski背景上线性引力波的Bondi-Sachs形式体系推广到包含任意 $\ell \ge 2$ 的球谐函数角依赖性。他们通过分析应力-能量赝张量耦合来计算引力波与物质之间的能量转移，从而得到阻尼（引力波能量损失）和加热（能量沉积到物质中）率关于 $\ell$ 的显式表达式。

Result: The derived formulas show that both damping and heating effects increase with $\ell$, implying stronger dissipation for higher multipole gravitational waves. Consequently, high-$\ell$ modes are expected to be significantly attenuated during propagation through matter, rendering them unlikely to be detectable in post-merger gravitational wave signals from binary neutron stars or core-collapse supernovae.
\n\n
所推导的公式表明，阻尼和加热效应均随 $\ell$ 增大而增强，意味着高阶多极引力波具有更强的耗散。因此，高$\ell$模式在穿过物质传播时会被显著衰减，使其在双中子星并合后或核心坍缩超新星的引力波信号中难以被探测到。

Conclusion: Higher multipole gravitational waves ($\ell > 2$) experience enhanced interaction with matter compared to quadrupolar modes, leading to greater damping and heating. This strong dissipation likely suppresses their amplitudes below detectability in current or near-future GW observatories for typical astrophysical transients.
\n\n
与四极矩模式相比，高阶多极引力波（$\ell > 2$）与物质的相互作用更强，导致更大的阻尼和加热效应。这种强烈的耗散很可能使它们的振幅在典型天体物理暂现源中被压制到当前或近期引力波天文台无法探测的水平。

Abstract: Previous work has developed the theory of linearized gravitational wave (GW) interactions with matter using the Bondi-Sachs formalism, but with the perturbations restricted to be quadrupolar, i.e., the angular dependence is spherical harmonic with $\ell=2$. Here, the theory is extended to the case of GWs on a Minkowski background with general $\ell$. Formulas for the GW damping and heating effects are obtained for arbitrary $\ell\ge 2$. It is found that the effects are, generally, enhanced, and this suggests that it is unlikely that higher $\ell$-modes will be seen in GW observations of the post-merger signal of a binary neutron star merger, or of a core collapse supernova.

</details>


### [29] [Hyperboloidal evolution for scalar scattering in Minkowski space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26028)
*Ekrem S Demirboğa,Anıl Zenginoğlu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper introduces a time-domain numerical framework for global scalar wave scattering in Minkowski spacetime using exact conformal matching across three compactified regions: past and future hyperboloidal domains attached to $\mathscr I^-$ and $\mathscr I^+$, and a central Penrose domain around spatial infinity $i^0$. The method avoids artificial boundaries and gauge interpolation, enabling stable long-time evolution and accurate radiation extraction. Numerical tests confirm fourth-order convergence for linear and certain nonlinear cases, though cubic nonlinearity reveals boundary regularity limitations.
\n\n
本文提出了一种在闵可夫斯基时空中进行全局标量波散射的时域数值框架，通过精确共形匹配三个紧致化区域实现：分别附着于$\mathscr I^-$和$\mathscr I^+$的过去与未来双曲面区域，以及围绕空间无穷远$i^0$的Penrose区域。该方法避免了人为类时边界和scri-fixing规范之间的插值，实现了稳定的长时间演化和精确的辐射提取。数值实验验证了线性及部分非线性情形下的四阶收敛性，但立方非线性情形揭示了紧致化边界附近正则性处理的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to overcome limitations of existing numerical relativity methods that rely on artificial timelike outer boundaries or require interpolation between different scri-fixing gauges, which can introduce errors or instabilities in long-term simulations of wave propagation in asymptotically flat spacetimes. A global, boundary-free evolution scheme connecting $\mathscr I^-$, $i^0$, and $\mathscr I^+$ is needed for accurate modeling of scattering and radiation.
\n\n
动机在于克服现有数值相对论方法的局限性——这些方法依赖人为的类时外边界或需要在不同scri-fixing规范之间插值，可能在渐近平直时空中的波传播长时间模拟中引入误差或不稳定性。为了精确建模散射与辐射过程，亟需一种连接$\mathscr I^-$、$i^0$和$\mathscr I^+$的全局无边界演化方案。

Method: The authors construct a global numerical scheme by exactly matching three conformally compactified coordinate charts: (1) a past hyperboloidal chart adapted to $\mathscr I^-$, (2) a future hyperboloidal chart adapted to $\mathscr I^+$, and (3) a central Penrose-type chart covering a neighborhood of spatial infinity $i^0$. The matching hypersurfaces are identical conformal hypersurfaces in adjacent charts, ensuring smooth transitions without interpolation. The conformally rescaled scalar wave equation is solved numerically in spherical symmetry for linear (free and Pöschl–Teller potential) and semilinear (cubic, quintic, septic) cases using finite-difference methods with fourth-order accuracy.
\n\n
作者通过精确匹配三个共形紧致化的坐标卡构建全局数值方案：(1) 适配于$\mathscr I^-$的过去双曲面坐标卡，(2) 适配于$\mathscr I^+$的未来双曲面坐标卡，以及(3) 覆盖空间无穷远$i^0$邻域的中心Penrose型坐标卡。相邻坐标卡间的匹配超曲面是相同的共形超曲面，确保无插值的光滑过渡。在球对称假设下，使用四阶有限差分法数值求解共形重标度后的标量波方程，涵盖线性情形（自由传播与Pöschl–Teller势）及半线性情形（立方、五次、七次非线性项）。

Result: The numerical experiments demonstrate stable propagation across the matching interfaces and direct extraction of radiation data at $\mathscr I^+$. Fourth-order convergence is achieved for free waves and those with linear scattering potentials. For semilinear equations, the quintic and septic cases show approximately fourth-order convergence and reproduce the correct late-time tail decay rates (e.g., $t^{-3}$ for quintic). However, the cubic case exhibits only first-order convergence due to the non-vanishing nature of the conformally rescaled nonlinear source near compactified boundaries, indicating a regularity issue at $i^0$.
\n\n
数值实验表明波在匹配界面处稳定传播，并可在$\mathscr I^+$直接提取辐射数据。自由波及含线性散射势的情形达到四阶收敛。对于半线性方程，五次和七次情形表现出近似四阶收敛，并复现了正确的晚期尾部衰减率（例如五次情形为$t^{-3}$）。然而，立方情形仅呈现一阶收敛，这是由于共形重标度后的非线性源项在紧致化边界附近不消失，暴露出$i^0$处的正则性问题。

Conclusion: The conformal matching strategy successfully enables global, long-time numerical simulations of scalar wave scattering without artificial boundaries or gauge interpolation, validating its use for linear and sufficiently regular nonlinear problems. However, the breakdown in convergence for the cubic nonlinearity highlights the need for improved treatment of spatial infinity $i^0$, particularly when the conformally rescaled field equations lack sufficient smoothness at the compactification boundary.
\n\n
共形匹配策略成功实现了无需人工边界或规范插值的全局长时间标量波散射数值模拟，验证了其在线性及足够正则的非线性问题中的有效性。然而，立方非线性情形下的收敛性退化凸显了改进空间无穷远$i^0$处理方法的必要性，特别是当共形重标度后的场方程在紧致化边界处缺乏足够光滑性时。

Abstract: We develop a time-domain numerical framework for global scalar wave scattering in Minkowski spacetime. The main contribution is an exact conformal matching of three compactified regions: a past hyperboloidal domain attached to $\mathscr I^-$, a Penrose domain covering a neighborhood of spatial infinity $i^0$, and a future hyperboloidal domain attached to $\mathscr I^+$. The matching surfaces are identical conformal hypersurfaces in the adjacent charts. This yields a global evolution scheme connecting $\mathscr I^-$, the neighborhood of $i^0$, and $\mathscr I^+$ without artificial timelike outer boundaries and without interpolation between scri-fixing gauges.
  We implement the construction for spherically symmetric scalar waves, including free propagation, localized linear scattering potentials such as the Pöschl--Teller potential, and semilinear wave equations with cubic, quintic, and septic nonlinearities. The numerical experiments demonstrate stable propagation across the matching interfaces, direct extraction of radiation at $\mathscr I^+$, and fourth-order convergence for the free and linear-potential tests. The quintic and septic nonlinear tests exhibit approximately fourth-order convergence and recover the expected late-time tail rates. The cubic case, by contrast, shows only first-order convergence, revealing a limitation of our treatment near compactified boundaries when the conformally rescaled nonlinear source remains non-vanishing. These results validate the conformal matching strategy for long-time simulations, while identifying the boundary regularity issues that must be addressed using a more robust treatment of spatial infinity.

</details>

Method: The authors construct a Debye series decomposition of the scalar-field Green’s function in the frequency domain, inspired by scattering theory. Abstract: We introduce a new series decomposition of the waveform constructed in the spirit of Debye expansions in scattering theory, and we use this to analyse the time-domain response of compact, horizonless bodies to scalar-field perturbations on curved spacetimes. The Debye decomposition separates out direct exterior propagation, surface reflection, and successive transmissions through the interior of a compact body, and it provides an intuitive interpretation of the waveform in terms of geodesic trajectories. By analysing the quasinormal-mode (QNM) content of individual Debye terms, we set out a Debye-QNM description that is complementary to the standard QNM description. With this framework, we examine a scalar field propagating on two illustrative `Schwarzschild star' compact-body spacetimes: a neutron-star-like model \(R>3M\) and an ultracompact object \(R<3M\). We show that the Debye reconstruction matches well with the exact waveform, and that (unlike the standard QNM reconstruction) it converges even at early times, giving an accurate description of all waveform features including the prompt response. In the neutron-star case, the low-order Debye terms mainly describe the ringdown and a non-modal component associated with the sub-threshold branch cut. In the ultracompact case, the Debye series organizes the waveform into a prompt/ringdown contribution followed by a succession of individually resolved echo-like wavepackets. The new Debye-QNM expansion and the standard QNM expansion have complementary spectral interpretations: the former identifies modes in individual propagation channels, whereas the latter describes collective resonances that are resummations of the former. ### [31] [Detecting Gravitational-Wave Anisotropies with Simulation-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24082)

TL;DR: This paper introduces a Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) framework using neural network classifiers to detect anisotropies in the nanohertz gravitational-wave background (GWB) observed by Pulsar Timing Arrays (PTAs), outperforming traditional frequentist methods that assume Gaussian-distributed correlation estimators. The SBI approach boosts $3σ$ detection probabilities by ~90% for single-hotspot and ~200% for double-hotspot anisotropy scenarios.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current frequentist methods for detecting anisotropies in the gravitational-wave background rely on the unjustified assumption that correlation estimators derived from pulsar timing residuals follow a Gaussian distribution. This assumption degrades detection sensitivity, especially for localized anisotropic sources such as hotspots. The authors aim to overcome this limitation by leveraging modern machine learning techniques that can learn the true non-Gaussian statistics directly from simulated data.

当前用于探测引力波背景各向异性的频率学方法依赖于一个未经证实的假设，即从脉冲星计时残差导出的关联估计量服从高斯分布。这一假设会降低对局域化各向异性源（如热点）的探测灵敏度。作者旨在通过现代机器学习技术，直接从模拟数据中学习真实的非高斯统计特性，从而克服这一局限。

Method: The authors develop a Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) framework where a neural network classifier is trained on synthetic pulsar timing residual datasets generated under both isotropic and anisotropic (e.g., single- or double-hotspot) gravitational-wave background models. Instead of using an analytic Gaussian likelihood, the classifier learns the full statistical structure—including non-Gaussian features—of the correlation estimators. This learned likelihood is then used to perform hypothesis testing between isotropic and anisotropic GWB scenarios.
\n\n
作者构建了一个基于模拟的推理（SBI）框架，其中神经网络分类器在合成的脉冲星计时残差数据集上进行训练，这些数据集分别基于各向同性和各向异性（例如单热点或双热点）引力波背景模型生成。该方法摒弃了解析的高斯似然，转而让分类器学习关联估计量的完整统计结构（包括非高斯特征），并利用所学得的似然函数在各向同性与各向异性GWB模型之间进行假设检验。

Result: The SBI method significantly enhances detection sensitivity: the probability of achieving a $3σ$ detection increases by approximately 90% in single-hotspot configurations and by about 200% in double-hotspot configurations compared to standard frequentist approaches that assume Gaussianity of correlation estimators.
\n\n
SBI方法显著提升了探测灵敏度：与假设关联估计量服从高斯分布的标准频率学方法相比，在单热点构型下实现$3σ$探测的概率提升约90%，在双热点构型下提升约200%。

Conclusion: By replacing the Gaussian likelihood assumption with a data-driven neural network trained on realistic simulations, the proposed SBI framework captures the intrinsic non-Gaussianity of PTA correlation statistics and substantially improves the ability to detect anisotropic gravitational-wave backgrounds. This demonstrates the power of simulation-based machine learning for next-generation gravitational-wave astronomy.
\n\n
通过用基于真实模拟训练的数据驱动神经网络取代高斯似然假设，所提出的SBI框架能够捕捉PTA关联统计量的内在非高斯性，从而大幅提升探测各向异性引力波背景的能力。这展示了基于模拟的机器学习在下一代引力波天文学中的强大潜力。

Abstract: Over the last five years, multiple Pulsar Timing Array (PTA) collaborations have reported mounting evidence for a gravitational-wave background (GWB) at nanohertz frequencies. Measuring anisotropies in the sky distribution of the GWB power is one of the most promising ways to identify and characterize its source. These anisotropies are expected to manifest as deviations from the Hellings-Downs (HD) correlations between the timing residuals of different pulsars. Current search strategies include Bayesian methods, which model anisotropies in the timing residuals likelihood, and faster frequentist approaches, which construct correlation estimators from timing residuals and use these to test the isotropic assumption. However, frequentist methods rely on the assumption that correlation estimators are Gaussian-distributed, an assumption that is not justified and that -- as we will show -- severely limits detection sensitivity. In this work, we present a Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) framework that replaces the analytic Gaussian likelihood used in frequentist searches with a neural network classifier trained on synthetic data. This approach captures the non-Gaussian structure of the data and significantly improves performance. Specifically, we find that the probability of $3σ$ detection increases by approximately 90% for single-hotspot scenarios and by 200% for double-hotspot scenarios compared to standard frequentist methods.

Abstract: [abridged] We constrain a phenomenological dark radiation (DR) framework consisting of free-streaming and fluid-like components, providing a model-independent extension of the standard radiation sector. The tension with SH0ES is reduced from highly significant in $Λ$CDM to statistically non-significant for CMB+BAO data according to the $\mathcal{T}$-statistic. Bayesian model comparison shows no decisive preference for DR over $Λ$CDM when SH0ES is excluded, with results in the regime of weakly disfavoured. However, including SH0ES data leads to decisive Bayesian evidence in favour of the DR scenario. Overall, DR provides a compelling framework for resolving the Hubble tension. When CMB, BAO, Pantheon$+$ and SH0ES data are considered, we find an increased effective radiation content, $N_{\rm tot}=3.63^{+0.13}_{-0.15}$, with a fraction of free-streaming radiation, $f_{\rm fs}=0.392\pm 0.026$, a reduced sound horizon scale, $r_d = 141.8^{+1.3}_{-1.2}\,\mathrm{Mpc}$, and a higher primordial helium fraction, $Y_{\rm He}=0.2530 \pm 0.0017$, which lies at the level of approximately $\sim 2$-$2.5σ$ above direct determinations from metal-poor H II regions, while remaining broadly consistent with other abundance measurements within current uncertainties.

作者比较了多种核心坍缩SIDM子晕的参数化方案，变化包括：(1) 密度轮廓的时间演化（平滑坍缩 vs. 瞬时坍缩），(2) 坍缩处理方式（概率性系综 vs. 单个晕轨迹），以及 (3) 初始/最终密度轮廓参数（如核心尺度、中心密度）。他们为每种模型计算有效透镜偏折场的汇聚分量（$\\kappa$）和旋度分量（$\\beta$）的两点关联函数 $\\xi_\\kappa(\\theta)$ 和 $\\xi_\\beta(\\theta)$。

Conclusion: Robust interpretation of strong lensing constraints on SIDM requires careful treatment of core-collapse modeling uncertainties. 该联合早期和晚期修正模型从Planck+ACT+DESI+CMB透镜数据中得出$H_0 = 69.83 \\pm 0.98~\\mathrm{km\\,s^{-1}\\,Mpc^{-1}}$，将与SH0ES（$H_0 = 73.04 \\pm 1.04$）的张力降至约${\\sim}2.2\\sigma$，与H0DN的张力降至约${\\sim}2.9\\sigma$。当拟合中包含SH0ES和Pantheon+时，$H_0 = 70.20 \\pm 0.66~\\mathrm{km\\,s^{-1}\\,Mpc^{-1}}$。贝叶斯证据表明该模型相比$\\Lambda$CDM并无显著优势，尽管增加了参数，但拟合优度相当。

Abstract: We investigate an extension of $Λ$CDM in which a fraction of cold Dark Matter (DM) decays into invisible dark radiation (DR) around the radiation-matter equality epoch, together with a non-standard dark energy (DE) equation of state characterized by $w_0$. The Bayesian evidence suggests that this framework offers a fit to the relevant cosmological datasets at a statistically similar level as $Λ$CDM. It is observed that correlated early- and late-time modifications to the cosmological expansion history provide a more effective route to reducing the $H_0$ tension than either class of modification alone.

Abstract: We investigate whether early- and late-time dark energy could arise from a single scalar field. Adopting a bottom-up perspective, we first identify the sequence of dynamical regimes that any unified scenario must traverse to account for both an early dark energy phase relevant for pre-recombination solutions of the Hubble tension and the late-time acceleration of the Universe. We derive the corresponding requirements on the scalar energy density and equation of state. We then adopt a complementary top-down perspective and translate these requirements into constraints on the phase-space structure of minimally coupled scalar fields with tracking-like dynamics. We show that satisfying all requirements necessitates a potential with three distinct slopes, arranged in a steep-steeper-shallow hierarchy. This conclusion remains unchanged in the presence of conformal couplings to dark matter. These results place strong constraints on attempts to construct unified models of early- and late-time dark energy. We discuss implications for model-building.

They relate the generalized entropy function to the inflationary potential and compute predictions for $n_s$ and $r$. Parameter estimation is performed in two regimes: (i) direct fitting to $n_s$ and $r$, and (ii) marginalizing over observational uncertainty in the upper bound of $r$ (denoted $σ_r$) to assess its impact on posterior distributions of entropy parameters.

作者采用暴胀的Hamilton–Jacobi形式，引入Hubble参数$H(ϕ)$的一种新非线性参数化。他们将广义熵函数与暴胀势关联，并计算$n_s$和$r$的理论预言。参数估计分两个步骤进行：(i) 直接拟合$n_s$和$r$；(ii) 对$r$上限的观测不确定性（记为$σ_r$）进行边缘化，以评估其对熵参数后验分布的影响。

Result: The analysis yields tight constraints on generalized entropy parameters: Tsallis parameter $δ\simeq1.1-1.2$, Rényi parameter $α\sim\mathcal{O}(10^{-14})$, and Kaniadakis parameter $K\sim\mathcal{O}(10^{-17})$, all consistent with Planck/BICEP/Keck data. Conclusion: Generalized entropy formulations provide a theoretically consistent and observationally viable extension of slow-roll inflation. The Hamilton–Jacobi approach, combined with a flexible Hubble parametrization, enables precise mapping between entropy parameters and cosmological observables. 广义熵表述为慢滚暴胀提供了一个理论自洽且观测可行的扩展。结合灵活的Hubble参数化，Hamilton–Jacobi方法能够精确建立熵参数与宇宙学观测量之间的映射。未来对$r$（尤其是其不确定性$σ_r$）测量精度的提升将进一步检验这些模型，并可能区分不同的非广延统计框架。

Abstract: The slow-roll inflation paradigm can be systematically generalized within the framework of non-standard entropy formalisms, giving rise to a broad class of inflationary models that deviate from the conventional Bekenstein--Hawking case. We adopt a pragmatic observational strategy, employing the Hamilton--Jacobi formalism to establish a direct link between the inflationary potential, the generalized entropy function, and the resulting cosmological observables. In this approach we introduce a novel non-linear parametrization of the Hubble parameter, yielding sensible results, including consistency with recent observational data and new estimates of the cosmological parameters of the generalized entropy framework: the Tsallis parameter $δ\simeq1.1-1.2$, the Rényi parameter $α\sim\mathcal{O}(10^{-14})$, and the Kaniadakis statistics parameter $K\sim\mathcal{O}(10^{-17})$. Our analysis proceeds in two regimes: first, by constraining models directly with the primary inflationary parameters including the scalar spectral index ($n_s$) and the tensor-to-scalar ratio ($r$); second, by exploring the impact of the observational uncertainty on the upper bound of $r$ ($σ_r$), which we vary to assess its influence on parameter estimation. This dual approach yields complementary posterior distributions that restrict the viable parameter space of entropy-based inflationary models. We further highlight the implications of the Hamilton--Jacobi method for the dynamics of the inflationary epoch, the reheating process, and, as a secondary objective, the subsequent evolution of cosmic structure in the late universe.

They incorporate in-medium renormalization of vector couplings and distinguish between two regimes: superconducting protons (leading to unscreened longitudinal modes) and normal-conducting protons (where transverse photon screening modifies phase space). Temperature scaling of energy loss rates is derived analytically in both cases.

作者在中子星核心的超简并条件下，计算了标量、矢量和赝标量玻色子在轻子-轻子及轻子-质子碰撞中的轫致辐射发射率。他们纳入了矢量耦合的介质内重整化，并区分两种情形：超导质子（导致无屏蔽的纵向模式）和正常导电质子（横向光子屏蔽改变相空间）。在两种情况下解析推导了能量损失率的温度标度。

Result: For superconducting protons, scalar/vector boson emission scales as $T^4$, pseudoscalar as $T^6$, compared to $T^8$ for modified Urca neutrino losses. For normal-conducting protons, screening reduces the exponents by $1/3$, yielding $T^{11/3}$ (scalars/vectors) and $T^{17/3}$ (pseudoscalars). Notably, even muon-philic vectors are dominantly emitted by ultra-relativistic electrons due to in-medium coupling renormalization. These losses become relevant at late cooling stages when photon emission ($\\sim T^2$) dominates.

对于超导质子，标量/矢量玻色子辐射标度为 $T^4$，赝标量为 $T^6$，而修正Urca中微子损失为 $T^8$。对于正常导电质子，屏蔽使指数降低 $1/3$，得到 $T^{11/3}$（标量/矢量）和 $T^{17/3}$（赝标量）。值得注意的是，即使μ子亲和矢量玻色子也主要由超相对论电子辐射，这是由于介质内耦合重整化所致。这些损失在冷却晚期变得重要，此时光子辐射（$\\sim T^2$）占主导。

Conclusion: Neutron star cooling data can place strong constraints on leptophilic new bosons, but accurate limits require proper treatment of in-medium effects and the superconducting state of protons. The derived temperature scalings provide a robust framework for comparing exotic energy losses with standard cooling mechanisms.
