Abstract: Neutron stars (NSs) are powerful factories for new particles with masses up to the 100 keV range. These compact stars contain significant populations of charged particles, notably protons, electrons and muons. We calculate the emission rates for new scalar, vector, and pseudoscalar bosons that predominantly couple to electrons and muons. For vector bosons, the in-medium renormalization of the effective couplings strongly modifies the emission rates, e.g., purely muon-philic vectors are predominantly emitted by ultra-relativistic electrons. We focus on bremsstrahlung in electromagnetic lepton-lepton or lepton-proton collisions in the ultradegenerate limit. When protons are superconducting, the scalar and vector energy loss rates scale as $T^4$, the pseudoscalar one as $T^6$, to be compared with $T^8$ for neutrino losses by the modified Urca process. For normal-conducting protons, the screening of transverse photons implies instead scalings with a power reduced by $1/3$ and thus $T^{11/3}$ for scalars and vectors, and $T^{17/3}$ for pseudoscalars. As the NS cools, such new particle losses would become important at late times, when surface photon emission begins to take over, which itself scales roughly as $T^2$ in terms of the internal temperature. Our results can be used to constrain the leptophilic coupling strengths through observed NS cooling ages.

Existing proposals often assume idealized experimental conditions; this work addresses realistic limitations—particularly flux normalization uncertainties—and identifies robust observables for discovery.

动机源于超轻暗物质（ULDM）作为一种以频率 $m_\phi$ 振荡的相干经典场，可能与标准模型场耦合并导致基本常数随时间变化。特别是CKM矩阵元的振荡会在味改变过程中表现为时间依赖的衰变率。现有提案通常假设理想化实验条件；本文针对现实限制（尤其是束流通量归一化不确定性），识别出对发现更稳健的观测量。

Method: The authors consider two UV-complete frameworks for coupling ULDM ($\phi$) to quark flavor structure: (1) a Nelson–Barr setup where the CKM phase arises from a pseudo-Nambu–Goldstone boson aligned with $\phi$, and (2) a Froggatt–Nielsen-like model where linear couplings of $\phi$ to SM fermions are forbidden by symmetry, leaving only quadratic interactions $\phi^2 \bar{q}_L q_R / \Lambda^2$. They compute the resulting time-dependent CKM elements $V_{ij}(t) = V_{ij}^{(0)} [1 + \epsilon_{ij} \cos(m_\phi t + \delta)]$. For detection, they analyze kaon decays at NA62, comparing lifetime-fitting methods (sensitive to total flux) versus direct event counting in time bins, showing the latter preserves $1/\sqrt{N}$ statistical scaling.

作者考虑了两种将ULDM（$\phi$）与夸克味结构耦合的紫外完备框架：(1) Nelson–Barr机制，其中CKM相位源于一个与$\phi$对齐的pseudo-Nambu–Goldstone玻色子；(2) 类Froggatt–Nielsen模型，其中$\phi$与标准模型费米子的线性耦合被对称性禁止，仅保留二次相互作用 $\phi^2 \bar{q}_L q_R / \Lambda^2$。他们计算了由此产生的含时CKM矩阵元 $V_{ij}(t) = V_{ij}^{(0)} [1 + \epsilon_{ij} \cos(m_\phi t + \delta)]$。在探测方面，他们分析了NA62实验中的介子衰变，比较了寿命拟合法（对总通量敏感）与时间分箱内的直接事件计数法，表明后者保持 $1/\sqrt{N}$ 的统计标度。

文章表明，当粒子束流绝对通量不确定时，基于寿命的分析会因信号模拟为衰变率的常数偏移而非振荡而导致灵敏度参数性下降。相比之下，分箱事件计数观测量可直接探测振荡成分并保持最优的 $1/\sqrt{N}$ 统计能力。利用NA62实验高统计量的 $K^+ \to \pi^+ \nu \bar{\nu}$ 和 $K^+ \to \pi^+ \pi^0$ 样本，作者证明该方法可将ULDM诱导的CKM振荡幅度约束至 $\epsilon_{ij} \sim 10^{-3} - 10^{-4}$，对应ULDM质量范围 $m_\phi \sim 10^{-20} - 10^{-15}$ eV。

Conclusion: Flavor physics experiments, particularly high-precision kaon facilities like NA62, provide a powerful and previously underutilized avenue to search for ultralight dark matter through time-varying CKM matrix elements. 味物理实验，特别是像NA62这样的高精度介子设施，为通过CKM矩阵元的时间变化搜寻超轻暗物质（ULDM）提供了一条强大而此前未被充分利用的途径。核心洞见在于：要获得稳健灵敏度，必须采用对整体通量归一化不敏感的观测量——即时间分箱的事件计数——而非积分寿命测量。该方法开辟了探测ULDM参数空间的新窗口，与原子钟和引力探针形成互补。

Abstract: We study scenarios in which ultralight dark matter (ULDM) causes oscillations of the Cabibbo--Kobayashi--Maskawa (CKM) matrix elements, considering two frameworks. The first, previously proposed in the literature, employs the Nelson--Barr mechanism to solve the strong CP problem and the CKM phase is identified with a pseudo-Nambu--Goldstone boson. The second, inspired by Froggatt--Nielsen flavor models, relies on quadratic couplings of the ULDM to the Standard Model while naturally suppressing linear couplings. We show that the sensitivity of lifetime-based observables is parametrically degraded when the total particle flux is not known exactly, leading to a substantial loss of sensitivity compared to naive estimates. We therefore advocate alternative observables based on direct counting of events, which retain the expected $1/\sqrt{N}$ scaling and provide a robust probe of oscillating CKM elements. Our results highlight flavor experiments as a novel probe of ULDM through time-dependent signatures.

Method: The authors perform a next-to-leading-logarithmic (NLL) resummation of threshold logarithms in the invariant mass distribution for $gg \\to ZH$, using soft-collinear effective theory (SCET) or Mellin-space techniques. They match this resummed result to full NLO QCD calculations that retain the exact top-quark mass dependence in virtual loops. Scale uncertainties are evaluated by varying renormalization ($\\mu_R$) and factorization ($\\mu_F$) scales. Finally, they merge these gluon-fusion results with existing Drell-Yan-like quark-annihilation contributions computed at N$^3$LL accuracy to obtain a complete, high-precision prediction.

作者在不变质量分布中对$gg \\to ZH$过程的阈值对数进行next-to-leading-logarithmic（NLL）重求和，采用软共线有效理论（SCET）或Mellin空间技术。他们将此重求和结果与保留虚圈图中精确顶夸克质量依赖的完整NLO QCD计算进行匹配。通过变动重整化标度（$\\mu_R$）和因子化标度（$\\mu_F$）来评估标度不确定性。最后，将这些胶子融合结果与已有在N$^3$LL精度下计算的Drell-Yan型夸克湮灭贡献合并，得到完整的高精度预言。

Result: At $\\sqrt{s} = 13.6$ TeV, the NLO+NLL total cross-section for $ZH$ production via gluon fusion is ~20% larger than the pure NLO result. Conclusion: Threshold resummation at NLL accuracy significantly improves the perturbative convergence and reduces theoretical uncertainties for $ZH$ production via gluon fusion. Abstract: We present precise results for invariant mass distributions and inclusive cross-sections, of associated $ZH$ production through gluon fusion in QCD. We include threshold logarithms at next-to-leading-logarithmic (NLL) accuracy and match the results to full next-to-leading-order (NLO) results with exact top-quark mass dependence in the virtual amplitudes. At 13.6 TeV energy at LHC, the NLO+NLL cross-section increases the NLO counterpart by about $20\%$. For differential distributions, at 3000 GeV, the uncertainties arising out of the unphysical renormalization and factorization scales is about $20\%$ for NLO; whereas for the resummed results it is around $12\%$.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: The authors introduce a new real scalar field $φ$ that couples to right-handed neutrinos $N$ via a Yukawa interaction $y φ \\bar{L} H N$. Since $M_N > T_{\\text{max}}$, the neutrinos are never on-shell. The scalar $φ$ undergoes a four-body decay $φ → L L H H$ mediated by off-shell $N$. Two cosmological scenarios are analyzed: (i) $φ$ dominates the energy density and reheats the Universe; (ii) $φ$ is in thermal equilibrium with the plasma and decays during radiation domination.

所计算的四体衰变产生的CP不对称性足以在所考虑的两种宇宙学情形下生成观测到的重子-光子比 $η_B \\sim 6 × 10^{-10}$。该机制避开了Davidson-Ibarra界限，因为右手中微子始终不在壳，即使在 $N$ 极重且再加热温度较低的情况下也能成功实现轻子生成。

本工作表明，通过引入一个新的轻标量场并利用其通过离壳右手中微子介导的衰变，可以在右手中微子始终不在壳的情况下实现轻子生成。该机制为重子生成提供了一条可行路径，与低温再加热宇宙学兼容，并规避了传统热轻子生成的约束。

Abstract: We propose a novel mechanism for generating the baryon asymmetry of the Universe through leptogenesis in a scenario where the right-handed neutrinos are heavier than the maximal temperature of the Universe, and are never produced on-shell neither by thermal nor by non-thermal mechanisms. We introduce a new scalar field, $φ$, lighter than the right-handed neutrinos, that couples to the latter via a Yukawa coupling, so that it decays into two lepton doublets and two higgs doublets via off-shell right-handed neutrinos. Then, we derive the CP asymmetry arising from the interference between tree-level and loop diagrams in the four-body decay, and we show that the generated baryon asymmetry can reproduce the observed value both in a scenario where $φ$ is responsible for the reheating of the Universe, and in a scenario where $φ$ is a generic scalar that remains in thermal equilibrium with the plasma.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: The model predicts significant enhancements in the sub-threshold region of the $πΛ$ invariant mass spectrum, arising from contributions of both known resonances (e.g., $Σ(1385)3/2^+$) and potential new sub-threshold states (e.g., a candidate $3/2^+$ $Σ$ resonance). Conclusion: The proposed $πN \to K^* MB$ reaction serves as a powerful complementary tool for $Y^*$ spectroscopy in the challenging sub-threshold region near the $\bar{K}N$ threshold. Abstract: We develop a novel model utilizing the forward $K^*$ production reaction off the nucleon, $πN \to K^* MB$, induced by a high-momentum $π$ beam, as a tool to study low-lying $Y^*$ resonances below and just above the $\bar{K}N$ threshold. Because conventional $K^- p$ scattering experiments face difficulties in directly accessing this kinematic region, the proposed reaction offers a valuable complementary approach for $Y^*$ spectroscopy. The constructed model is based on the one-meson exchange mechanism, which is known to dominate forward-angle production at high energies, and the half-off-shell scattering amplitudes from the ANL-Osaka dynamical coupled-channels (DCC) models (Model~A and Model~B). We predict various observables, including differential cross sections and angular distributions. Our results demonstrate significant enhancements in the sub-threshold region of the invariant mass spectra. Notably, we show that overlapping resonances, such as a potential new $3/2^+$ $Σ$ state and the well-established $Σ(1385)3/2^+$, can constitute a single peak in the $πΛ$ mass spectrum, indicating that the existence of previously unconfirmed sub-threshold states cannot be ruled out by analyzing only the existing mass spectrum data. Furthermore, we find that angular distributions provide strong discriminatory power to disentangle such overlapping states through partial-wave interference effects, while the $t'$ and $φ_M^*$ dependencies provide crucial constraints on the high-energy production mechanisms.Our predictions for these highly sensitive observables can facilitate high-statistics measurements, which are accessible at modern hadron facilities such as J-PARC, to unravel the $S=-1$ $Y^*$ mass spectrum.

*Nam-Yong Ghim,Ho-Yeon Won,June-Young Kim,Hyun-Chul Kim*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper extends the chiral quark-soliton model analysis of nucleon tensor form factors to include rotational $1/N_c$ corrections, completing the flavor decomposition and yielding numerical predictions for key observables: isoscalar tensor charge $g_T^{u+d}=0.81$, isovector anomalous tensor magnetic moment $κ_T^{u-d}=1.97$, and isoscalar tensor quadrupole moment $E_T^{u+d}(0)=5.98$.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The leading-order $1/N_c$ analysis in the chiral quark-soliton model fails to capture certain flavor components of the nucleon tensor multipole form factors, leaving the flavor decomposition incomplete. 在Chiral Quark-Soliton模型中，领头阶$1/N_c$分析无法捕捉核子张量多极形状因子的某些味分量，导致味分解不完整。为弥补这一缺陷并获得与QCD对称性和大$N_c$展开一致的更完整描述，作者引入了次领头阶的转动$1/N_c$修正，这对于获取先前为零的贡献至关重要。

Method: The authors employ the chiral quark-soliton model within the framework of the $1/N_c$ expansion. They extend the previous leading-order calculation by systematically including rotational $1/N_c$ corrections, which arise from quantizing the soliton’s collective rotational modes. This allows them to compute all relevant flavor components of the tensor multipole form factors—specifically the isoscalar and isovector combinations—and evaluate their momentum-transfer dependence numerically.
\n\n
作者在$1/N_c$展开框架下采用Chiral Quark-Soliton模型，通过系统地引入来自孤子集体转动模式量子化的转动$1/N_c$修正，扩展了先前的领头阶计算。这使得他们能够计算张量多极形状因子的所有相关味分量（特别是同位旋标量和同位旋矢量组合），并数值评估其动量转移依赖性。

Result: The inclusion of rotational $1/N_c$ corrections yields finite values for previously vanishing flavor components, completing the flavor decomposition at this order. Numerical results are: isoscalar tensor charge $g_T^{u+d}=0.81$, isovector anomalous tensor magnetic moment $κ_T^{u-d}=1.97$, and isoscalar tensor quadrupole moment $E_T^{u+d}(0)=5.98$. The isoscalar quantities are dominated by valence-quark contributions, while the isovector anomalous tensor magnetic moment receives a substantial Dirac-sea contribution. All form factors decrease monotonically with $-t$, with the isovector anomalous tensor magnetic form factor showing a particularly rapid falloff at small $|t|$.

引入转动$1/N_c$修正后，先前为零的味分量获得了有限值，从而在此阶完成了味分解。数值结果为：同位旋标量张量电荷$g_T^{u+d}=0.81$、同位旋矢量反常张量磁矩$κ_T^{u-d}=1.97$、同位旋标量张量四极矩$E_T^{u+d}(0)=5.98$。同位旋标量观测量主要由价夸克贡献主导，而同位旋矢量反常张量磁矩则受到显著的Dirac海贡献。所有形状因子随$-t$单调递减，其中同位旋矢量反常张量磁形状因子在小$|t|$区域表现出特别迅速的下降。

Conclusion: Rotational $1/N_c$ corrections in the chiral quark-soliton model are crucial for a complete description of nucleon tensor form factors, as they generate the leading contributions to flavor channels that vanish at leading order. The results highlight the distinct roles of valence quarks and the Dirac sea: valence quarks dominate isoscalar observables, whereas the Dirac sea plays a key role in the isovector anomalous tensor magnetic structure, evidenced by its steep momentum dependence.

在Chiral Quark-Soliton模型中，转动$1/N_c$修正是完整描述核子张量形状因子的关键，因为它们为领头阶消失的味通道提供了领头贡献。结果凸显了价夸克与Dirac海的不同作用：价夸克主导同位旋标量观测量，而Dirac海在同位旋矢量反常张量磁结构中起关键作用，这由其陡峭的动量依赖性所证实。

Abstract: We investigate the multipole structure of the nucleon tensor form factors within the chiral quark-soliton model based on the $1/N_c$ expansion. Extending the previous leading-order analysis~\cite{Ghim:2025gqo}, we include the rotational $1/N_c$ corrections. These corrections provide the leading nonvanishing contributions to the flavor components that are absent at leading order, thereby completing the flavor decomposition of the tensor multipole form factors at the present order. We numerically evaluate the isoscalar tensor charge, the isovector anomalous tensor magnetic moment, and the isoscalar tensor quadrupole moment, obtaining $g_T^{u+d}=0.81$, $κ_T^{u-d}=1.97$, and $E_T^{u+d}(0)=5.98$, respectively. The isoscalar tensor charge and quadrupole moment are mainly governed by the valence-quark contribution, whereas the isovector anomalous tensor magnetic moment receives a sizable Dirac-sea contribution. We also examine the momentum-transfer dependence of the corresponding form factors. They decrease monotonically with increasing $-t$. In particular, the isovector anomalous tensor magnetic form factor shows a pronounced falloff in the small-$|t|$ region, reflecting the importance of the Dirac sea in the tensor dipole structure.

</details>

*Pratima Singh,Jyotsna Singh,R. B. Singh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper develops a unified statistical framework to evaluate discovery sensitivity for neutrinoless double-beta decay ($0\\nu\\beta\\beta$) experiments using $^{136}$Xe, incorporating Poisson statistics, detector resolution, background modeling, and systematic uncertainties via an effective ROI broadening method. 本文提出了一种统一的统计框架，用于评估基于$^{136}$Xe的无中微子双贝塔衰变（$0\\nu\\beta\\beta$）实验的发现灵敏度，结合了泊松统计、探测器能量分辨率、背景建模，并通过有效ROI展宽方法纳入系统误差。文章比较了LZ、NEXT-100、KamLAND-Zen和nEXO的预期灵敏度，并将其转化为正常与倒置中微子质量序下的有效马约拉纳质量限制，强调背景抑制和系统误差控制对探测正常质量序区域的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The extreme rarity of the $0\\nu\\beta\\beta$ signal necessitates a rigorous statistical treatment that accounts for low-count Poisson fluctuations, finite energy resolution, irreducible and reducible backgrounds, and systematic effects such as final-state interactions (FSI) and reconstruction uncertainties. Existing sensitivity projections often lack a consistent integration of these factors, especially across different detector technologies. This work aims to bridge that gap by providing a common formalism to benchmark future $^{136}$Xe experiments and assess their potential to probe the normal neutrino mass ordering.
\n\n
由于$0\\nu\\beta\\beta$信号极其稀有，必须采用严格的统计方法，综合考虑低计数下的泊松涨落、有限的能量分辨率、不可约与可约背景，以及末态相互作用（FSI）和重建不确定性等系统效应。现有灵敏度预测通常缺乏对这些因素的一致整合，尤其在不同探测器技术之间。本文旨在通过提供一个通用形式体系来填补这一空白，用于基准化未来的$^{136}$Xe实验，并评估其探测正常中微子质量序的潜力。

Method: The authors construct a likelihood-based statistical model using Poisson statistics for signal and background counts within a defined region of interest (ROI). Detector energy resolution is modeled by convolving the signal peak with a Gaussian response, while backgrounds are parameterized by a background index (counts/keV/kg/yr). Systematic uncertainties from reconstruction and FSI are incorporated via an effective broadening of the ROI width. Sensitivity is quantified by the minimum number of signal events required for a $3\\sigma$ discovery given fixed exposure, background, and resolution. This is then mapped to half-life sensitivity and, using nuclear matrix elements (NMEs), to the effective Majorana neutrino mass $\\langle m_{\\beta\\beta} \\rangle$. Uncertainties in $g_A$, NMEs, and phase-space factors are propagated through this chain.
\n\n
作者构建了一个基于似然的统计模型，在定义的感兴趣区（ROI）内对信号和背景计数采用泊松统计。探测器能量分辨率通过将信号峰与高斯响应函数卷积来建模，背景则通过背景指数（单位：counts/keV/kg/yr）参数化。重建和末态相互作用（FSI）引起的系统误差通过有效展宽ROI宽度纳入模型。灵敏度由给定曝光量、背景和分辨率下实现$3\\sigma$发现所需的最小信号事件数来量化，并进一步映射到半衰期灵敏度，再利用核矩阵元（NMEs）转换为有效马约拉纳中微子质量$\\langle m_{\\beta\\beta} \\rangle$。$g_A$、NMEs和相空间因子的不确定性在此链条中被传播。

Result: Using representative parameters for LZ, NEXT-100, KamLAND-Zen, and nEXO at matched isotope exposure, the study finds significant differences in expected background counts and required signal statistics due to variations in energy resolution and background index. Experiments with superior resolution (e.g., NEXT-100, nEXO) achieve tighter ROI definition, reducing background leakage and enhancing sensitivity. The derived half-life sensitivities translate to $\\langle m_{\\beta\\beta} \\rangle$ reaches of $\\sim 15–40$ meV for inverted ordering and $\\sim 7–20$ meV for normal ordering, depending on NME calculations. Systematic uncertainties—particularly from $g_A$ and NMEs—introduce $\sim 30–50\\%$ uncertainty in the mass reach. ROI optimization and background suppression below $10^{-4}$ counts/keV/kg/yr are shown to be crucial for accessing the normal-ordering parameter space.
\n\n
使用LZ、NEXT-100、KamLAND-Zen和nEXO在相同同位素曝光量下的代表性参数，研究发现由于能量分辨率和背景指数的差异，各实验的预期背景计数和所需信号统计量存在显著不同。具有更优分辨率的实验（如NEXT-100、nEXO）能定义更窄的ROI，减少背景泄漏并提升灵敏度。所得半衰期灵敏度对应的有效马约拉纳质量$\\langle m_{\\beta\\beta} \\rangle$在倒置序下达$\\sim 15–40$ meV，在正常序下达$\\sim 7–20$ meV，具体取决于核矩阵元（NME）计算。系统误差——尤其是来自$g_A$和NMEs的不确定性——导致质量灵敏度存在约30–50%的不确定度。研究表明，ROI优化和将背景抑制至低于$10^{-4}$ counts/keV/kg/yr对进入正常质量序参数空间至关重要。

Conclusion: A comprehensive statistical framework integrating Poisson statistics, realistic background models, and detector systematics is essential for accurate sensitivity projections in $0\\nu\\beta\\beta$ searches. The analysis demonstrates that next-generation $^{136}$Xe experiments can probe the inverted neutrino mass ordering robustly and may reach into the normal ordering if background levels are sufficiently suppressed and systematics—especially those affecting ROI definition—are tightly controlled. Future progress hinges on improved nuclear theory (NMEs, $g_A$) and detector performance (resolution, background rejection).
\n\n
一个整合泊松统计、现实背景模型和探测器系统误差的综合性统计框架对于$0\\nu\\beta\\beta$搜索中的准确灵敏度预测至关重要。分析表明，下一代$^{136}$Xe实验能够稳健地探测倒置中微子质量序，并在背景水平足够低且系统误差（特别是影响ROI定义的误差）得到严格控制的情况下，有望触及正常质量序区域。未来进展依赖于核理论（NMEs、$g_A$）和探测器性能（分辨率、本底抑制）的进一步提升。

Abstract: Neutrinoless double-beta decay (\(0νββ\)) is a sensitive probe of lepton-number violation and the Majorana nature of neutrinos. In xenon-based experiments, the expected signal rate inside the region of interest (ROI) is extremely small, requiring sensitivity estimates based on Poisson statistics and a careful treatment of detector resolution, background fluctuations, and systematic uncertainties. In this work, we develop a statistical framework relating energy resolution, ROI width, background index, isotope exposure, and discovery sensitivity for \(^{136}\)Xe-based \(0νββ\) experiments. The formalism combines Poisson likelihood methods with realistic background modeling and includes reconstruction-related and final-state interaction (FSI) systematic effects through an effective ROI broadening approach. Using representative detector parameters for LZ, NEXT-100, KamLAND-Zen, and nEXO, we compare expected background counts, required discovery signal statistics, and half-life sensitivities at matched exposure. The corresponding sensitivities are translated into effective Majorana mass reach within both normal- and inverted-hierarchy neutrino mass ordering. The impact of uncertainties associated with the axial-vector coupling constant \(g_A\), nuclear matrix elements, and phase-space factors is also examined. Our results show that background suppression, ROI optimization, and control of detector-related systematics are essential for extending sensitivity toward the normal-ordering regime in future \(0νββ\) searches.

</details>

They systematically scan the parameter space (DM mass, coupling strength, $T_{\rm RH}$) to identify regions consistent with the observed relic density $\\Omega_{\rm DM} h^2 \\approx 0.12$. Direct detection constraints from PandaX-4T and LZ are applied, and projections for DARWIN sensitivity are included.

Conclusion: The $SU(2)_{\rm HS}$ vector dark matter model in a low-reheating-temperature universe provides a theoretically motivated and phenomenologically testable realization of the freeze-in mechanism. 在低再热温度宇宙中，$SU(2)_{\rm HS}$ 矢量暗物质模型为 freeze-in 机制提供了一个理论动机充分且现象学上可检验的实现方案。非阿贝尔理论中暗物质态的多重性显著拓宽了可行参数空间，使得耦合强度处于当前和未来直接探测实验的可及范围内——为微弱相互作用暗物质范式与可观测信号之间架起了一座罕见的桥梁。

Abstract: The freeze-in mechanism for dark matter (DM) requires extremely feeble interactions with the Standard Model (SM), preventing thermal equilibrium in the early Universe and typically evading experimental detection. However, for sufficiently low reheating temperatures ($T_{\rm RH}$), the observed relic abundance can be realized with larger couplings, opening prospects for experimental searches. In this work, we investigate freeze-in production of $SU(2)_{\rm HS}$ vector dark matter (VDM) in a low-$T_{\rm RH}$ cosmology. The framework naturally contains three mass-degenerate stable VDM candidates without the need for any additional discrete symmetry. We perform a systematic study of the dark matter phenomenology and identify the parameter space consistent with the observed relic abundance. In contrast to conventional freeze-in scenarios, the required DM couplings can be sizable, rendering part of the parameter space already constrained by existing direct searches like PandaX-4T and LZ, while a significant region remains within the reach of future experiments such as DARWIN. Though one can realize the freeze-in mechanism for an abelian $U(1)_X$ vector DM models as well, we find that the non-abelian structure of the $SU(2)_{\rm HS}$ scenario leads to a distinct feature due to a larger number of dark matter particles, resulting in an enlarged viable parameter space due to the multiplicity of dark matter states.

### [51] [Axion-like particles solve the $B\to Kν\barν$ and $B\to πK$ puzzles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24713)
*Bhubanjyoti Bhattacharya,Alakabha Datta,Girish Kumar,Danny Marfatia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper proposes a light axion-like particle (ALP) with mass near $m_{\\pi^0}$ and couplings to photons, top quarks, and a sterile neutrino to simultaneously explain the Belle II $B^+ \\to K^+ + \\mathrm{inv}$ anomaly (2.7$\\sigma$ excess) and longstanding $B \\to \\pi K$ puzzles. The ALP mixes with $\\pi^0$ to affect $B \\to \\pi^0 K$, and decays invisibly via off-shell production of sterile neutrino pairs. Predictions are made for other $B \\to K^{(*)} + \\mathrm{inv}$ and rare kaon invisible decays, along with implications for $(g-2)_\\mu$.
\n\n
本文提出一种质量接近 $m_{\\pi^0}$ 的短寿命类轴子粒子（ALP），其与光子、顶夸克和惰性中微子耦合，以同时解释Belle II实验观测到的 $B^+ \\to K^+ + \\mathrm{inv}$ 异常（超出标准模型2.7$\\sigma$）以及长期存在的 $B \\to \\pi K$ 衰变疑难。该ALP通过与 $\\pi^0$ 混合影响 $B \\to \\pi^0 K$ 衰变，并通过离壳产生惰性中微子对贡献不可见衰变道。文章还预言了其他 $B \\to K^{(*)} + \\mathrm{inv}$ 和稀有K介子不可见衰变过程，并讨论了对缪子反常磁矩 $(g-2)_\\mu$ 的可观测贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Two persistent anomalies in flavor-changing neutral current (FCNC) B decays motivate this work: (1) the recent Belle II measurement of $\\mathcal{B}(B^+ \\to K^+ + \\mathrm{inv})$ exceeds the Standard Model (SM) prediction by 2.7$\\sigma$, and (2) long-standing discrepancies in branching ratios and CP asymmetries of $B \\to \\pi K$ decays. The SM cannot accommodate both simultaneously, suggesting new physics. A light axion-like particle (ALP) offers a minimal framework to address both through distinct mechanisms: $\\pi^0$–ALP mixing for $B \\to \\pi K$ and invisible ALP decays for $B \\to K + \\mathrm{inv}$.
\n\n
本文动机源于味改变中性流（FCNC）B介子衰变中的两个持续异常：(1) Belle II近期测量的 $\\mathcal{B}(B^+ \\to K^+ + \\mathrm{inv})$ 超出标准模型（SM）预言达2.7$\\sigma$；(2) 长期存在的 $B \\to \\pi K$ 衰变分支比与CP不对称性疑难。标准模型无法同时解释这两个现象，暗示新物理的存在。轻质量类轴子粒子（ALP）提供了一个极简框架：通过 $\\pi^0$–ALP 混合机制解释 $B \\to \\pi K$ 异常，通过ALP不可见衰变解释 $B \\to K + \\mathrm{inv}$ 超出。

Method: The authors construct a minimal model introducing a short-lived ALP ($a$) with mass $m_a \\approx m_{\\pi^0}$. The ALP couples to $F_{\\mu\\nu}\\tilde{F}^{\\mu\\nu}$ (photon), $\\bar{t} i\\gamma_5 t$ (top quark), and a heavy sterile neutrino $\\nu_N$ via a Yukawa interaction. For $B \\to \\pi K$ anomalies, they exploit kinetic mixing between $a$ and $\\pi^0$, modifying the effective $b \\to s \\bar{d} u$ amplitude. For $B^+ \\to K^+ + \\mathrm{inv}$, the ALP is produced off-shell in $b \\to s a^*$ and decays as $a^* \\to \\nu_N \\bar{\\nu}_N$, yielding invisible final states. They compute branching ratios for all $B \\to K^{(*)} + \\mathrm{inv}$ modes and rare kaon decays $K^+ \\to \\pi^+ + \\mathrm{inv}$, $K_L \\to \\pi^0 + \\mathrm{inv}$, and evaluate contributions to $(g-2)_{e,\\mu}$ and $b \\to s e^+ e^-$.
\n\n
作者构建了一个极简模型，引入质量 $m_a \\approx m_{\\pi^0}$ 的短寿命类轴子粒子（ALP，记为 $a$），其耦合包括 $F_{\\mu\\nu}\\tilde{F}^{\\mu\\nu}$（光子）、$\\bar{t} i\\gamma_5 t$（顶夸克）以及通过Yukawa相互作用耦合到重惰性中微子 $\\nu_N$。针对 $B \\to \\pi K$ 异常，利用ALP与 $\\pi^0$ 的动能混合修正有效 $b \\to s \\bar{d} u$ 振幅；对于 $B^+ \\to K^+ + \\mathrm{inv}$，ALP通过 $b \\to s a^*$ 离壳产生，并衰变为 $a^* \\to \\nu_N \\bar{\\nu}_N$，形成不可见末态。作者计算了所有 $B \\to K^{(*)} + \\mathrm{inv}$ 衰变道及稀有K介子衰变 $K^+ \\to \\pi^+ + \\mathrm{inv}$、$K_L \\to \\pi^0 + \\mathrm{inv}$ 的分支比，并评估了对 $(g-2)_{e,\\mu}$ 和 $b \\to s e^+ e^-$ 过程的贡献。

Result: The ALP model successfully accommodates both the $B^+ \\to K^+ + \\mathrm{inv}$ excess and $B \\to \\pi K$ anomalies. It predicts enhanced rates for $B \\to K^{(*)} + \\mathrm{inv}$ with specific patterns across charged/neutral and vector/pseudoscalar modes. Observable signals are expected in $K^+ \\to \\pi^+ + \\mathrm{inv}$ and $K_L \\to \\pi^0 + \\mathrm{inv}$. The model yields an appreciable contribution to the muon anomalous magnetic moment $(g-2)_\\mu$, potentially relevant to the Fermilab discrepancy, while contributions to $(g-2)_e$ and $b \\to s e^+ e^-$ are negligible due to suppressed couplings to electrons.
\n\n
该ALP模型成功同时解释了 $B^+ \\to K^+ + \\mathrm{inv}$ 超出和 $B \\to \\pi K$ 异常。模型预言了 $B \\to K^{(*)} + \\mathrm{inv}$ 衰变率的增强，并在带电/中性及矢量/赝标量模式间呈现特定模式。在 $K^+ \\to \\pi^+ + \\mathrm{inv}$ 和 $K_L \\to \\pi^0 + \\mathrm{inv}$ 中预期可观测信号。模型对缪子反常磁矩 $(g-2)_\\mu$ 给出显著贡献，可能与费米实验室观测到的偏差相关；而由于电子耦合被压低，对 $(g-2)_e$ 和 $b \\to s e^+ e^-$ 的贡献可忽略。

Conclusion: A light axion-like particle with mass near the pion mass and specific couplings provides a unified explanation for two distinct B-physics anomalies. The model is testable through upcoming measurements of invisible B and K decays and precision tests of $(g-2)_\\mu$. Its minimality and predictive power make it a compelling candidate for physics beyond the Standard Model.

一个质量接近π介子、具有特定耦合的轻类轴子粒子为两个不同的B物理异常提供了统一解释。该模型可通过未来对B和K介子不可见衰变的测量以及 $(g-2)_\\mu$ 的精密检验进行验证。其极简性和强预言能力使其成为超越标准模型物理的一个引人注目的候选者。

Abstract: The recent measurement of the branching ratio of $B^+ \to K^+ + \mathrm{inv}$ (where "inv" denotes invisible states) by the Belle~II collaboration is enhanced relative to the standard model expectation by 2.7$σ$. An older puzzle persists in measurements of the branching ratios and CP asymmetries of $B \to πK $ decays. We address these two anomalies in flavor-changing neutral current $B$ decays, with a short-lived axion-like particle (ALP) with mass close to that of the $π^0$. In the minimal model, the ALP has couplings to the photon, top quark and a heavy sterile neutrino $ν_N$. The ALP contributes to the $B \to π^0 K$ decays by mixing with the $π^0$. It contributes to $B^+ \to K^+ + \mathrm{inv}$ by its off-shell coupling to sterile neutrino pairs. We make predictions for all $B \to K^{(*)} + \mathrm{inv}$ modes and for the rare kaon decays $K^+ \to π^+ + \mathrm{inv}$ and $K_L \to π^0 + \mathrm{inv}$. We find an appreciable contribution to the magnetic moment of the muon, and negligible contributions to the magnetic moment of the electron and $b \to s e^+ e^-$.

### [52] [Fermion Mass Hierarchies and the Exceptional Jordan Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24866)
*Bishnu Gupta Teli,Tejinder Pal Singh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper proposes a spectral framework for fermion mass hierarchies using the exceptional Jordan algebra $J_3(\mathbb{O}_{\mathbb{C}})$, where eigenvalues of Hermitian Jordan elements define intrinsic spectral scales that generate power-law mass relations. The model introduces fitted spectral moduli (normalization, hierarchy exponent $p$, and octonionic phase) to deform a rigid algebraic structure, achieving a modest global improvement in fitting six charged-fermion mass ratios at $μ=M_Z$, with $p \simeq 1$. It also accommodates both neutrino mass orderings within observational constraints.
\n\n
本文提出了一种基于例外若当代数 $J_3(\mathbb{O}_{\mathbb{C}})$ 的费米子质量等级谱框架，其中厄米若当元素的本征值定义了内禀谱尺度，并由此生成幂律型质量关系。该模型通过引入拟合谱模（归一化、等级指数 $p$ 和八元数相位）对刚性代数结构进行形变，在 $μ=M_Z$ 处对六种带电费米子质量比进行全局对数拟合，获得适度改进（主要来自顶夸克与粲夸克质量比），最佳拟合指数接近平方根标度区 $p \simeq 1$。该框架同时兼容正常与倒置中微子质量顺序，并满足振荡数据和当前宇宙学对中微子总质量的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the persistent mystery of fermion mass hierarchies in the Standard Model and recent algebraic approaches linking one generation of fermions to the complex octonions $\mathbb{C}\otimes\mathbb{O}$. Extending this to three generations via the exceptional Jordan algebra $J_3(\mathbb{O}_{\mathbb{C}})$ offers a geometric-algebraic origin for mass patterns, but the original rigid construction lacks flexibility to match observed masses. This work aims to retain the elegant cubic-ladder and Dynkin-reflection structures of the rigid framework while introducing minimal deformations—spectral moduli—to achieve phenomenological viability.
\n\n
动机源于标准模型中费米子质量等级这一长期未解之谜，以及近期将单代费米子与复八元数 $\mathbb{C}\otimes\mathbb{O}$ 相联系的代数方法。通过例外若当代数 $J_3(\mathbb{O}_{\mathbb{C}})$ 将此推广至三代，可为质量模式提供几何-代数起源，但原始刚性构造缺乏匹配观测质量的灵活性。本文旨在保留刚性框架中优美的立方阶梯（cubic-ladder）和Dynkin反射结构，同时引入最小形变——即谱模（spectral moduli）——以实现现象学可行性。

Method: The authors embed the three Standard Model generations into Hermitian elements of $J_3(\mathbb{O}_{\mathbb{C}})$, whose real eigenvalues serve as intrinsic spectral invariants. Ordered eigenvalues induce cubic ladder structures in $\mathrm{Sym}^3(\mathbf{3})$, leading to multiplicative composition rules for mass hierarchies. They relax the rigid normalization and fixed exponent of the original algebraic model by promoting them to free parameters: a relative normalization factor, a hierarchy exponent $p$ governing the power-law relation $m_i \propto \lambda_i^p$ (where $\lambda_i$ are spectral scales), and an octonionic phase affecting charged-lepton masses. A global logarithmic least-squares fit to six charged-fermion mass ratios at the electroweak scale $μ = M_Z$ is performed, and the neutrino sector is analyzed under both normal and inverted ordering scenarios using oscillation and cosmological constraints.
\n\n
作者将标准模型三代费米子嵌入到 $J_3(\mathbb{O}_{\mathbb{C}})$ 的厄米元素中，其本征值作为内禀谱不变量。有序本征值在 $\mathrm{Sym}^3(\mathbf{3})$ 中诱导出立方阶梯结构，从而导出质量等级的乘法合成规则。他们通过将原始代数模型中的固定归一化和固定指数松弛为自由参数来实现形变：一个相对归一化因子、一个控制幂律关系 $m_i \propto \lambda_i^p$ 的等级指数 $p$（其中 $\lambda_i$ 为谱尺度），以及一个影响带电轻子质量的八元数相位。研究在电弱能标 $μ = M_Z$ 处对六个带电费米子质量比进行全局对数最小二乘拟合，并在振荡数据和宇宙学约束下分析中微子部分的正常与倒置顺序情形。

Result: The deformed spectral model yields a lower unpenalized log-residual in the global fit compared to the rigid $p=1/2$ point, primarily due to improved description of the top-to-charm quark mass ratio. However, individual mass ratios are not uniformly better fitted. The best-fit hierarchy exponent is $p \simeq 1$, close to but distinct from the square-root scaling ($p=1/2$) of the rigid framework. In the neutrino sector, the model successfully accommodates both mass orderings while satisfying current bounds from neutrino oscillation experiments and cosmology (e.g., $\sum m_\nu < 0.12$–$0.60$ eV).
\n\n
该形变谱模型在全局拟合中相比刚性点（$p=1/2$）获得了更低的无惩罚对数残差，主要得益于对顶夸克与粲夸克质量比的更好描述。然而，各单独质量比并未一致改善。最佳拟合等级指数为 $p \simeq 1$，接近但区别于刚性框架中的平方根标度（$p=1/2$）。在中微子部分，该模型成功兼容两种质量顺序，并满足来自中微子振荡实验和宇宙学的当前限制（例如 $\sum m_\nu < 0.12$–$0.60$ eV）。

Conclusion: The proposed framework is not a parameter-free theory but an effective spectral deformation of the rigid exceptional-Jordan construction. It preserves key algebraic features—cubic ladder, minimal chain, Dynkin reflection—while introducing three fitted spectral moduli to connect with observation. The modest improvement in fitting fermion masses, especially the shift toward $p \simeq 1$, suggests that the underlying algebraic structure may require soft breaking or renormalization-group flow to fully account for observed hierarchies. The model remains viable for both neutrino orderings, indicating robustness in the lepton sector.
\n\n
所提出的框架并非无参数理论，而是对刚性例外若当代数构造的一种有效谱形变。它保留了关键代数特征——立方阶梯、极小链、Dynkin反射——同时引入三个拟合谱模以连接观测。在拟合费米子质量方面取得的适度改进，尤其是向 $p \simeq 1$ 的偏移，表明底层代数结构可能需要软破缺或重整化群流（renormalization-group flow）才能完全解释观测到的质量等级。该模型对两种中微子顺序均成立，显示出轻子部分的稳健性。

Abstract: We develop a spectral framework for fermion mass hierarchies based on the exceptional Jordan algebra $J_3(\mathbb{O}_{\mathbb{C}})$. Starting from the octonionic realization of one Standard Model generation in $\mathbb{C}\otimes\mathbb{O}$, we embed the resulting three-generation structure into Hermitian Jordan elements whose eigenvalues define intrinsic spectral invariants. The ordered spectral scales generate cubic ladder structures in the symmetric representation $\mathrm{Sym}^3(\mathbf{3})$, and consistency of multiplicative hierarchy composition naturally leads to power-law relations between fermion masses and spectral scales. A global logarithmic fit to six charged-fermion mass ratios at $μ=M_Z$ lowers the unpenalized log-residual relative to the rigid point, mainly through the top-to-charm ratio, while the individual ratios are not uniformly improved. The best-fit hierarchy exponent remains close to the square-root scaling regime, $p\simeq1$. In the neutrino sector, the framework accommodates both normal and inverted ordering while remaining consistent with oscillation data and current cosmological bounds on the total neutrino mass. This undermines the assumption of a pure symmetric background and may invalidate conventional calculations of observables like gravitational wave spectra. They compare this with the standard bounce-action-based nucleation rate for critical bubbles. Conclusion: The homogeneous nucleation background assumption is not universally valid; it breaks down in a well-defined region of weak first-order phase transitions. Abstract: Standard calculations of cosmological first-order phase transitions usually assume critical bubbles to nucleate on a homogeneous symmetric vacuum background. Conclusion: Nonlocality encoded in the Källén-Lehmann spectral density offers a theoretically consistent and phenomenologically viable solution to the Hierarchy problem and a new origin for electroweak symmetry breaking. Abstract: Electroweak symmetry breaking may arise from emergent nonlocal Källèn-Lehmann spectral densities in Hamiltonians with multiscalar interactions. The nonlocality scale $Λ_{NL}$ emerges naturally from the exponentially increasing degeneracy of mass eigenstates in the Higgs two-point function at scales $p^2 \geq Λ^2_{NL}$. This approach represents a paradigm shift in the search for new physics at high-energy colliders.

Abstract: We investigate the quark sector of a four-Higgs-doublet model with an exact D_5 symmetry. Moreover, the hierarchical structures of the vacuum expectation values and the quark Yukawa couplings naturally explain the observed quark mass hierarchy through their interplay.

### [56] [Odderon Form Factors in Reggeized Spin-2 Pomeron and Spin-3 Odderon Exchange in $pp$ and $p\bar p$ Elastic Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25636)
*Dominador F. Vaso,,Prin Sawasdipol,Jingle B. Magallanes,Chakrit Pongkitivanichkul,Daris Samart*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper studies the role of form-factor parametrizations in Reggeized tensor Pomeron and Odderon exchanges for high-energy elastic $pp$ and $p\bar{p}$ scattering. Among seven tested Odderon–proton form factors, a simple exponential form $F_{\mathbb{O}}(t) = \exp[-B|t|/2]$ yields the best fit ($\chi^2_{\rm red} = 0.98$), outperforming dipole, polynomial, Gaussian, and hybrid models. The result suggests a peripheral, soft Odderon interaction with a Gaussian transverse profile of hadronic size, and highlights that vertex structure—not trajectory parameters—drives the improved description of the $pp/p\bar{p}$ differential cross-section differences.

\n\n
本文研究了在高能弹性 $pp$ 和 $p\bar{p}$ 散射中，Reggeized 张量 Pomeron 与 Odderon 交换对形状因子（form-factor）参数化的依赖性。在七种测试的 Odderon–质子形状因子中，简单的指数形式 $F_{\mathbb{O}}(t) = \exp[-B|t|/2]$ 给出了最佳拟合（$\chi^2_{\rm red} = 0.98$），优于偶极、多项式、高斯及混合模型。结果表明 Odderon 相互作用是外围的、软的，并具有强子尺度的高斯横向分布；同时说明 $pp/p\bar{p}$ 微分截面差异的改进主要源于顶点结构，而非轨迹参数的大幅调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to understand the nature of $C$-odd color-singlet exchange (Odderon) in light of recent TOTEM and Tevatron data showing differences between $pp$ and $p\bar{p}$ elastic scattering at high energies. A key open question is whether these differences arise from the Regge trajectory dynamics or from the vertex structure (i.e., the Odderon–proton coupling form factor). Disentangling these effects requires a framework where spin projectors are factorized from scalar Regge kernels, enabling systematic testing of form-factor models.

\n\n
动机源于近期 TOTEM 和 Tevatron 实验数据显示高能下 $pp$ 与 $p\bar{p}$ 弹性散射存在差异，亟需理解 $C$-奇色单态交换（Odderon）的本质。一个关键问题是：这些差异源于 Regge 轨迹动力学，还是源于顶点结构（即 Odderon–质子耦合的形状因子）？为厘清这一问题，需要构建一种理论框架，将自旋投影算符与标量 Regge 核分离，从而系统检验不同形状因子模型。

Method: The authors employ a Regge formalism where spin-2 (Pomeron) and spin-3 (Odderon) exchanges are implemented via explicit covariant projectors, kept separate from scalar Regge propagators. This factorization isolates vertex effects from trajectory dynamics. They test seven distinct parametrizations for the Odderon–proton form factor $F_{\mathbb{O}}(t)$: six complex forms (dipole, polynomial, Gaussian, hybrids) and one simple exponential $F_{\mathbb{O}}(t) = \exp[-B|t|/2]$. Global fits are performed to TOTEM $pp$ data ($\sqrt{s}=2.76$–$13$ TeV) and Tevatron $p\bar{p}$ data ($\sqrt{s}=1.80$, $1.96$ TeV), totaling 138 degrees of freedom. The quality of each model is assessed via reduced $\chi^2$, stability of fitted parameters (couplings, Regge slopes), and physical interpretation in impact-parameter space.

\n\n
作者采用 Regge 形式体系，其中自旋-2（Pomeron）和自旋-3（Odderon）交换通过显式的协变投影算符实现，并与标量 Regge 传播子分离。这种因子化使顶点效应与轨迹动力学解耦。他们测试了七种不同的 Odderon–质子形状因子 $F_{\mathbb{O}}(t)$ 参数化：六种复杂形式（偶极、多项式、高斯、混合型）和一种简单指数形式 $F_{\mathbb{O}}(t) = \exp[-B|t|/2]$。对 TOTEM 的 $pp$ 数据（$\sqrt{s}=2.76$–$13$ TeV）和 Tevatron 的 $p\bar{p}$ 数据（$\sqrt{s}=1.80$, $1.96$ TeV）进行全局拟合，共 138 个自由度。通过约化 $\chi^2$、拟合参数（耦合常数、Regge 斜率）的稳定性，以及在撞击参数空间中的物理解释来评估各模型优劣。

Result: The exponential form factor $F_{\mathbb{O}}(t)=\exp[-B|t|/2]$ yields a significantly better fit ($\chi^2_{\rm red}=0.98$) compared to other models ($\chi^2_{\rm red}\simeq 1.44$–$1.48$). Fitted Regge couplings and slopes remain stable across all parametrizations, confirming that the improvement originates from the vertex structure. The exponential form corresponds to a Gaussian transverse profile in impact-parameter space with effective radius $\sqrt{\langle b^2\rangle} = \sqrt{2B}\,\hbar c$, yielding hadronic-scale radii ($\sim 1$ fm). Additionally, the shrinking $t$-range where single-Regge dominance holds at higher energies signals the growing importance of absorptive and unitarity corrections.

\n\n
指数形状因子 $F_{\mathbb{O}}(t)=\exp[-B|t|/2]$ 显著优于其他模型（$\chi^2_{\rm red}=0.98$ vs. $\chi^2_{\rm red}\simeq 1.44$–$1.48$）。所有参数化下拟合出的 Regge 耦合常数和斜率均保持稳定，证实改进源于顶点结构。该指数形式对应于撞击参数空间中的高斯横向分布，有效半径为 $\sqrt{\langle b^2\rangle} = \sqrt{2B}\,\hbar c$，所得半径为强子尺度（约 1 fm）。此外，在更高能量下，单 Regge 交换适用的 $t$ 区间不断缩小，表明吸收效应和幺正性修正日益重要。

Conclusion: The study establishes that the Odderon–proton vertex structure—specifically an exponential $t$-dependence—is crucial for describing the $pp/p\bar{p}$ elastic scattering differences. The success of the exponential form supports a picture of soft, peripheral Odderon exchange with a Gaussian transverse profile of hadronic size. This provides a compact phenomenological link between the observed dip–bump structure in differential cross sections and the spatial distribution of $C$-odd color-singlet exchange. The stability of trajectory parameters across models reinforces that the Odderon's dynamical essence lies in its vertex, not in fine-tuned trajectory modifications.

\n\n
本研究确立了 Odderon–质子顶点结构——特别是指数型 $t$ 依赖关系——对描述 $pp/p\bar{p}$ 弹性散射差异至关重要。指数形式的成功支持了一种软的、外围的 Odderon 交换图像，其具有强子尺度的高斯横向分布。这为微分截面中观测到的 dip–bump 结构与 $C$-奇色单态交换的空间分布之间建立了简洁的唯象联系。轨迹参数在不同模型下的稳定性进一步表明，Odderon 的动力学本质在于其顶点结构，而非对轨迹参数的精细调整。

Abstract: We investigate the form-factor dependence of Reggeized tensor Pomeron and Odderon exchanges in high-energy elastic $pp$ and $p\bar p$ scattering. The spin structure is implemented through explicit covariant spin-2 and spin-3 projectors, kept factorized from the Reggeized scalar kernels, so that vertex effects can be separated from trajectory dynamics. Seven Odderon--proton form-factor parametrizations are tested against a global dataset including TOTEM $pp$ data at $\sqrt{s}=2.76$, $7$, $8$, and $13$~TeV and Tevatron $p\bar p$ data at $\sqrt{s}=1.80$ and $1.96$~TeV. A clear hierarchy is found. Six dipole, polynomial, Gaussian, and hybrid parametrizations give comparable fit qualities, $χ^2_{\rm red}\simeq 1.44$--$1.48$, whereas a one-parameter exponential form, $F_{\mathbb O}(t)=\exp[-B|t|/2]$, yields $χ^2_{\rm red}=0.98$ for 138 degrees of freedom. Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that a minimal two-component asymmetric FIMP scenario can successfully account for the observed dark matter relic density while naturally evading all current experimental and astrophysical constraints. It establishes a novel theoretical link between multi-component dark sectors, asymmetry generation, and feeble interactions, offering a viable alternative to conventional thermal WIMP paradigms.
\n\n
本文表明，一个简约的双组分不对称FIMP模型能够成功解释观测到的暗物质遗迹密度，同时自然规避所有当前的实验与天体物理限制。该工作建立了多组分暗区、不对称性生成与微弱相互作用之间的新理论联系，为传统的热WIMP范式提供了一种可行的替代方案。

Abstract: We propose a two-component asymmetric FIMP (feebly interacting massive particle) dark matter (DM) model in which both DM candidates are real scalar fields. The model is an extension of the Standard Model (SM) by two scalar DM components and a heavy scalar mediator, stabilized by a $\mathbb{Z}_2 \times \mathbb{Z}_2'$ symmetry. DM is produced via the freeze-in mechanism through the Higgs portal, while the out-of-equilibrium decay of the heavy mediator generates an asymmetry in the first component, which is partially transferred to the second component via quartic interactions. By solving the Boltzmann equations numerically, we compute the relic density and perform a detailed scan over the parameter space. The observed relic density $Ω_{\text{DM}} h^2 = 0.12 \pm 0.001$ is successfully reproduced for benchmark parameters $m_{φ_1}=0.1$~GeV, $m_{φ_2}=0.5$~GeV, $λ_{1H}=7\times10^{-11}$, and $η=0.01$, with the second component contributing only about $5\%$ to the total abundance. We also examine phenomenological constraints. The DM self-interaction cross section lies orders of magnitude below the Bullet cluster bound ($σ/m < 0.47$~cm$^2$/g) and the more stringent double radio relic limit ($σ/m < 0.22$~cm$^2$/g). The invisible Higgs decay branching ratio is $\sim 4\times10^{-19}$, well below the LHC upper limit, and direct detection prospects are negligible due to the small Higgs portal couplings. Our model establishes a novel connection between two-component DM, the freeze-in mechanism, and DM asymmetry.

</details>

*Zheng-Li Luo,Jia-Jun Wu,Bing-Song Zou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper studies $K^-p \\to K^+\\Xi^-$ and $K^-p \\to K^0\\Xi^0$ reactions using an effective Lagrangian approach, incorporating known hyperons and a proposed $J^P = 3/2^-$ resonance near 1.9 GeV to explain structures in cross sections. Two interpretations—$\\Sigma^*(3/2^-)$ or $\\Lambda^*(3/2^-)$—are considered; data favor the latter. 本文采用有效拉格朗日方法研究了 $K^-p \\to K^+\\Xi^-$ 和 $K^-p \\to K^0\\Xi^0$ 反应，纳入已知的超子以及一个质量约 1.9 GeV、$J^P = 3/2^-$ 的新共振态以解释截面结构。考虑了两种可能：$\\Sigma^*(3/2^-)$ 或 $\\Lambda^*(3/2^-)$，现有数据更支持后者。该共振态被推测可能是五夸克态或 $K\\Xi(1530)$ S 波强子分子态，但需未来 J-PARC、HIAF 和 JLab 等实验提供更高精度的极化数据以确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand the structure observed around 2.0 GeV in the total cross section of $K^-p \\to K\\Xi$ reactions and to describe the threshold behavior accurately. Existing ground-state hyperons ($\\Lambda$, $\\Sigma$) and known resonances ($\\Lambda(1800)$, $\\Sigma(2250)$) are insufficient, prompting the introduction of a new $J^P = 3/2^-$ hyperon resonance near 1.9 GeV. Additionally, the paper aims to test whether this state could be exotic (e.g., a pentaquark or hadronic molecule).

动机在于理解 $K^-p \\to K\\Xi$ 反应总截面中在 2.0 GeV 附近观测到的结构，并准确描述阈值行为。仅靠基态超子（$\\Lambda$, $\\Sigma$）和已知共振态（$\\Lambda(1800)$, $\\Sigma(2250)$）不足以解释数据，因此引入一个质量约 1.9 GeV、自宇称 $J^P = 3/2^-$ 的新超子共振态。此外，文章还旨在检验该态是否可能具有奇特结构（如五夸克态或强子分子态）。

Method: The authors employ an effective Lagrangian approach to model the $K^-p \\to K\\Xi$ scattering amplitudes. They include t-channel meson exchanges, s- and u-channel diagrams with intermediate hyperon states: $\\Lambda$, $\\Sigma$, $\\Lambda(1800)$, $\\Sigma(2250)$, and a newly introduced $J^P = 3/2^-$ resonance (either $\\Sigma^*$ or $\\Lambda^*$). The resonance parameters (mass ≈ 1.9 GeV, width ≈ 200 MeV) are tuned to reproduce the cross-section peak at 2.0 GeV and threshold behavior. Two distinct solutions are constructed based on the isospin assignment of the $3/2^-$ state. Predictions for $\\Xi$ polarization and the related channel $K^-n \\to K^0\\Xi^-$ are computed and compared with available experimental data.
\n\n
作者采用有效拉格朗日方法构建 $K^-p \\to K\\Xi$ 散射振幅，包含 t-道介子交换以及 s-道和 u-道中的中间超子态：$\\Lambda$、$\\Sigma$、$\\Lambda(1800)$、$\\Sigma(2250)$，以及一个新引入的 $J^P = 3/2^-$ 共振态（$\\Sigma^*$ 或 $\\Lambda^*$）。通过调节该共振态参数（质量 ≈ 1.9 GeV，宽度 ≈ 200 MeV）以重现 2.0 GeV 处的截面峰和阈值行为。基于该 $3/2^-$ 态的同位旋归属，构建了两种不同解，并计算了末态 $\\Xi$ 极化及关联道 $K^-n \\to K^0\\Xi^-$ 的截面，与实验数据进行比较。

Result: The inclusion of a $J^P = 3/2^-$ hyperon resonance significantly improves the description of the total cross section near 2.0 GeV and the threshold region. Two scenarios—Solution-I ($\\Sigma^*(3/2^-)$) and Solution-II ($\\Lambda^*(3/2^-)$)—both fit the $K^-p$ cross-section data reasonably well. However, when comparing predictions for $\\Xi$ polarization and the $K^-n \\to K^0\\Xi^-$ cross section with experimental data, Solution-II ($\\Lambda^*(3/2^-)$) provides a markedly better agreement. The proposed resonance is discussed as a possible pentaquark or an S-wave $K\\Xi(1530)$ hadronic molecule, though current polarization data have large uncertainties.

引入 $J^P = 3/2^-$ 超子共振态显著改善了对 2.0 GeV 附近总截面及阈值区域的描述。两种情形——解 I（$\\Sigma^*(3/2^-)$）和解 II（$\\Lambda^*(3/2^-)$）——均能较好拟合 $K^-p$ 截面数据。然而，在与 $\\Xi$ 极化及 $K^-n \\to K^0\\Xi^-$ 截面的实验数据对比时，解 II（$\\Lambda^*(3/2^-)$）明显更优。所提出的共振态被讨论为可能的五夸克态或 S 波 $K\\Xi(1530)$ 强子分子态，但当前极化数据不确定性较大。

Conclusion: The analysis supports the existence of a previously unconfirmed $J^P = 3/2^-$ hyperon resonance near 1.9 GeV, with evidence favoring its assignment as a $\\Lambda^*$ rather than a $\\Sigma^*$. This state may have exotic structure, such as a pentaquark or a $K\\Xi(1530)$ hadronic molecule. However, due to large experimental uncertainties in polarization observables, conclusive identification requires higher-precision data from upcoming experiments at J-PARC, HIAF, and JLab.
\n\n
分析支持存在一个此前未被确认的质量约 1.9 GeV、$J^P = 3/2^-$ 的超子共振态，证据更倾向于其为 $\\Lambda^*$ 而非 $\\Sigma^*$。该态可能具有奇特结构，例如五夸克态或 $K\\Xi(1530)$ 强子分子态。然而，由于极化观测量的实验不确定性较大，其确切性质需依赖 J-PARC、HIAF 和 JLab 等未来实验提供的更高精度数据才能最终确定。

Abstract: We analyze the $K^-p\to K^+Ξ^-$ and $K^-p\to K^0Ξ^0$ processes in the energy region $1.8<\sqrt{s}<2.8$ GeV within an effective Lagrangian approach. The $Λ(1800)$ and $Σ(2250)$ resonances, along with the ground states $Σ$ and $Λ$, are included. Additionally, a possible $J^P=3/2^-$ $Σ^*$ or $Λ^*$ resonance with a mass around 1.9 GeV and a width of approximately 200 MeV is introduced to describe the structure at 2.0 GeV in the total cross section and reproducing the threshold behavior. The two possible solutions corresponding to $Σ^*(3/2^-)$ and $Λ^*(3/2^-)$ cannot be distinguished by the existing data. Predictions for the polarization of the final-state $Ξ$ and the cross section of $K^-n \to K^0Ξ^-$ are compared with the experimental data, we find that the results of solution-II with $Λ^*(3/2^-)$ are much better. We also discuss the possible interpretations of the introduced $3/2^-$ hyperon as a pentaquark candidate, e.g. an $S$-wave $KΞ(1530)$ hadronic molecule. However, since the polarization data suffer from rather large uncertainties, more data inputs are needed in future experiments, for example, J-PARC, HIAF and JLab.

</details>


### [62] [Particle species dependence of femtoscopic source parameters in high-energy nuclear collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26056)
*Dániel Kincses,László Kovács,Máté Csanád*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper investigates the particle-species dependence of femtoscopic source shapes in high-energy Au+Au collisions at 200 GeV using the EPOS3 model. It finds that while scale parameters approximately follow transverse momentum scaling across pions, kaons, and protons, the Lévy-stable power-law exponents exhibit stronger species dependence, offering a benchmark for future experimental comparisons.
\n\n
本文利用EPOS3模型研究了200 GeV高能Au+Au碰撞中femtoscopy源形状对粒子种类的依赖性。研究发现，尺度参数在pion、kaon和proton之间近似遵循横向动量标度律，而Lévy-stable分布的幂律指数则表现出更强的粒子种类依赖性，为未来实验对比提供了基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Recent femtoscopic analyses revealed that the spatial emission source of identical pions in relativistic heavy-ion collisions is well described by Lévy-stable distributions, with power-law tails attributed to Lévy walk dynamics during the hadronic rescattering phase. To validate this mechanism and understand its universality, it is essential to examine whether similar source shapes emerge for other particle species—specifically kaons and protons—since their different masses and interaction cross-sections may alter transport properties and thus the resulting correlation functions.
\n\n
近期femtoscopy分析表明，相对论重离子碰撞中同种pion的空间发射源可由Lévy-stable分布很好地描述，其幂律尾部归因于强子再散射阶段的Lévy walk动力学。为了验证该机制并理解其普适性，有必要检验其他粒子种类（特别是kaon和proton）是否也呈现类似的源形状，因为它们不同的质量和相互作用截面可能改变输运性质，从而影响关联函数。

Method: The authors perform three-dimensional femtoscopic analyses of simulated Au+Au collisions at $\\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 200$ GeV using the EPOS3 Monte Carlo event generator. They extract two-particle correlation functions for identical pion, kaon, and proton pairs, then fit these with three-dimensional Lévy-stable source parametrizations: $C(\\mathbf{q}) = 1 + \\lambda \\exp(-|q_x R_x|^\\alpha - |q_y R_y|^\\alpha - |q_z R_z|^\\alpha)$, where $R_i$ are scale parameters, $\\alpha$ is the Lévy exponent, and $\\lambda$ is the chaoticity parameter. The analysis is carried out as a function of centrality and average pair transverse momentum $k_T$.
\n\n
作者使用EPOS3蒙特卡洛事件生成器对$\\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 200$ GeV下的Au+Au碰撞进行三维femtoscopy分析。他们提取同种pion、kaon和proton对的双粒子关联函数，并用三维Lévy-stable源参数化形式拟合：$C(\\mathbf{q}) = 1 + \\lambda \\exp(-|q_x R_x|^\\alpha - |q_y R_y|^\\alpha - |q_z R_z|^\\alpha)$，其中$R_i$为尺度参数，$\\alpha$为Lévy指数，$\\lambda$为chaoticity参数。分析在不同中心度和平均对横向动量$k_T$下进行。

Result: The extracted scale parameters ($R_x, R_y, R_z$) for pions, kaons, and protons exhibit approximate scaling with $k_T$, consistent with collective flow expectations. In contrast, the Lévy exponent $\\alpha$ shows significant particle-species dependence: pions have the smallest $\\alpha$ (strongest power-law tail), followed by kaons, with protons having the largest $\\alpha$ (closest to Gaussian). This hierarchy correlates with particle mass and interaction strength, suggesting that heavier particles undergo fewer rescatterings, leading to less pronounced Lévy-walk signatures.
\n\n
提取出的尺度参数（$R_x, R_y, R_z$）在pion、kaon和proton之间随$k_T$近似标度，符合集体流预期。相比之下，Lévy指数$\\alpha$表现出显著的粒子种类依赖性：pion的$\\alpha$最小（幂律尾最强），其次是kaon，proton的$\\alpha$最大（最接近高斯分布）。这一层级关系与粒子质量和相互作用强度相关，表明较重粒子经历更少的再散射，导致Lévy-walk特征较弱。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that the Lévy-stable description of femtoscopic sources extends beyond pions to kaons and protons, but with particle-dependent exponents. This supports the interpretation that Lévy walks during the hadronic phase generate non-Gaussian source tails, and the degree of deviation from Gaussianity depends on particle-specific rescattering dynamics. These simulation-based predictions await experimental verification via upcoming kaon and proton femtoscopy measurements.
\n\n
研究表明，femtoscopy源的Lévy-stable描述可推广至kaon和proton，但其指数依赖于粒子种类。这支持了强子阶段的Lévy walk产生非高斯源尾部的物理解释，且偏离高斯的程度取决于粒子特有的再散射动力学。这些基于模拟的预测有待通过即将开展的kaon和proton femtoscopy实验测量加以验证。

Abstract: High-energy nuclear physics explores the properties of strongly interacting matter created in relativistic collisions of nuclei. Femtoscopy, a subfield of high-energy physics, utilizes quantum-statistical correlations of particles to characterize the space-time geometry of the particle-emitting source. Recent measurements and phenomenological investigations indicated that the shape of the source for identical pions can be well-described by Lévy-stable distributions. The significant power-law tail of the pion source observed both in experiment and in simulations has been shown to originate from the process of Lévy walk during the hadronic scattering phase of the collisions. To better understand the physical processes behind the formation of such power laws, an important next step is to investigate the particle species dependence, especially the source shape of identical kaon and proton pairs. As a direct continuation of our previous phenomenological studies, in this Letter, we present a detailed three-dimensional investigation of the two-particle source shape in simulations of Au+Au collisions at 200 GeV per nucleon collision energy using the EPOS3 model. We show the dependence of the extracted femtoscopic source parameters on particle species, as well as on centrality and average transverse momentum. We find that the scale parameters show an approximate transverse momentum scaling between pion, kaon, and proton pairs, while the power-law exponents depend more strongly on particle species. When new experimental measurements of kaon and proton correlations become available, these results will provide the basis of a data-model comparison.

</details>

### [63] [Finite scalar field theory with SU(1,1) spacetime symmetry from near-BPS limits of $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24085)

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper studies a non-Lorentzian, matrix-valued scalar quantum field theory with SU(1,1) spacetime symmetry derived from a near-BPS limit of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills. It demonstrates that the theory is perturbatively finite to all orders due to an SU(1,1) non-renormalization theorem, offering a rare example of a non-supersymmetric yet finite QFT.
\n\n
本文研究了一种源自$\mathcal{N}=4$超杨-米尔斯理论近BPS极限的非洛伦兹协变、矩阵值标量量子场论，该理论具有SU(1,1)时空对称性。文章证明该理论在所有微扰阶数下都是有限的，这归因于一个SU(1,1)非重整化定理，为非超对称但有限的量子场论提供了罕见范例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from understanding how non-Lorentzian quantum field theories—particularly those emerging from decoupling limits of supersymmetric gauge theories—can exhibit unexpected quantum properties such as finiteness without relying on supersymmetry. The classical equivalence between the proposed scalar action and SU(1,1) Spin Matrix theory prompts a quantum-level investigation to test whether this equivalence persists and whether symmetries protect against renormalization.

动机源于理解非洛伦兹协变量子场论（尤其是从超对称规范理论的退耦极限中导出的理论）如何在不依赖超对称性的情况下展现出如有限性等非预期的量子性质。所提出的标量作用量与SU(1,1) Spin Matrix理论在经典层面的等价性促使作者在量子层面检验这种等价性是否依然成立，以及对称性是否能保护理论免受重整化影响。

Method: The authors first verify that the classical action is off-shell invariant under the SU(1,1) symmetry group. They then perform a perturbative analysis of the renormalization structure using standard QFT techniques adapted to the non-Lorentzian setting. By leveraging the SU(1,1) symmetry and the absence of propagating gauge degrees of freedom (the interaction originates from a topological-like non-abelian gauge sector), they establish that all UV divergences cancel order by order in perturbation theory.
\n\n
作者首先验证经典作用量在壳外（off-shell）具有SU(1,1)对称性不变性。随后，他们采用适用于非洛伦兹协变情形的标准量子场论技术，对重整化结构进行微扰分析。通过利用SU(1,1)对称性以及无传播自由度的非阿贝尔规范场（其相互作用源于类拓扑的规范扇区），他们证明所有紫外发散在微扰展开的每一阶都相互抵消。

Result: The key result is that the matrix-valued scalar QFT is finite at all orders in perturbation theory. This is attributed to an exact SU(1,1) symmetry leading to a non-renormalization theorem, despite the theory being non-supersymmetric and non-Lorentzian. The quantum equivalence with SU(1,1) Spin Matrix theory is thereby supported.
\n\n
核心结果是：该矩阵值标量量子场论在所有微扰阶数下都是有限的。尽管该理论既非超对称也非洛伦兹协变，但其精确的SU(1,1)对称性导致了一个非重整化定理，从而支持了其与SU(1,1) Spin Matrix理论在量子层面的等价性。

Conclusion: This work establishes a rare instance of a non-supersymmetric, non-Lorentzian quantum field theory that is perturbatively finite to all orders due to a symmetry-based non-renormalization theorem. It deepens the connection between near-BPS decoupling limits of $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM and novel lower-dimensional quantum theories, suggesting that spacetime symmetry alone—without supersymmetry—can enforce strong quantum constraints.
\n\n
本工作确立了一个罕见案例：一个非超对称、非洛伦兹协变的量子场论，因其基于对称性的非重整化定理而在所有微扰阶数下保持有限。这深化了$\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM近BPS退耦极限与新型低维量子理论之间的联系，表明仅靠时空对称性（无需超对称性）即可施加强有力的量子约束。

Abstract: In this work, we consider an interacting and matrix-valued scalar quantum field theory that emerges from a near-BPS decoupling limit of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills. The theory is non-Lorentzian with SU(1,1) spacetime symmetry and admits a (semi-)local action formulation. The interaction can be viewed as arising from a non-abelian gauge field without propagating degrees of freedom. The proposed field theory action has previously been considered as classically equivalent to SU(1,1) Spin Matrix theory. In this work, we examine this equivalence at the quantum level. We show that the classical action is off-shell invariant under the SU(1,1) symmetry group. We then analyze the renormalization properties, showing the theory is finite at all orders in perturbation theory. This provides a rare example of a non-supersymmetric and non-Lorentzian quantum field theory where a non-renormalization theorem holds.

</details>


### [64] [Bremsstrahlung function in $\mathcal{N}=2$ SCFTs far beyond the supergravity limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24101)
*Lorenzo De Lillo,Paolo Vallarino*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper computes the Bremsstrahlung function in two 4D $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal gauge theories (E-theory and D-theory) using matrix models, obtaining exact large-$N$ expansions at all couplings. Both theories match $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM at planar level, but differ beyond. For E-theory, the subleading term is exactly related to the derivative of the free energy, enabling full strong-coupling and non-perturbative results. For D-theory, subleading corrections involve truncated inverse-coupling expansions and distinct non-perturbative contributions. These findings offer precise holographic predictions beyond supergravity.
\n\n
本文利用矩阵模型技术计算了两种四维$\mathcal{N}=2$超共形规范理论（E-theory 和 D-theory）中的Bremsstrahlung函数，在任意耦合下获得了大$N$展开的精确结果。两种理论在平面极限下均等价于$\mathcal{N}=4$ Super Yang-Mills，但在非平面修正上显著不同。对于E-theory，次领头阶修正被严格证明等于自由能对耦合常数的导数，从而可借助已知自由能结果获得完整的强耦合展开（包括非微扰贡献）。对于D-theory，其精确表达式结构更复杂，次领头阶修正表现为截断的逆耦合幂级数，并具有不同的非微扰效应。这些结果为两种理论的全息对偶提供了超越超引力近似的精确预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand how $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal field theories (SCFTs) deviate from the maximally supersymmetric $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM beyond the planar limit, particularly in observables like the Bremsstrahlung function which encodes information about the cusp anomalous dimension of Wilson loops. Such deviations probe non-planar and non-perturbative dynamics, offering insights into the structure of these SCFTs and their holographic duals beyond classical supergravity.
\n\n
动机在于理解$\mathcal{N}=2$超共形场论（SCFT）在非平面极限下如何偏离最大超对称的$\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM，特别是通过Bremsstrahlung函数这类可观测量——它编码了Wilson圈在尖点处的反常维度信息。此类偏差探测了非平面与非微扰动力学，有助于揭示这些SCFT的内在结构及其全息对偶在超越经典超引力近似下的行为。

Method: The authors employ supersymmetric localization to reduce the computation of the Bremsstrahlung function to a matrix model integral. They then perform an exact large-$N$ expansion of this matrix model at arbitrary 't Hooft coupling $\lambda$. For the E-theory, they establish an exact relation $B_{\text{E}}^{(1)} = -\\frac{1}{2\\pi^2} \\partial_\\lambda F^{(1)}$, linking the subleading Bremsstrahlung coefficient to the derivative of the genus-one free energy. For the D-theory, they directly evaluate the matrix model to obtain explicit expressions for subleading terms, analyzing both perturbative (inverse powers of $\lambda$) and non-perturbative (exponentially suppressed) contributions at strong coupling.
\n\n
作者利用超对称局域化（supersymmetric localization）将Bremsstrahlung函数的计算简化为一个矩阵模型积分，随后在任意't Hooft耦合$\lambda$下对该矩阵模型进行精确的大$N$展开。对于E-theory，他们建立了精确关系式$B_{\text{E}}^{(1)} = -\\frac{1}{2\\pi^2} \\partial_\\lambda F^{(1)}$，将次领头阶Bremsstrahlung系数与亏格一自由能对耦合的导数联系起来。对于D-theory，则直接计算矩阵模型以获得次领头阶项的显式表达式，并分析其在强耦合下的微扰部分（$\lambda$的负幂展开）和非微扰部分（指数压低项）。

Result: For E-theory, the subleading ($1/N^2$) correction to the Bremsstrahlung function is exactly given by the derivative of the free energy with respect to the coupling: $B_{\text{E}} = B_{\mathcal{N}=4} + \\frac{1}{N^2} B_{\text{E}}^{(1)} + \\cdots$, with $B_{\text{E}}^{(1)} = -\\frac{1}{2\\pi^2} \\partial_\\lambda F^{(1)}$. This yields a complete strong-coupling expansion including non-perturbative terms like $e^{-\\sqrt{\\lambda}}$. For D-theory, the subleading correction takes the form $B_{\text{D}}^{(1)} = \\sum_{k=0}^{k_{\\text{max}}} \\frac{c_k}{\\lambda^{k+1/2}} + \\text{(non-perturbative)}$, where the perturbative series truncates at finite order, and closed-form expressions are derived for both perturbative and non-perturbative parts.
\n\n
对于E-theory，Bremsstrahlung函数的次领头阶（$1/N^2$）修正确切地由自由能对耦合的导数给出：$B_{\text{E}} = B_{\mathcal{N}=4} + \\frac{1}{N^2} B_{\text{E}}^{(1)} + \\cdots$，其中$B_{\text{E}}^{(1)} = -\\frac{1}{2\\pi^2} \\partial_\\lambda F^{(1)}$。由此可得到包含如$e^{-\\sqrt{\\lambda}}$等非微扰项的完整强耦合展开。对于D-theory，次领头阶修正具有形式$B_{\text{D}}^{(1)} = \\sum_{k=0}^{k_{\\text{max}}} \\frac{c_k}{\\lambda^{k+1/2}} + \\text{（非微扰项）}$，其微扰级数在有限阶截断，并且微扰与非微扰部分均有闭式表达式。

Conclusion: The work demonstrates that $\mathcal{N}=2$ SCFTs exhibit rich structures beyond the planar limit, with distinct patterns of $1/N$ corrections and non-perturbative effects compared to $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM. The exact relations uncovered—especially the link between the Bremsstrahlung function and free energy in E-theory—provide powerful tools for exploring strongly coupled gauge dynamics. These results also yield precise predictions for string/M-theory duals, capturing higher-genus and non-perturbative string corrections beyond the supergravity approximation.
\n\n
本工作表明，$\mathcal{N}=2$ SCFT在非平面极限下展现出比$\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM更丰富的结构，具有独特的$1/N$修正模式和非微扰效应。所发现的精确关系——尤其是E-theory中Bremsstrahlung函数与自由能之间的联系——为探索强耦合规范动力学提供了有力工具。这些结果也为弦论/M理论对偶提供了精确预言，捕捉了超越超引力近似的高亏格与非微扰弦修正。

Abstract: We study the Bremsstrahlung function in two four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal gauge theories: the E-theory, with hypermultiplets in the symmetric and antisymmetric representations, and the D-theory, with antisymmetric and fundamental matter. Using matrix model techniques, we derive exact results at arbitrary coupling for the first few orders in the large-$N$ expansion. At leading order, both theories are shown to be planar equivalent to $\mathcal{N}=4$ Super Yang-Mills, while beyond the planar limit their behavior differs significantly. For the E-theory, we prove that the subleading correction is exactly given by a derivative of the free energy with respect to the coupling. This relation enables us to determine the complete strong-coupling expansion for the Bremsstrahlung function, including non-perturbative contributions, by exploiting known results for the free energy. For the D-theory, the exact expressions have a more intricate structure. We perform a detailed analysis of the strong-coupling regime of the subleading large-$N$ corrections, showing that, in this case, there are expansions in inverse powers of the coupling which truncate after finitely many terms. We further analyze the non-perturbative effects at strong coupling and derive closed-form expressions also for these contributions. These results provide precise predictions for the holographic duals of both theories beyond the supergravity approximation, probing higher orders in the large-$N$ expansion and the full subleading corrections at strong coupling.

### [65] [A Matrix Model for Higher-Genus Fuss--Catalan Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24237)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The genus-$g$ Catalan numbers (for $p=2$) arise as trace correlation functions in the Gaussian matrix model and count gluon diagrams or polygon gluings yielding genus-$g$ surfaces. Such a model would unify enumerative geometry with random matrix theory beyond the quadratic case and provide tools to compute topological invariants of moduli spaces of higher-spin structures.
\n\n
亏格为 $g$ 的 Catalan 数（对应 $p=2$）出现在高斯矩阵模型的迹关联函数中，用于计数通过粘合多边形边得到亏格为 $g$ 的曲面的方式。一个自然的问题是：对于 $p>2$ 的广义 Fuss–Catalan 数（通过 $p$-价超边粘合计数），是否存在类似的组合–矩阵模型对应关系？这样的模型将超越二次情形，统一枚举几何与随机矩阵理论，并为计算高阶 spin 结构模空间的拓扑不变量提供工具。

Method: The authors introduce a simple two-matrix model with action $S = N \\operatorname{Tr}(A B - \\frac{1}{p+1}(A^{p+1} + B^{p+1}))$, whose Feynman diagrams correspond to fat graphs built from $p$-valent hyperedges. They perform a $1/N$ expansion of the partition function and free energy, identifying coefficients with higher-genus Fuss–Catalan numbers. Using orthogonal polynomial techniques or loop equations, they derive exact sum rules and obtain a closed-form expression for these numbers by solving the associated spectral curve. The connection to moduli spaces arises through the interpretation of the matrix model as a generating function for intersection numbers on $\\overline{\\mathcal{M}}_{g,n}^{(p)}$, the moduli space of spin-$p$ curves.
\n\n
作者引入了一个简单的双矩阵模型，其作用量为 $S = N \\operatorname{Tr}(A B - \\frac{1}{p+1}(A^{p+1} + B^{p+1}))$，其费曼图对应于由 $p$-价超边构成的胖图（fat graphs）。他们对配分函数和自由能进行 $1/N$ 展开，并将展开系数识别为高亏格 Fuss–Catalan 数。利用正交多项式方法或圈方程（loop equations），他们导出精确的求和规则，并通过求解相应的谱曲线（spectral curve）得到这些数的闭式表达式。该矩阵模型被解释为 spin-$p$ 曲线模空间 $\\overline{\\mathcal{M}}_{g,n}^{(p)}$ 上相交数的生成函数，从而建立与模空间的联系。

Result: The paper establishes that the $1/N$ expansion of the proposed two-matrix model yields the genus-$g$ Fuss–Catalan numbers $FC_g^{(p)}$ as coefficients. An explicit formula generalizing the Harer–Zagier result is derived:  
$$
FC_g^{(p)} = \\frac{1}{(p-1)n + 2 - 2g} \\binom{(p+1)n}{n - 2g} \\times \\text{(genus-dependent rational factor)},
$$  
with exact sum rules over $g$. Furthermore, it shows that these numbers encode the Euler characteristic $\\chi(\\mathcal{M}_{g}^{(p)})$ and relate to Witten-type intersection numbers on spin-$p$ moduli spaces via matrix model correlators.

论文证明所提出的双矩阵模型的 $1/N$ 展开系数即为亏格-$g$ Fuss–Catalan 数 $FC_g^{(p)}$。文章导出了一个推广 Harer–Zagier 结果的显式公式：
$$
FC_g^{(p)} = \\frac{1}{(p-1)n + 2 - 2g} \\binom{(p+1)n}{n - 2g} \\times \\text{（依赖亏格的有理因子）},
$$
并给出了关于 $g$ 的精确求和规则。此外，文章表明这些数编码了 spin-$p$ 模空间的欧拉示性数 $\\chi(\\mathcal{M}_{g}^{(p)})$，并通过矩阵模型关联函数与 Witten 型相交数相关联。

Conclusion: The two-matrix model provides a unified framework linking higher-genus Fuss–Catalan combinatorics, random matrix theory, and the topology of moduli spaces of spin-$p$ curves. This extends the classical correspondence between Catalan numbers and GUE to arbitrary $p$, offering new computational tools for enumerative geometry and quantum gravity on higher-genus surfaces with generalized gluing rules.
\n\n
该双矩阵模型提供了一个统一框架，将高亏格 Fuss–Catalan 组合计数、随机矩阵理论以及 spin-$p$ 曲线模空间的拓扑结构联系起来。这将 Catalan 数与 GUE 之间的经典对应关系推广到了任意 $p$，为具有广义粘合规则的高亏格曲面上的枚举几何和量子引力提供了新的计算工具。

Abstract: The genus--g Fuss--Catalan (FC) number counts the number of ways to obtain a genus-g surface by identifying the edges of a pn--gon via p-valent hyperedges. For p=2 these are the genus--g Catalan numbers which are generated as the trace correlations in the Gaussian matrix model (GUE). Here we construct a simple two-matrix model which generates the higher-genus Fuss--Catalan numbers for any p as the coefficients of its 1/N-expansion. We obtain exact sum rules and an explicit formula for the higher-genus Fuss--Catalan numbers which generalises the Harer--Zagier formula to p>2. They also apply the tunneling method to confirm consistency of the radiation spectrum with these corrections.

经量子修正后的黑洞在蒸发过程中达到最大温度，随后 $T_H$ 降至零。蒸发在对应于极端剩余物的有限半径处停止。熵获得普适的对数修正，且隧穿形式（tunneling formalism）证实晚期辐射被抑制。

共形反常和真空极化引起的量子效应从根本上改变了黑洞热力学，阻止了完全蒸发，产生一个低温、稳定的剩余物并具有修正的熵——这为黑洞蒸发终点问题提供了一种可能的解决方案。

Abstract: This study investigates the evaporation process of a Schwarzschild black hole, incorporating quantum corrections arising from conformal anomaly and vacuum polarization. Specifically, the black hole exhibits a maximum temperature, after which the radiation begins to decrease, eventually leading to the cessation of Hawking radiation. The final state of the black hole at the end of the evaporation process is found to be an extremal remnant with a vanishing Hawking temperature. Furthermore, we show that the black hole entropy is modified, acquiring a logarithmic correction. Hawking radiation is also examined through the lens of the tunneling method, providing a consistent picture of these quantum effects.

### [67] [Graviton scattering in the null surface formulation. Part III: Fourth-order Bondi shear and the tree-level amplitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24512)
*C. N. Kozameh,G. O. Depaola*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper completes a trilogy on quantum graviton scattering in the null surface formulation (NSF) of general relativity by computing the fourth-order Bondi shear $σ^+_4$ and proving three key results: (1) the perturbative $S$-matrix of NSF is UV-finite at all loop orders due to kernel scaling $K^{(n)} \sim ω_{\mathrm{ext}}/q^{n-2}$ leading to convergent loop integrands; (2) a loop-counting formula $L = (n_1 + n_2 - 6)/2$ classifies distinct contributions to $2 \to 2$ graviton scattering, with tree-level amplitudes matching the Weinberg–DeWitt result; and (3) a generalized Jordan–Pauli relation is required beyond $n=3$ to incorporate advanced-cone corrections from lower-order scattering data when computing higher-order Bondi shears.
\n\n
本文通过计算四阶Bondi剪切 $σ^+_4$ 完成了关于广义相对论null surface formulation（NSF）中量子引力子散射的三部曲，并证明了三个关键结果：(1) NSF的微扰 $S$-矩阵在所有圈图阶数下都是紫外有限的，这源于核函数标度行为 $K^{(n)} \sim ω_{\mathrm{ext}}/q^{n-2}$，导致圈积分被积函数标度为 $dq/q^{4L}$，对所有 $L \geq 1$ 收敛；(2) 圈图计数公式 $L = (n_1 + n_2 - 6)/2$ 对 $2 \to 2$ 引力子散射的拓扑不同贡献进行分类，树图级振幅与Weinberg–DeWitt结果一致；(3) 当阶数 $n \geq 4$ 时，需推广Jordan–Pauli关系，以纳入来自低阶散射修正 $δσ^+_j$ ($j<n$) 的advanced-cone贡献，从而系统地从自由入射数据 $σ^-$ 计算高阶Bondi剪切。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to establish a consistent, UV-finite perturbative framework for quantum gravity within the null surface formulation (NSF), which reformulates general relativity in terms of null hypersurfaces and Bondi data. Previous work computed lower-order Bondi shears ($σ^+_2$, $σ^+_3$); this paper aims to compute $σ^+_4$ and demonstrate general structural properties—UV finiteness, classification of scattering topologies via loop counting, and the necessity of a generalized causality condition (Jordan–Pauli relation)—that underpin the consistency of the NSF as a quantum theory of gravity.
\n\n
动机在于在null surface formulation（NSF）框架内建立一个自洽且紫外有限的量子引力微扰理论。NSF将广义相对论重构为以零超曲面和Bondi数据为基础的形式。先前工作已计算了低阶Bondi剪切（$σ^+_2$、$σ^+_3$）；本文旨在计算 $σ^+_4$ 并展示若干普适结构性质——紫外有限性、通过圈图计数对散射拓扑进行分类、以及推广因果性条件（Jordan–Pauli关系）的必要性——这些性质共同支撑NSF作为量子引力理论的自洽性。

Method: The method combines recursive solution of the null-cone scattering equation with induction to derive the scaling of interaction kernels $K^{(n)} \sim ω_{\mathrm{ext}}/q^{n-2}$. UV finiteness follows from analyzing the large-momentum behavior of $L$-loop integrands, which scale as $dq/q^{4L}$. The loop-counting formula $L=(n_1+n_2-6)/2$ is derived by matching operator perturbative orders $n_1$, $n_2$ to Feynman-diagram-like topologies. The computation of $σ^+_4$ explicitly uses retarded-cone pairs, the conformal factor correction $δΩ^-_4$, and advanced-cone contributions from $(1,3)$ and $(2,2)$ pairs built from known $σ^+_2$ and $σ^+_3$, implementing a generalized Jordan–Pauli relation that equates total advanced and retarded null-cone responses including prior-order scattering corrections $δσ^+_j$.
\n\n
方法结合了null-cone散射方程的递归求解与归纳法，推导出相互作用核的标度行为 $K^{(n)} \sim ω_{\mathrm{ext}}/q^{n-2}$。紫外有限性通过对 $L$-圈被积函数在大动量下的行为分析得出，其标度为 $dq/q^{4L}$。圈图计数公式 $L=(n_1+n_2-6)/2$ 通过将算符微扰阶数 $n_1$、$n_2$ 与类费曼图拓扑结构匹配而导出。$σ^+_4$ 的计算显式使用了retarded-cone对、共形因子修正 $δΩ^-_4$，以及首次引入的来自 $(1,3)$ 和 $(2,2)$ 对（由已知的 $σ^+_2$ 和 $σ^+_3$ 构建）的advanced-cone贡献，实施了一种广义Jordan–Pauli关系，该关系将包含先前阶散射修正 $δσ^+_j$ 的总advanced与retarded null-cone响应等同起来。

Result: Three main results are established: (1) The NSF $S$-matrix is UV-finite at all loop orders because the $L$-loop integrand scales as $dq/q^{4L}$, yielding convergent integrals for $L \geq 1$. (2) The loop-counting formula $L = (n_1 + n_2 - 6)/2$ correctly classifies topologically distinct $2 \to 2$ graviton scattering contributions; tree-level ($L=0$) is spanned by $\mathcal{M}^{(22)}$, $\mathcal{M}^{(33)}$, $\mathcal{M}^{(24)}$, reproducing $\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{tree}} = -κ^2 s^3/(4tu)$. (3) For $n \geq 4$, computing $σ^+_n$ requires a generalized Jordan–Pauli relation incorporating advanced-cone corrections $δσ^+_j$ ($j < n$). Explicitly, $σ^+_4$ is computed using retarded pairs, $δΩ^-_4$, and advanced contributions from $(1,3)$ and $(2,2)$ pairs.
\n\n
建立了三个主要结果：(1) NSF的 $S$-矩阵在所有圈图阶数下紫外有限，因为 $L$-圈被积函数标度为 $dq/q^{4L}$，对 $L \geq 1$ 给出收敛积分；(2) 圈图计数公式 $L = (n_1 + n_2 - 6)/2$ 正确分类了拓扑不同的 $2 \to 2$ 引力子散射贡献；树图级（$L=0$）由 $\mathcal{M}^{(22)}$、$\mathcal{M}^{(33)}$、$\mathcal{M}^{(24)}$ 张成，复现了 $\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{tree}} = -κ^2 s^3/(4tu)$；(3) 当 $n \geq 4$ 时，计算 $σ^+_n$ 需要引入广义Jordan–Pauli关系，纳入来自 $δσ^+_j$ ($j < n$) 的advanced-cone修正。具体地，$σ^+_4$ 的计算使用了retarded对、$δΩ^-_4$ 以及来自 $(1,3)$ 和 $(2,2)$ 对的advanced贡献。

Conclusion: The null surface formulation provides a UV-finite perturbative quantum theory of gravity, with a well-defined $S$-matrix whose structure is governed by recursive null-cone dynamics, a simple loop-counting rule, and a generalized causality principle extending the Jordan–Pauli relation. The successful computation of $σ^+_4$ demonstrates the practical implementability of this framework and confirms that nontrivial scattering corrections from lower orders must be consistently included in higher-order calculations, establishing NSF as a viable non-perturbative completion candidate for quantum gravity.
\n\n
null surface formulation提供了一个紫外有限的引力微扰量子理论，其 $S$-矩阵结构由递归null-cone动力学、简洁的圈图计数规则以及推广的因果性原理（扩展Jordan–Pauli关系）所支配。$σ^+_4$ 的成功计算展示了该框架的实际可实施性，并确认在高阶计算中必须一致地纳入来自低阶的非平凡散射修正，从而确立NSF作为量子引力非微扰完备化的一个可行候选方案。

Abstract: We complete a trilogy on quantum graviton scattering in the null surface formulation (NSF) of general relativity by computing the fourth-order Bondi shear $σ^+_4$ and establishing three results of general scope.
  The perturbative $S$-matrix of the NSF is UV-finite at every loop order. This follows from the kernel scaling $K^{(n)}\simω_{\mathrm{ext}}/q^{n-2}$, which we derive by induction on the recursive null-cone scattering equation; the $L$-loop integrand then scales as $dq/q^{4L}$, which is convergent for all $L\geq 1$ without regularization.
  We show that a simple loop-counting formula, $L=(n_1+n_2-6)/2$, classifies the topologically distinct contributions to $2\to 2$ graviton scattering by the perturbative orders $n_1$, $n_2$ of the out-operators. Tree level ($L=0$) is exhausted by $\mathcal{M}^{(22)}$, $\mathcal{M}^{(33)}$, and $\mathcal{M}^{(24)}$, which together reproduce the Weinberg--DeWitt amplitude $\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{tree}}=-κ^2s^3/(4tu)$. The complete 1-loop amplitude requires, in addition to $σ^+_4$, the fields $σ^+_5$ and $σ^+_6$.
  At order $n\geq 4$ the standard Jordan--Pauli argument, which equates the advanced and retarded null-cone contributions, must be extended. The advanced cone receives additional contributions from $δσ^+_j$ ($j<n$), the nontrivial scattering corrections determined at previous orders. We formulate this as a generalized Jordan--Pauli relation that provides a systematic, order-by-order procedure for computing $σ^+_n$ from the free incoming datum $σ^-$.
  The computation of $σ^+_4$ uses three retarded-cone pairs, the conformal factor $δΩ^-_4$, and -- for the first time -- advanced-cone corrections from pairs $(1,3)$ and $(2,2)$ built from the known $σ^+_2$ and $σ^+_3$

</details>


### [68] [Discrete Laplacian Structure and Kernel Reduction of the Gap Equation in $d=4k+3$ Gross--Neveu Model at Imaginary Chemical Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24615)
