  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the observation of unexpected regularities in the gap equations of the large-$N$ Gross–Neveu model at imaginary chemical potential in specific odd dimensions. The authors aim to uncover the underlying mathematical origin of these regularities and explore potential connections between thermal quantum field theories in odd $d$ and integrable quantum mechanical systems in one dimension.
\n\n
动机源于在特定奇数维度下，大-$N$ Gross–Neveu 模型在虚化学势处的能隙方程中观察到的意外规律性。作者旨在揭示这些规律性的深层数学起源，并探索奇数维热量子场论与一维可积量子力学系统之间的潜在联系。

Method: The authors analyze the gap equations of the large-$N$ Gross–Neveu model in $d = 4k+3$ dimensions at imaginary chemical potential. They decompose the equations into two components: (1) a universal part expressible via higher-order discrete Laplacian operators on lattice-like structures, and (2) a cutoff-dependent contribution derived from the truncated asymptotic expansion of a hypergeometric function $_pF_q$. They further relate the fixed points of the thermal mass—where internal energy balances entropic contributions—to solutions of transcendental equations analogous to those arising in OPE inversion formula techniques.
\n\n
作者分析了在 $d = 4k+3$ 维度、虚化学势下的大-$N$ Gross–Neveu 模型的能隙方程。他们将方程分解为两个部分：(1) 一个普适部分，可通过格点结构上的高阶离散 Laplacian 算子表达；(2) 一个截断依赖项，来自超几何函数 $_pF_q$ 的截断渐近展开。此外，他们将热质量的固定点（内能与热模熵贡献平衡之处）与 OPE inversion formula 技术中出现的超越方程的解联系起来。

Result: The gap equations are shown to split cleanly into a discrete-Laplacian-driven universal sector and a cutoff-sensitive asymptotic sector. At special values of imaginary chemical potential, the thermal mass satisfies transcendental equations determined by the balance of energy and entropy, yielding quantized or algebraically constrained mass values. This provides evidence for a hidden correspondence between thermal field theories in odd $d = 4k+3$ and exactly solvable 1D quantum models.
\n\n
能隙方程被清晰地分解为由离散 Laplacian 主导的普适部分和对截断敏感的渐近部分。在特定虚化学势值处，热质量满足由能量与熵平衡决定的超越方程，从而给出量子化或代数约束的质量值。这为奇数维 $d = 4k+3$ 热场论与可精确求解的一维量子模型之间存在隐含对应关系提供了证据。

Conclusion: The observed structure suggests a profound link between the analytic properties of thermal correlators in odd-dimensional large-$N$ field theories and spectral data of integrable quantum mechanical systems. This connection may offer new non-perturbative tools—inspired by OPE inversion and discrete spectral geometry—for solving strongly coupled thermal systems.
\n\n
所观察到的结构表明，奇数维大-$N$ 场论中热关联函数的解析性质与可积量子力学系统的谱数据之间存在深刻联系。这一联系可能启发基于 OPE inversion 和离散谱几何的新非微扰方法，用于求解强耦合热系统。

Abstract: We observe a remarkable mathematical structure in the gap equations of the large-$N$ Gross--Neveu model at imaginary chemical potential in odd spacetime dimensions $d = 4k+3$. We show they can be written as the sum of two parts: one defined by higher-order discrete Laplacian patterns and a cut-off dependent part given by truncated asymptotic expansion of a hypergeometric function. We argue that this picture corresponds to a deeper relationship between thermal field theories in these odd $d$ and exactly-solvable one-dimensional quantum problems. We find that the thermal mass at specific imaginary chemical potential values is fixed where internal energy balances entropic states of thermal modes which is physically equivalent with OPE inversion formula techniques where thermal mass values arise from transcendental sets of equations.

Conclusion: The study suggests that the scope of geometries relevant to fundamental physics extends beyond classical algebraic settings, yet remains tightly constrained by dualities like mirror symmetry. 研究表明，与基础物理相关的几何范围超出了经典代数范畴，但仍受到镜像对称等对偶性的严格约束。这指向在量子引力与弦论背景下，复几何与辛几何之间可能存在更深层次的融合。

Abstract: Active feedback between geometry and physics is woven throughout the study of Nature at its fundamental level, and is of key importance in string theory. Methods of complex algebraic geometry in particular have brought about an unrivaled abundance of solutions, counted well into hundreds of orders of magnitude, reciprocated by the discovery of the wholly unexpected mirror symmetry. However, recent developments demonstrate that there are rich additional possibilities, made possible by certain generalizations that, at first glance, appear to be non-algebraic. Nevertheless, they are remarkably well-aligned within an overall mirror-symmetric framework, are amenable to almost as comprehensive quantitative analysis, and hint at a deeper relationship with symplectic geometry.

### [70] [Chirality loss during brane merging: a universal power law from the Jackiw-Rebbi index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24739)
*H. P. Pinheiro,C. A. S. Almeida*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper studies the loss of chiral fermion localization during the merging of two domain walls in extra-dimensional braneworld models, using the (1+1)-dimensional Jackiw-Rebbi model. It finds that the spatial separation between left- and right-handed zero modes follows a universal power law $|Δ_{\mathrm{abs}}|\propto d^γ$ as inter-brane distance $d\to0^+$, where the exponent $γ$ depends only on the topological index $N_{\mathrm{JR}}$, not on details like the scalar potential or mass gap. For $N_{\mathrm{JR}}=1$, $γ≈0.93–0.985$, with an exact result $I(d)=2d/\sinh(2d)$ in the sine-Gordon case, revealing $γ$ as a crossover plateau of an effective exponent tied to the Pöschl-Teller form of the zero mode.
\n\n
本文研究了在额外维度膜世界模型中两个畴壁合并时手征费米子局域性的丢失速率，采用$(1+1)$维Jackiw-Rebbi模型作为可控的解析平台。研究发现，当膜间距$d\to0^+$时，左右手零模的空间分离遵循普适幂律$|Δ_{\mathrm{abs}}|\propto d^γ$，其中指数$γ$仅由拓扑指标$N_{\mathrm{JR}}$决定，与标量势细节、质量能隙等无关。对于$N_{\mathrm{JR}}=1$，$γ\in[0.930,0.985]$；在sine-Gordon模型中，作者导出了精确的重叠积分$I(d)=2d/\sinh(2d)$，表明$γ$是有效指数$γ_{\mathrm{eff}}(d)$的交叉平台，其普适性源于零模的Pöschl-Teller结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand how chiral symmetry breaking emerges dynamically during brane collisions or mergers in braneworld scenarios, which are relevant for model-building in particle physics and cosmology. Specifically, the authors aim to determine whether the rate at which four-dimensional Yukawa couplings vanish as branes merge is governed by universal, topological principles rather than model-dependent details of the scalar field configuration.
\n\n
动机在于理解在膜世界场景中膜碰撞或合并过程中手征对称性如何动态破缺，这对粒子物理和宇宙学中的模型构建具有重要意义。作者特别关注四维Yukawa耦合在膜合并时趋于零的速率是否由普适的拓扑原理决定，而非依赖于标量场构型的模型细节。

Method: The authors employ the $(1+1)$-dimensional Jackiw-Rebbi model as an analytically tractable framework to study fermion zero modes bound to domain walls. They compute the spatial separation $|Δ_{\mathrm{abs}}|$ between left- and right-handed zero modes as a function of inter-wall distance $d$. By comparing integrable (sine-Gordon) and non-integrable (double sine-Gordon) scalar potentials—all sharing the same Jackiw-Rebbi topological index $N_{\mathrm{JR}}=1$—they isolate topological effects from dynamical ones. In the sine-Gordon case, they derive an exact closed-form expression for the overlap integral $I(d) = \int \psi_L(x)\psi_R(x)\,dx = 2d/\sinh(2d)$, and define a local effective exponent $γ_{\mathrm{eff}}(d) = d(\ln |Δ_{\mathrm{abs}}|)'/d(\ln d)$ to analyze the approach to the power-law regime.
\n\n
作者采用$(1+1)$维Jackiw-Rebbi模型作为可解析处理的框架，研究束缚在畴壁上的费米子零模。他们计算左右手零模的空间分离$|Δ_{\mathrm{abs}}|$作为膜间距$d$的函数。通过比较可积（sine-Gordon）与不可积（double sine-Gordon）的标量势——所有情形均具有相同的Jackiw-Rebbi拓扑指标$N_{\mathrm{JR}}=1$——以分离拓扑效应与动力学效应。在sine-Gordon情形下，他们导出了重叠积分$I(d) = \int \psi_L(x)\psi_R(x)\,dx = 2d/\sinh(2d)$的精确闭式表达，并定义局部有效指数$γ_{\mathrm{eff}}(d) = d(\ln |Δ_{\mathrm{abs}}|)'/d(\ln d)$来分析幂律行为的渐近过程。

Result: The key result is the discovery of a universal power-law scaling $|Δ_{\mathrm{abs}}|\propto d^γ$ as $d\to0^+$, with $γ$ determined solely by the topological index $N_{\mathrm{JR}}$. For $N_{\mathrm{JR}}=1$, numerical and analytical results across five distinct scalar potentials yield $γ\in[0.930,0.985]$. In the sine-Gordon model, the exact overlap $I(d)=2d/\sinh(2d)$ leads to an explicit formula for chiral separation, and the effective exponent $γ_{\mathrm{eff}}(d)$ exhibits a plateau near $d\sim1$ that defines the observed $γ<1$. This sub-unit exponent arises from the exponential tails of the Pöschl-Teller-like zero-mode wavefunctions, linking universality to the underlying supersymmetric quantum mechanics structure of the $N_{\mathrm{JR}}=1$ sector.
\n\n
核心结果是发现了当$d\to0^+$时普适的幂律标度$|Δ_{\mathrm{abs}}|\propto d^γ$，其中$γ$仅由拓扑指标$N_{\mathrm{JR}}$决定。对于$N_{\mathrm{JR}}=1$，在五种不同标量势下的数值与解析结果给出$γ\in[0.930,0.985]$。在sine-Gordon模型中，精确重叠积分$I(d)=2d/\sinh(2d)$导出了手征分离的显式公式，而有效指数$γ_{\mathrm{eff}}(d)$在$d\sim1$附近呈现平台，定义了观测到的$γ<1$。该小于1的指数源于Pöschl-Teller型零模波函数的指数尾部，其普适性可追溯至$N_{\mathrm{JR}}=1$扇区所对应的超对称量子力学结构。

Conclusion: The rate at which chiral fermion localization—and hence four-dimensional Yukawa couplings—vanishes during brane merging is a topological invariant, encoded in the Jackiw-Rebbi index $N_{\mathrm{JR}}$, and insensitive to the microscopic details of the scalar field theory that generates the domain walls. This universality stems from the shared asymptotic form of zero-mode wavefunctions dictated by topology, particularly their Pöschl-Teller structure for $N_{\mathrm{JR}}=1$. The work establishes that low-energy effective couplings in braneworld scenarios can be robustly predicted from topological data alone, even in non-integrable or strongly coupled settings.
\n\n
在膜合并过程中，手征费米子局域性（以及由此产生的四维Yukawa耦合）消失的速率是一个拓扑不变量，由Jackiw-Rebbi指标$N_{\mathrm{JR}}$编码，且对标量场论生成畴壁的微观细节不敏感。这种普适性源于拓扑所决定的零模波函数的共同渐近形式，特别是$N_{\mathrm{JR}}=1$情形下的Pöschl-Teller结构。该工作表明，即使在不可积或强耦合情形下，膜世界模型中的低能有效耦合也可仅从拓扑数据可靠预测。

Abstract: We investigate the rate at which chiral fermion localisation is lost when two domain walls merge in extra-dimensional braneworld scenarios, using the $(1+1)$-dimensional Jackiw-Rebbi framework as a controlled analytical laboratory. As the inter-brane separation $d$ decreases, left- and right-handed zero modes hybridise and chiral asymmetry is progressively lost. We show that the spatial separation between the chiral zero modes follows a universal power law $|Δ_{\mathrm{abs}}|\propto d^γ$ in the merging limit $d\to 0^{+}$, with the critical exponent $γ$ determined solely by the Jackiw-Rebbi topological index $N_{\mathrm{JR}}$, and independent of the fermionic mass gap, the integrability of the scalar sector, and the detailed shape of the domain wall profile. Comparing the integrable sine-Gordon model with four members of the non-integrable double sine-Gordon family, all sharing $N_{\mathrm{JR}}=1$, we find $γ\in[0.930,0.985]$. For the sine-Gordon model we derive the closed-form overlap integral $I(d)=2d/\sinh(2d)$, from which the exact chiral separation follows as a ratio of hyperbolic functions without free parameters. This result identifies $γ$ as the crossover plateau of a local effective exponent $γ_{\mathrm{eff}}(d)$, explaining the sub-unit value analytically and tracing the universality to the Pöschl-Teller structure of the $N_{\mathrm{JR}}=1$ zero mode. The universality of $γ$ implies that the rate of four-dimensional Yukawa coupling collapse during brane merging is a topological invariant, insensitive to the microscopic scalar dynamics generating the walls.

</details>


### [71] [Multicritical points of gravitational solitons and a black hole in four dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24783)
*Moaathe Belhaj Ahmed,Moisés Bravo-Gaete,Robert B. Mann,Constanza Quijada*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper constructs multicritical points in four-dimensional general relativity coupled to Plebański nonlinear electrodynamics via a polynomial structural function $\mathcal{H}(P)$, revealing rich thermodynamic phase structures with multiple coexisting magnetically charged solitons and a single electrically charged black hole, while the black hole branch lacks such multicritical behavior.
\n\n
本文通过多项式结构函数 $\mathcal{H}(P)$ 在四维广义相对论与 Plebański 非线性电动力学耦合系统中构建了多临界点，揭示了丰富的热力学相结构：多个磁荷孤子可与单个电荷黑洞共存，而黑洞分支则不表现出此类多临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to extend the understanding of critical phenomena in gravitational thermodynamics beyond standard black hole phase transitions by realizing multicritical points—generalizations of triple points—in four-dimensional general relativity. This requires going beyond Maxwell electrodynamics, using Plebański nonlinear electrodynamics as a framework where higher-order couplings can be systematically introduced to control phase structure.
\n\n
动机在于将引力热力学中的临界现象理解从标准黑洞相变拓展至多临界点（三重点的推广）的实现。这要求超越 Maxwell 电动力学，采用 Plebański 非线性电动力学作为框架，其中高阶耦合可被系统引入以调控相结构。

Method: The authors employ Plebański nonlinear electrodynamics with a polynomial structural function $\mathcal{H}(P)$, where $P = -\\frac{1}{4} F_{\\mu\\nu} F^{\\mu\\nu}$. They establish a mapping between matter Lagrangians expressed as power series in the Maxwell invariant $F$ and the Plebański formulation. Using this, they construct new families of electrically charged asymptotically AdS black holes and magnetically charged solitons. Thermodynamic analysis is performed in the grand-canonical ensemble, focusing on the free energy to identify swallowtail structures indicative of first-order phase transitions.
\n\n
作者采用具有多项式结构函数 $\mathcal{H}(P)$ 的 Plebański 非线性电动力学，其中 $P = -\\frac{1}{4} F_{\\mu\\nu} F^{\\mu\\nu}$。他们建立了以 Maxwell 不变量 $F$ 的幂级数表示的物质拉格朗日量与 Plebański 形式的映射关系，并据此构造出新的渐近反 de Sitter（AdS）带电黑洞和磁荷孤子族。在巨正则系综中进行热力学分析，通过自由能识别标志一级相变的 swallowtail 结构。

Result: The magnetically charged soliton sector exhibits multiple swallowtail structures in its free energy, enabling the coexistence of several distinct soliton phases with a single electrically charged black hole—realizing multicritical points that generalize triple points. The number of coexisting phases is directly controlled by the degree of the polynomial $\mathcal{H}(P)$. In contrast, the electrically charged black hole branch shows no swallowtail behavior and does not support coexistence with multiple solitons.
\n\n
磁荷孤子分支在其自由能中展现出多个 swallowtail 结构，使得多个不同的孤子相可与单个电荷黑洞共存，从而实现了对三重点的推广——多临界点。共存相的数量由多项式 $\mathcal{H}(P)$ 的次数直接控制。相比之下，电荷黑洞分支不显示 swallowtail 行为，也不支持与多个孤子共存。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that multicriticality in four-dimensional gravity can be engineered through the choice of nonlinear electrodynamics couplings, specifically via the degree of the polynomial structural function in Plebański theory. This establishes a direct correspondence between the microphysical parameters of the matter sector and macroscopic thermodynamic phase complexity, with solitons—not black holes—serving as the primary carriers of multicritical behavior.
\n\n
本文表明，四维引力中的多临界性可通过选择非线性电动力学耦合（特别是 Plebański 理论中多项式结构函数的次数）进行调控。这建立了物质扇区微观参数与宏观热力学相复杂性之间的直接对应关系，其中孤子（而非黑洞）是多临界行为的主要载体。

Abstract: We present the first realization of multicritical points in four-dimensional general relativity, specifically within the context of Plebański nonlinear electrodynamics, using a polynomial structural function denoted as $\mathcal{H}(P)$. We show that this construction provides a systematic mechanism to engineer multicritical behavior in gravitational systems. By establishing an explicit mapping between matter theories expressed as power series in the Maxwell invariant $F$ and the Plebański formulation, we construct new families of electrically charged asymptotically anti-de Sitter black holes and magnetically charged solitons. In the grand-canonical ensemble, we analyze their thermodynamic properties and uncover a rich phase structure. We demonstrate that the soliton sector develops multiple swallowtail structures, signaling first-order phase transitions and allowing the coexistence of several magnetically charged solitons with a single electrically charged black hole. These configurations define multicritical points that generalize previously known triple points. We further show that the number of coexisting phases is controlled by the degree of the polynomial structural function, providing a direct link between the nonlinear electrodynamics couplings and the thermodynamic phase structure. In contrast, the black hole branch does not display swallowtail behavior, and it does not allow multiple electrically charged black holes to coexist with a magnetically charged soliton.

</details>


### [72] [Defect Conformal Manifolds along RG Domain Walls between $\mathbb Z_N$-Parafermions and Minimal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24978)
*Jin-Rui Zhang,Jing-Hao Jin,Ting-Kai Chen,Jin Chen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper studies non-perturbative renormalization group (RG) domain walls between the $\mathbb{Z}_N$ parafermion theory (critical $N$-state Potts model) and the Virasoro minimal model $\mathcal{M}_{N+1}$, using a bottom-up approach based on ``phantom currents'' arising from preserved non-invertible $\mathfrak{so}(3)_N$ symmetries. The authors identify a spin-1 phantom current that generates a continuous defect conformal manifold and a spin-2 operator—descendant under a $W^{(3)}$-algebra—that fixes UV-IR stress tensor mixing. They compute the transmission rate across the conformal manifold exactly and show it vanishes as $N \\to \\infty$, signaling macroscopic target space collapse.
\n\n
本文研究了在$\mathbb{Z}_N$ parafermion理论（临界$N$态Potts模型）与Virasoro minimal model $\mathcal{M}_{N+1}$之间的非微扰重整化群（RG）domain wall。作者采用一种自下而上的方法，基于由保留的non-invertible对称性$\mathfrak{so}(3)_N$所诱导的“phantom currents”。他们识别出一个spin-1 phantom current，该流生成了一个连续的defect conformal manifold；同时发现一个spin-2算符——作为$W^{(3)}$-algebra下spin-1 phantom current的descendant——通过cluster decomposition principle严格约束了UV-IR应力张量混合。基于此代数框架，作者精确计算了conformal manifold上依赖参数的transmission rate，并证明其在大$N$极限下严格趋于零，表明发生了macroscopic target space collapse。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the lack of explicit constructions for non-perturbative RG domain walls—specifically Gaiotto-type interfaces—between the $\mathbb{Z}_N$ parafermion CFT and the Virasoro minimal model $\mathcal{M}_{N+1}$. Standard perturbative techniques fail in this genuinely strong-coupling regime, necessitating alternative strategies to understand the structure of such RG flows and the associated defect dynamics.
\n\n
动机源于目前尚无显式的非微扰RG domain wall（特别是Gaiotto型interface）在$\mathbb{Z}_N$ parafermion共形场论与Virasoro minimal model $\mathcal{M}_{N+1}$之间的构造。在这一本质上强耦合的区域，标准微扰方法失效，因此需要替代策略来理解此类RG流的结构及相关的defect动力学。

Method: The authors adopt a bottom-up approach centered on symmetry preservation: they track the non-invertible $\mathfrak{so}(3)_N$ symmetry along the RG flow and deduce the existence of localized operators—``phantom currents''—on the defect. Using representation theory of the $W^{(3)}$-algebra and constraints from cluster decomposition, they determine the exact spectrum of these defect-localized currents. The spin-1 current is shown to generate marginal deformations, while its $W^{(3)}$-descendant (spin-2) fixes the mixing between UV and IR stress tensors. This algebraic data enables exact computation of the transmission coefficient as a function of the defect coupling.
\n\n
作者采用一种以对称性保持为核心的自下而上方法：沿RG流追踪non-invertible对称性$\mathfrak{so}(3)_N$，并推断出位于defect上的局域算符——“phantom currents”。利用$W^{(3)}$-algebra的表示论及cluster decomposition原理的约束，他们确定了这些defect局域currents的精确谱。其中spin-1 current被证明可生成边缘形变，而其$W^{(3)}$-descendant（spin-2）则固定了UV与IR应力张量之间的混合。这一代数结构使得transmission coefficient可作为defect耦合参数的函数被精确计算。

Result: The key results are: (1) identification of a spin-1 phantom current that renders the RG interface marginally deformable, giving rise to a continuous defect conformal manifold; (2) discovery of a spin-2 operator—necessarily a $W^{(3)}$-descendant of the spin-1 current—that enforces a rigid relation between UV and IR stress tensors via cluster decomposition; (3) exact analytic expression for the transmission rate $\\mathcal{T}(\\lambda)$ across the conformal manifold; and (4) proof that $\\mathcal{T} \\to 0$ as $N \\to \\infty$, interpreted as macroscopic target space collapse in the large-$N$ limit.
\n\n

Conclusion: The paper establishes that non-perturbative RG domain walls between distinct rational CFTs can be systematically analyzed through preserved non-invertible symmetries and emergent defect-localized currents. 本文表明，不同rational CFT之间的非微扰RG domain wall可通过保留的non-invertible对称性及涌现的defect局域currents进行系统分析。$W$-algebra表示论与cluster decomposition原理的相互作用对defect动力学施加了精确约束，从而可精确计算transmission rate等物理观测量。大$N$极限下transmission的消失揭示了对称性增强、target space几何与量子相变中信息丢失之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We investigate the renormalization group (RG) domain walls interpolating between the $\mathbb{Z}_N$ parafermion theory (the critical $N$-state Potts model) and the Virasoro minimal model $\mathcal{M}_{N+1}$. Furthermore, we show that an extra spin-2 operator, crucially as a $W^{(3)}$-algebra descendant of the spin-1 phantom current, rigidly constrains the UV-IR stress tensor mixing via the cluster decomposition principle. Abstract: The traditional conjecture that RR-flux is quantized in stable K-cohomology fails to account for the presence of NS-brane sources: These impose nonlinear relations -- reductions of the famous quadratic relation on M-brane flux -- that can only be captured by unstable nonabelian cohomology theories.   <summary>Details</summary>

动机在于建立量子计算复杂性、伪随机性与全息理论中时空几何涌现之间的具体联系——特别是解释为何边界理论（如 SYK）中的典型量子态必须对应于具有视界的黑洞几何，而非无视界几何。这回应了“Cryptographic Censorship”原理，该原理认为区分无视界微观态与黑洞需要超越多项式时间的计算资源。

Method: The authors use Weingarten calculus and random matrix universality to prove that the SYK disorder ensemble forms an approximate unitary $k$-design for all $k = \poly(N)$, with error controlled by the spectral form factor. They introduce the planted-SYK hardness conjecture and support it via spectral universality and the low-degree polynomial method. Combining this with known results on efficient causal wedge reconstruction in JT gravity, they infer gravitational pseudorandomness. The distinguishing operator is identified as the regularized geodesic length of the maximal interior slice, analyzed through its time evolution.

作者使用 Weingarten calculus 和随机矩阵普适性，证明 SYK 无序系综对所有 $k = \poly(N)$ 构成近似酉 $k$-design，其误差由谱形因子（spectral form factor）控制。他们提出 planted-SYK hardness conjecture，并通过谱普适性和低次多项式框架提供证据。结合 JT 引力中已知的高效因果楔重构结果，推断出引力伪随机性。区分算符被确定为最大内部切片的正则化测地线长度，并通过其时间演化进行分析。

Abstract: We argue that the SYK model provides a conditional realization of Cryptographic Censorship in JT gravity. Its prediction gap grows linearly with time due to the stretching of the black hole interior, linking Cryptographic Censorship to the complexity equals volume conjecture.

本文利用非均匀Hartree近似对Friedberg-Lee-Sirlin模型中的Q-ball进行了实时量子模拟，揭示了经典与量子动力学机制的明显区分、平均场与涨落模之间Noether荷的周期性交换，以及在原本经典稳定的构型中由量子效应引发的不稳定性。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Simulations are performed in $3+1$ dimensions for single-Q-ball initial conditions.

Result: The simulations reveal two distinct regimes: (i) a classical regime where quantum fluctuations are small and dynamics follow classical trajectories, and (ii) a quantum regime where fluctuations carry a significant fraction of the Noether charge. A periodic transfer of Noether charge between mean fields and fluctuation modes is observed. Moreover, configurations that are classically stable become unstable under Hartree quantum corrections within an intermediate parameter window.
\n\n
模拟揭示了两种不同机制：(i) 经典机制，其中量子涨落较小，动力学遵循经典轨迹；(ii) 量子机制，其中涨落携带显著比例的Noether荷。此外，观察到平均场与涨落模之间Noether荷的周期性转移。更重要的是，在中间参数窗口内，原本经典稳定的构型在Hartree量子修正下变得不稳定。

Conclusion: This work demonstrates that quantum fluctuations can qualitatively alter the dynamics and stability of Q-balls beyond classical predictions. Abstract: We study the real-time quantum dynamics of Q-balls in the Friedberg-Lee-Sirlin model within the inhomogeneous Hartree approximation. The mean fields are evolved self-consistently with the leading quantum two-point functions, which are implemented numerically through a stochastic ensemble representation. After introducing a renormalized formulation and a classical-limit scaling, we simulate single-Q-ball configurations in $3+1$ dimensions and compare their quantum-corrected evolution with the corresponding classical dynamics. We find a clear separation between a classical regime, where quantum fluctuations remain small and the evolution closely follows the classical solution, and a quantum regime, where the fluctuation sector carries a sizable fraction of the Noether charge. We also observe a periodic exchange of Noether charge between the mean fields and the fluctuation modes within the Hartree approximation. We further investigate the stability of quantum-corrected Q-balls and find an intermediate window in which configurations that are classically stable become unstable once Hartree fluctuations are included. Our results provide a first step toward real-time quantum simulations of Q-balls in renormalizable two-field soliton models.

论文识别出不同模型配分函数结构中的强相似性，通过参数形变支持所提出的普适性猜想，并证明能谱性质与Wheeler-DeWitt波函数行为在各理论间一致。此外，在虫洞背景中对纠缠熵、复杂度和RG流的分析进一步强化了这些普适性，而虫洞-缺陷对应关系提供了额外的一致性检验。

Conclusion: The results suggest that diverse low-dimensional quantum gravity models exhibit deep universal features, likely rooted in shared holographic or topological origins. Abstract: In this work, first we discuss the connections between various low-dimensional quantum gravity models, including 3d Chern-Simons, 2d JT and 2d BF theory, 2d Liouville and 2d WZW, and 1d Schwarzian through holography and dimension reduction, and discuss some universalities in their partition functions. We change the parameters in each model and based on the change in the structure of the partition function, strengthen our conjectures. We also use eigenfunctions, spectra and the behaviors of Wheeler-DeWitt wavefunctions to generate more universalities between these low-dimensional quantum gravity models, specifically in their partition functions. Then, we use entanglement entropy, complexity and RG flows, particularly in the context of wormholes, to find more universalities in quantum gravity models. Finally, we use the new results about the connections between wormholes and defects to discuss our universalities further.

### [77] [Negative entropy in scrambling black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25315)

Main category: hep-th

本文在Hawking粒子对产生图像中，为将黑洞视界面积解释为相干信息（即负条件量子熵）提供了微观统计力学基础。当一个落入物体被吸收并混入黑洞微观自由度时，所引起的熵增在Hawking温度下具有普适的正则形式，与该物体初始携带的熵无关。将此熵公式应用于时间反演配对的Hawking辐射模式时，其配分函数贡献在相干信息平衡中相互抵消，最终视界面积的变化仅由能量流决定，与黑洞热力学第一定律一致。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to reconcile the geometric notion of black hole entropy (proportional to horizon area) with quantum information concepts—specifically coherent information and conditional quantum entropy—within a microscopic statistical-mechanical framework. 动机在于通过微观统计力学框架，将黑洞熵（正比于视界面积）的几何概念与量子信息概念（特别是相干信息和条件量子熵）相协调。这旨在回答关于信息在scrambling过程中如何编码、以及黑洞热力学第一定律如何从Hawking辐射中的量子纠缠动力学中涌现等基础问题。

Method: The authors employ a statistical-mechanical model of a scrambling black hole as a finite quantum reservoir at the Hawking temperature $T_H$. They compute the entropy increase $\Delta S_{\\text{BH}}$ when an arbitrary infalling system is absorbed and fully scrambled, using the relation between coherent information $I_c = -S(A|B)$ and horizon area. By modeling Hawking pair creation as time-reversed partners, they evaluate the partition-function contributions of infalling and outgoing modes and analyze their cancellation in the coherent-information balance.

作者将scrambling黑洞建模为处于Hawking温度 $T_H$ 的有限量子储层，采用统计力学方法。当任意落入系统被吸收并完全scrambled时，他们利用相干信息 $I_c = -S(A|B)$ 与视界面积的关系计算黑洞熵增 $\Delta S_{\\text{BH}}$。通过将Hawking粒子对产生视为时间反演配对，他们评估落入模式与出射模式的配分函数贡献，并分析其在相干信息平衡中的抵消机制。

Result: The entropy increase of the black hole upon absorbing an infalling object is given by $\Delta S_{\\text{BH}} = \\beta_H \\langle E \\rangle + O(1/S_{\\text{BH}})$, where $\\beta_H = 1/T_H$, $\\langle E \\rangle$ is the average energy of the infalling system, and corrections are suppressed by the large black hole entropy. For time-reversed Hawking pairs, the coherent-information contributions from the infalling and outgoing modes cancel exactly in the partition function, so the net change in horizon area depends solely on the energy flux: $\delta A = 8\\pi G \\langle T_{uu} \\rangle$, matching the first law $\\delta M = (\\kappa/8\\pi G) \\delta A$.
\n\n
黑洞吸收落入物体后的熵增为 $\Delta S_{\\text{BH}} = \\beta_H \\langle E \\rangle + O(1/S_{\\text{BH}})$，其中 $\\beta_H = 1/T_H$，$\\langle E \\rangle$ 为落入系统的平均能量，修正项受大黑洞熵压制。对于时间反演的Hawking对，落入模式与出射模式在配分函数中的相干信息贡献精确抵消，因此视界面积的净变化仅依赖于能量流：$\delta A = 8\\pi G \\langle T_{uu} \\rangle$，与第一定律 $\\delta M = (\\kappa/8\\pi G) \\delta A$ 一致。

Conclusion: The horizon area of a scrambling black hole fundamentally measures coherent information, not just thermodynamic entropy. The universality of the entropy response at the Hawking temperature and the exact cancellation of entanglement contributions from Hawking pairs provide a microscopic explanation for why the black hole first law depends only on energy flux, reinforcing the connection between quantum information theory and black hole thermodynamics.

scrambling黑洞的视界面积本质上度量的是相干信息，而不仅仅是热力学熵。在Hawking温度下熵响应的普适性，以及Hawking对纠缠贡献的精确抵消，为黑洞热力学第一定律仅依赖于能量流提供了微观解释，强化了量子信息理论与黑洞热力学之间的联系。

Abstract: We present a microscopic statistical-mechanical foundation for interpreting the horizon area of a scrambling black hole as coherent information, equivalently negative conditional quantum entropy, in Hawking's pair-creation picture. We derive the entropy increase induced in a black hole when an infalling object is absorbed and scrambled into its microscopic degrees of freedom. Up to finite-reservoir corrections, this increase takes a canonical form at the Hawking temperature, regardless of the entropy carried by the infalling object. Applying this entropy formula to an incoming mode paired by time reversal with an outgoing Hawking radiation mode, we show that their partition-function contributions cancel in the coherent-information balance associated with the horizon area. The resulting area response is then determined only by the energy flux, in agreement with the black-hole first law.

Abstract: We extend the celestial Roiban-Spradlin-Volovich-Witten (RSVW) formalism developed in our previous work to minitwistor superspace. Using the Drummond-Henn formula for all tree-level amplitudes in N=4 supersymmetric Yang-Mills (SYM) theory, we construct tree-level N^k-MHV celestial leaf amplitudes and show that their minitwistor-Fourier transforms are given by integrals over moduli spaces of families of minitwistor lines. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Recent extensions of the classical Magnusian formalism into quantum field theory have revealed rich loop structures and diagrammatic relations, but lacked an efficient method to compute the associated Murua coefficients at loop level. Conclusion: The quantum Magnusian’s diagrammatic structure is fully captured by recursive edge contraction rules derived from dual basis representations. 量子 Magnusian 的图示结构可完全由源自双重基表示的递归边收缩规则所刻画。这表明其矩阵元的计算可归约为组合图操作，从而为量子场论中时间演化算符对数建立了一个自洽的图示演算体系。

Abstract: The logarithm of the time-evolution operator has been termed Magnusian, on account of the fact that its expansion describes the Magnus series. The diagrammatic expansion and computation of the classical Magnusian has been completely established in terms of tree graphs and their Hopf algebra. Recent works initiated extensions into quantum field theory, revealing general structures of loop expansions while finding intriguing relations between different diagrams. In this work, we advance the loop expansion further by providing an efficient diagrammatic algorithm to calculate the weight factor of each graph in the quantum Magnusian, known as the Murua coefficient. This is achieved by incorporating two complementary perspectives on the Magnusian at the same time: the color basis and the black-and-white basis. We extract the Murua coefficients from the Magnus series by utilizing these two bases while implementing an exponentiated Wick contraction. In turn, we identify the loop-level extension of Murua's recursive formula. Eventually, we establish a set of edge contraction rules which facilitate a direct recursive computation of the Murua coefficients at the purely diagrammatic level, without referencing or directly manipulating the underlying Magnus expansion. This shows that the matrix elements of the quantum Magnusian can be computed from graph manipulations alone.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand how bulk locality and causal propagation in global AdS$_3$—particularly the distinctive periodicity due to its compact spatial slices—are holographically encoded in the dual CFT$_2$ on the cylinder. u} \\rangle$ of CFT$_2$ states dual to localized bulk excitations in global AdS$_3$. The resulting energy density is decomposed into left- and right-moving (chiral) components, whose propagation is tracked over global time $\\tau$.

作者分析了CFT$_2$中对应于全局AdS$_3$局域体激发的态的边界应力张量$\\langle T_{\\mu\
u} \\rangle$。他们考虑两类态：(1) 由主算符叠加构造的波包态，在能量密度中产生领头阶的手征类δ函数峰；(2) 由重主算符在欧几里得时间中正则化所制备的态，其精确应力张量可通过从平面到圆柱面的共形映射计算得到。所得能量密度被分解为左移和右移（手征）分量，并追踪其在全局时间$\\tau$下的传播行为。

Abstract: We study how bulk propagation in global AdS$_3$ is encoded in boundary energy densities of the dual CFT$_2$ on the cylinder. These pulses reach the antipodal point and return to the original point at the same global times as the radial null geodesic in the bulk. Thus the boundary energy density captures the boundary-to-boundary propagation and periodicity of localized bulk excitations in global AdS$_3$.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding black hole phase transitions and critical phenomena through the lens of non-equilibrium thermodynamics and dynamical systems, rather than solely via equilibrium thermodynamic analogies; the goal is to uncover universal features and relaxation dynamics near critical points that are independent of specific black hole models.

通过非平衡热力学和动力系统视角理解黑洞相变与临界现象，而非仅依赖平衡热力学类比；目标是揭示在临界点附近独立于具体黑洞模型的普适特征与弛豫动力学。

Method: The authors construct an effective thermodynamic landscape where black hole states evolve under a flow parameter $τ$, interpreted as phenomenological relaxation time. 在热力学临界点附近，流方程简化为由分岔类型（如鞍结分岔或叉式分岔）决定的普适数学结构，从而导出与具体模型无关的普适类。分析预言了临界慢化：当系统趋近临界点时，弛豫时间 $τ$ 发散，其发散指数完全由局部分岔结构决定。

Abstract: We present a dynamical systems approach to black hole thermodynamic criticality based on bifurcation equations. We construct an effective thermodynamic landscape in which black holes relax toward equilibrium fixed points. To describe this process, we introduce a flow parameter $τ$, interpreted as a phenomenological relaxation time, which governs the approach toward equilibrium configurations in thermodynamic state space. Near critical points, the thermodynamic flow simplifies into universal mathematical forms, which allows different black holes to be grouped into different universality classes based on their critical behaviour. Our analysis further shows critical slowing down, with relaxation timescales determined entirely by the local bifurcation structure.

</details>

This supports a bulk interpretation involving plane-wave black holes dressed by M2 giant gravitons.
\n\n
我们通过对所有由分拆标记的超对称真空扇区求和，计算了BMN矩阵量子力学的有限$N$ Witten指标。发展了两种互补方法：对称群特征标展开（将每个固定fugacity阶化为有限组合和）和以有根树（多分拆情形下推广为有色树）组织极点的留数展开，并通过直接积分和常数项提取进行交叉验证。显式计算至$N=9$的结果显示，在电荷$j \\sim N^2$附近存在$\\mathcal{O}(N^2)$的熵增长，且扇区求和不抵消该增长。我们观察到一种“主导性切换”现象：在$N=5$时，主导贡献扇区从单分拆型转变为双分拆型。这为将BMN指标作为受保护平面波黑洞微观态诊断工具提供了有限$N$定量基础，并暗示其体对偶解释涉及被宏观M2巨引力子修饰的平面波黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper aims to understand the microscopic origin of black hole entropy in the BMN (Berenstein–Maldacena–Nastase) deformation of $\\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM, which has a dual description in terms of M-theory on a plane-wave background. The Witten index counts protected supersymmetric states and serves as a robust observable for probing black hole microstates. However, prior studies focused on large-$N$ limits or specific sectors; a complete finite-$N$ analysis across all partition-labeled vacuum sectors is needed to uncover nonperturbative structure and possible phase transitions in the state counting.
\n\n
本文旨在理解BMN（Berenstein–Maldacena–Nastase）形变下的$\\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM理论中黑洞熵的微观起源，其对偶描述为平面波背景上的M理论。Witten指标计数受保护的超对称态，是探测黑洞微观态的稳健观测量。然而，先前研究集中于大$N$极限或特定扇区；要揭示态计数中的非微扰结构及可能的相变，需对所有由分拆标记的真空扇区进行完整的有限$N$分析。

Method: The authors evaluate the Witten index by summing contributions from all supersymmetric vacua labeled by integer partitions of $N$. For each sector, the index is given by a unitary-matrix integral. They develop two complementary computational techniques: (1) a symmetric-group character expansion that expresses the integrand in terms of Schur functions, reducing the coefficient of each fugacity monomial to a finite sum over representations; and (2) a multi-variable residue theorem approach where poles are classified by rooted trees, extended to colored trees for multi-partition sectors. These methods are validated against direct integration and constant-term extraction in low-$N$ examples.
\n\n
作者通过对所有由$N$的整数分拆标记的超对称真空扇区求和来计算Witten指标。每个扇区的指标由一个酉矩阵积分给出。他们发展了两种互补的计算技术：(1) 对称群特征标展开，将被积函数用Schur函数表示，将每个fugacity单项式的系数化为有限个表示的和；(2) 多变量留数定理方法，其中极点由有根树分类，并推广到多分拆扇区的有色树情形。这些方法在低$N$例子中通过直接积分和常数项提取进行了验证。

Result: Explicit evaluation for all sectors up to $N=9$ yields several key findings: (i) the index exhibits $\\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ entropy growth near charge $j \\sim N^2$, indicating an exponential number of protected states; (ii) this growth survives the full sum over sectors, ruling out exact cancellations; (iii) a “dominance switching” occurs at $N=5$: for $N<5$, the largest contribution near $j=N^2$ comes from single-partition sectors, while for $N\\geq5$, double-partition sectors dominate. The data provide the first finite-$N$ evidence for a rich sectoral structure underlying the index.
\n\n
对$N\\leq9$的所有扇区进行显式计算得到若干关键结果：(i) 指标在电荷$j \\sim N^2$附近呈现$\\mathcal{O}(N^2)$的熵增长，表明存在指数级数量的受保护态；(ii) 该增长在全扇区求和后依然存在，排除了精确抵消的可能性；(iii) 在$N=5$处发生“主导性切换”：对于$N<5$，$j=N^2$附近的主导贡献来自单分拆扇区，而$N\\geq5$时则由双分拆扇区主导。这些数据首次提供了有限$N$下指标背后丰富扇区结构的证据。

Conclusion: The finite-$N$ Witten index of BMN matrix quantum mechanics reveals a nontrivial organization of supersymmetric states with emergent $\\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ entropy, supporting its use as a probe of protected black hole microstates in the plane-wave geometry. The dominance switching suggests a transition in the nature of dominant configurations, interpreted in the bulk as plane-wave black holes dressed by macroscopic M2 giant gravitons—mirroring the $AdS_5\\times S^5$ picture of black holes with giant graviton hair. This work establishes a quantitative foundation for future studies of black hole microstructure in non-AdS holography.
\n\n
BMN矩阵量子力学的有限$N$ Witten指标揭示了超对称态的非平凡组织结构，并展现出涌现的$\\mathcal{O}(N^2)$熵，支持将其作为平面波几何中受保护黑洞微观态的探针。主导性切换暗示主导构型性质的转变，其体对偶解释为被宏观M2巨引力子修饰的平面波黑洞——类比于$AdS_5\\times S^5$中带有巨引力子“毛发”的黑洞图像。本工作为未来在非AdS全息框架下研究黑洞微观结构奠定了定量基础。

Abstract: We compute the finite-$N$ Witten index of BMN matrix quantum mechanics after summing over all partition-labeled supersymmetric vacuum sectors. Starting from the unitary-matrix integral for each sector, we develop two complementary evaluation methods: a symmetric-group character expansion, which reduces each fixed fugacity order to a finite combinatorial sum, and a residue expansion in which the contributing poles are organized by rooted trees, with a colored-tree generalization for multi-partition sectors. Direct integration and constant-term extraction provide independent checks in explicit examples. We evaluate every vacuum sector for $N\leq 9$. In the equal-fugacity expansion, the coefficients near charges $j\sim N^2$ show entropy growth of order $N^2$, and the sector sum does not cancel this growth. The finite-$N$ data also reveal a nontrivial sectoral organization: near $j=N^2$, the sector giving the largest contribution changes with $N$, from single-partition sectors at small rank to double-partition sectors starting at $N=5$. We call this phenomenon dominance switching. These results provide a quantitative finite-$N$ foundation for using the BMN index as a diagnostic of protected plane-wave black-hole microstates and suggest a bulk interpretation in terms of plane-wave black holes dressed by macroscopic M2 giant gravitons, analogous to dual dressed black holes in $AdS_5\times S^5$.

</details>


### [83] [Fermion renormalized vertex functions, effective mass, and condensate in an external Yang-Mills gauge field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25578)
*V. V. Parazian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper computes the renormalized fermion-gluon vertex, fermion effective mass, and fermion condensate for a fermion propagating in a classical non-Abelian plane-wave Yang-Mills background using an exact Green's function of the Dirac operator. Calculations are performed in axial gauge $k^{\\mu} \\mathcal{A}_{\\mu}^{a}=0$, preserving gauge invariance, with implications for strong-field QCD and non-Abelian Schwinger pair production.
\n\n
本文利用Dirac算符在经典非阿贝尔平面波Yang-Mills背景场中的精确Green函数，计算了传播费米子的重整化费米子-胶子顶点、费米子有效质量及费米子凝聚。所有计算在轴向规范$k^{\\mu} \\mathcal{A}_{\\mu}^{a}=0$下进行，保持规范不变性，对强场QCD和非阿贝尔Schwinger对产生具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding non-perturbative dynamics of QCD in strong external gauge fields is essential for phenomena like gluon saturation, color glass condensate, and non-Abelian analogs of the Schwinger effect. Standard perturbative methods fail in such regimes, necessitating exact or semi-analytic treatments of fermion dynamics in classical Yang-Mills backgrounds. The axial gauge choice avoids spurious singularities while maintaining manifest gauge covariance.
\n\n
理解强外规范场中QCD的非微扰动力学对于胶子饱和、色玻璃凝聚态以及Schwinger效应的非阿贝尔类比等现象至关重要。在这些强场区域，标准微扰方法失效，因此需要对经典Yang-Mills背景中场中费米子动力学进行精确或半解析处理。轴向规范的选择可避免虚假奇点，同时保持显式的规范协变性。

Method: The authors employ the exact Green's function (propagator) of the Dirac operator in a non-Abelian plane-wave gauge field $\\mathcal{A}_\\mu^a(x) = \\delta_\\mu^\\nu n_\\nu \\phi^a(k\\cdot x)$ with $n^2 = 0$. Using this propagator, they construct the renormalized fermion-gluon vertex via functional differentiation, compute the on-shell fermion self-energy $\\Sigma(p)$ to extract the effective mass $m_{\\text{eff}}$, and evaluate the gauge-background-dependent fermion condensate $\\langle \\bar{\\psi}\\psi \\rangle_{\\mathcal{A}}$. All calculations respect the axial gauge condition $k^\\mu \\mathcal{A}_\\mu^a = 0$.
\n\n
作者采用非阿贝尔平面波规范场$\\mathcal{A}_\\mu^a(x) = \\delta_\\mu^\\nu n_\\nu \\phi^a(k\\cdot x)$（其中$n^2 = 0$）中Dirac算符的精确Green函数（传播子）。利用该传播子，通过泛函微分构造重整化费米子-胶子顶点，计算壳层上费米子自能$\\Sigma(p)$以提取有效质量$m_{\\text{eff}}$，并评估依赖于规范背景的费米子凝聚$\\langle \\bar{\\psi}\\psi \\rangle_{\\mathcal{A}}$。所有计算均满足轴向规范条件$k^\\mu \\mathcal{A}_\\mu^a = 0$。

Result: The renormalized vertex is explicitly derived and shown to satisfy Ward–Takahashi identities in the background field formalism. The fermion acquires a field-dependent effective mass $m_{\\text{eff}}(\\mathcal{A})$, and a non-vanishing, gauge-invariant condensate $\\langle \\bar{\\psi}\\psi \\rangle_{\\mathcal{A}}$ is generated even for massless bare fermions, signaling spontaneous chiral symmetry breaking induced by the strong non-Abelian background. Results are analytic and non-perturbative in the gauge field strength.
\n\n
重整化顶点被显式导出，并证明在背景场形式下满足Ward–Takahashi恒等式。费米子获得依赖于场的有效质量$m_{\\text{eff}}(\\mathcal{A})$，即使裸费米子质量为零，也会产生非零且规范不变的凝聚$\\langle \\bar{\\psi}\\psi \\rangle_{\\mathcal{A}}$，表明强非阿贝尔背景诱导了手征对称性的自发破缺。结果在规范场强度中是非微扰且解析的。

Conclusion: This work provides an exact, non-perturbative framework for studying fermionic observables in strong classical Yang-Mills fields. It demonstrates that non-Abelian background fields can dynamically generate mass and condensates, offering a pathway to explore non-perturbative QCD phenomena such as the non-Abelian Schwinger mechanism and vacuum structure modification under extreme field conditions.
\n\n
本工作为研究强经典Yang-Mills场中的费米子可观测量提供了一个精确的非微扰框架。研究表明，非阿贝尔背景场可动态生成质量与凝聚，为探索非微扰QCD现象（如非阿贝尔Schwinger机制及极端场条件下真空结构的改变）开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We investigate the renormalized fermion-gluon vertex, the fermion effective mass, and the fermion condensate when the fermion propagates in an external Yang-Mills gauge field. We use an exact Green's function for the Dirac operator in a non-Abelian plane-wave gauge field to construct the renormalized vertex function, calculate the on-shell fermion self-energy, and the background-dependent condensate. We consider both the background and operator fields in the axial gauge $k^{μ} \mathcal{A}_{μ}^{a}=0$, thereby preserving the gauge. Its applications to strong-field QCD and non-Abelian Schwinger physics are discussed.

</details>


### [84] [A Casimir obstruction to asymptotically flat black-brane completions of non-supersymmetric 7-branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25588)
*Yuta Hamada,Hayate Kimura*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper investigates axisymmetric 7-brane solutions in 10d supergravity coupled to dilaton-gravity, incorporating Casimir energy from monodromies around the transverse circle. While naked singularities arise in the absence of Casimir effects, the inclusion of Casimir backreaction prevents a regular black-brane resolution with asymptotically locally flat geometry.
\n\n
本文研究了十维超引力中与胀子-引力耦合的轴对称7-brane解，并纳入了由横向圆周上单值性（monodromy）诱导的Casimir能量。在无Casimir效应时，系统可解析求解但存在裸奇点；而引入Casimir反作用后，在所设ansatz下无法构造具有渐近局部平坦性的正则黑brane解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from understanding how quantum effects—specifically Casimir energy due to monodromies—affect classical brane geometries in string theory. The authors aim to determine whether such quantum corrections can resolve the naked singularities present in classical 7-brane solutions by replacing them with regular black-brane horizons while preserving asymptotically locally flat boundary conditions.
\n\n
动机源于理解量子效应（特别是由单值性引起的Casimir能量）如何影响弦理论中的经典brane几何。作者旨在确定这类量子修正是否能通过用正则黑brane视界替代裸奇点，同时保持渐近局部平坦边界条件，从而解决经典7-brane解中的裸奇点问题。

Method: The authors consider axisymmetric 7-brane configurations in the dilaton-gravity sector of 10-dimensional supergravity. They first solve the system analytically without Casimir energy, revealing naked singularities at finite proper distance despite locally flat asymptotics. Then, they incorporate the backreaction of Casimir energy induced by monodromies around the compact transverse direction and analyze whether a regular black-brane solution with a finite horizon can emerge within their ansatz.
\n\n
作者在十维超引力的胀子-引力部分中考虑轴对称的7-brane构型。首先在忽略Casimir能量的情况下解析求解系统，发现尽管具有局部平坦渐近行为，但在有限固有距离处存在裸奇点。随后，他们纳入由横向紧致方向上单值性诱导的Casimir能量的反作用，并在其ansatz框架内分析是否能出现具有有限视界的正则黑brane解。

Result: Without Casimir energy, the 7-brane solutions are analytically solvable and admit arbitrary deficit angles with locally flat asymptotics, but contain a naked singularity. When Casimir backreaction is included, the authors find that it obstructs the existence of an asymptotically locally flat black-brane solution—i.e., no regular horizon can replace the singularity within the assumed symmetry and field content.
\n\n
在无Casimir能量时，7-brane解可解析求得，允许任意亏角（deficit angle）并具有局部平坦渐近性，但包含裸奇点。当纳入Casimir反作用后，作者发现它阻碍了渐近局部平坦黑brane解的存在——即在所假设的对称性和场内容下，无法用正则视界替代该奇点。

Conclusion: Casimir energy from monodromies does not resolve the naked singularity of classical 7-branes into a regular black-brane horizon under asymptotically locally flat boundary conditions within the considered framework. This suggests that either more general ansätze or additional physical ingredients beyond the dilaton-gravity sector are required for singularity resolution in such setups.
\n\n
由单值性产生的Casimir能量在所考虑的框架和渐近局部平坦边界条件下，并不能将经典7-brane的裸奇点解析为正则黑brane视界。这表明，要实现此类构型中的奇点解析，可能需要更一般的ansatz或超出胀子-引力部分的额外物理要素。

Abstract: We study axisymmetric 7-brane solutions in the dilaton-gravity sector of 10d supergravity, including the backreaction of the Casimir energy induced by monodromies around the transverse circle. Without Casimir energy, we find that the system is analytically solvable and admits locally flat asymptotics with arbitrary deficit angles, but the corresponding solutions contain a naked singularity at finite proper distance. We investigate whether this singularity can be replaced by a regular finite-horizon black-brane core once Casimir backreaction is included. We find that, within our ansatz, Casimir backreaction obstructs an asymptotically locally flat black-brane completion of the naked 7-brane solution.

</details>


### [85] [Endpoint formulation and Molien--Weyl structure for the \(N=2\), large--\(d\) BFSS/BMN models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25647)
*Badis Ydri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper investigates the $N=2$, large-$d$ sector of BFSS/BMN matrix quantum mechanics in the Gaussian regime using a novel radial endpoint formulation. This approach integrates out bulk, gauge, and longitudinal degrees of freedom, yielding an effective theory for transverse endpoint variables governed by a holonomy potential. The authors establish equivalence with the angular Molien–Weyl description up to a universal factor, enabling extraction of the low-temperature expansion. In the continuum limit, the quadratic coefficient splits into Gaussian, $D$-, and $\\beta$-channels; the naive Gaussian part vanishes, while singular dependence in the holonomy kernel yields finite contributions. The relevant saddle is a constrained boundary saddle on the aligned branch. A non-polynomial toy model $V_{\\rm toy}(B) = -\\log\\cosh B$ exactly reproduces the $D$-channel contribution $-2d$, interpreted geometrically as Wishart–Stiefel entropy tied to an emergent 4D geometry in $\\mathbb{R}^d$.
\n\n
本文研究高维极限（large-$d$）下 $N=2$ 的 BFSS/BMN 矩阵量子力学在 Gaussian 区域的行为，提出一种径向端点表述（radial endpoint formulation），通过积分掉体模、规范和纵向自由度，得到由整体势（holonomy potential）控制的横向端点变量的有效理论。作者证明该平面端点表述与规范投影配分函数的角向 Molien–Weyl 描述在相差一个普适因子的意义下等价，从而可从 Molien–Weyl 结果导出低温展开。在连续极限下，二次项系数分解为 Gaussian 项、$D$-通道和 $\\beta$-通道；其中平凡 Gaussian 项消失，而整体核（holonomy kernel）通过对端点 Gaussian 宽度和各向异性耦合的奇异依赖产生有限贡献。相关鞍点是位于对齐分支（aligned branch）上的约束边界鞍点，而非无约束临界点。任何有限阶多项式截断的横向展开在连续极限下均平凡。作者引入非多项式玩具模型 $V_{\\rm toy}(B) = -\\log\\cosh B$，精确复现了 $D$-通道贡献 $-2d$，并将其几何诠释为与嵌入 $\\mathbb{R}^d$ 中的涌现四维几何相关的 Wishart–Stiefel 熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand the structure of singlet states and thermal properties in the Gaussian regime of BFSS/BMN matrix models at large dimensionality $d$ and small $N=2$, where traditional methods like perturbation theory or direct lattice simulation become inefficient. By focusing on the planar (large-$N$ inspired but here fixed $N=2$) endpoint degrees of freedom, the authors aim to isolate the transverse dynamics governed by holonomy effects, which encode non-trivial continuum physics obscured in naive Gaussian approximations. They seek a geometric interpretation of the $D$-channel contribution, which survives the continuum limit and hints at emergent spacetime structure.
\n\n
动机在于理解在高维 $d$ 和小 $N=2$ 极限下，BFSS/BMN 矩阵模型在 Gaussian 区域中单态（singlet）结构与热力学性质。在此区域，传统方法如微扰论或直接格点模拟效率低下。作者聚焦于平面（受大-$N$ 启发但此处固定 $N=2$）端点自由度，旨在分离出由整体效应（holonomy effects）主导的横向动力学——这些效应编码了在朴素 Gaussian 近似中被掩盖的非平凡连续极限物理。他们进一步寻求对 $D$-通道贡献的几何诠释，该贡献在连续极限下存留，并暗示了涌现时空结构的存在。

Method: The method involves constructing a radial endpoint formulation by integrating out all non-endpoint degrees of freedom (bulk matrices, gauge fields, longitudinal modes) in the lattice-regularized BFSS/BMN path integral, resulting in an effective action for transverse endpoint variables $B_i$ with a non-local holonomy potential $V_{\\rm hol}(B)$. The authors relate this to the Molien–Weyl formula for the $SU(2)$-invariant partition function via group integration, showing equivalence up to a spectator factor. They compute the low-temperature expansion by extracting the quadratic coefficient from the Molien–Weyl result. The continuum limit is analyzed by examining the scaling of the Gaussian width and coupling anisotropy, revealing singular behavior in the holonomy kernel. Saddle-point analysis identifies a constrained boundary saddle on the aligned branch. Finally, a solvable non-polynomial toy potential $V_{\\rm toy}(B) = -\\log\\cosh B$ is introduced to model the full transverse interaction and capture the exact $D$-channel.
\n\n
方法包括在格点正则化的 BFSS/BMN 路径积分中构建径向端点表述：通过积分掉所有非端点自由度（体矩阵、规范场、纵向模），得到仅含横向端点变量 $B_i$ 的有效作用量，其势能为非局域的整体势 $V_{\\rm hol}(B)$。作者通过群积分将此表述与 $SU(2)$ 不变配分函数的 Molien–Weyl 公式关联，证明二者在相差一个旁观因子（spectator factor）下等价。他们从 Molien–Weyl 结果中提取二次项系数，获得低温展开。通过分析 Gaussian 宽度与耦合各向异性的标度行为，研究连续极限，揭示整体核中的奇异依赖。鞍点分析识别出位于对齐分支上的约束边界鞍点。最后引入可解的非多项式玩具势 $V_{\\rm toy}(B) = -\\log\\cosh B$，以建模完整的横向相互作用并精确捕捉 $D$-通道。

Result: Key results include: (1) Equivalence between the radial endpoint formulation and the Molien–Weyl description of the gauge-invariant partition function up to a universal factor; (2) The quadratic coefficient in the low-temperature expansion is $d(d+1)/2$, counting Gaussian singlet states; (3) In the continuum limit, this coefficient decomposes as $\\frac{d(d+1)}{2} \\to 0 + (-2d) + \\text{(}\\beta\\text{-channel)}$, where the naive Gaussian term vanishes, the $D$-channel yields $-2d$, and the $\\beta$-channel remains; (4) The physical saddle is a constrained boundary saddle, not a standard critical point; (5) The toy model $V_{\\rm toy}(B) = -\\log\\cosh B$ exactly reproduces the $-2d$ $D$-channel contribution; (6) This $D$-channel is interpreted as Wishart–Stiefel entropy associated with an emergent four-dimensional geometry embedded in $\\mathbb{R}^d$.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 径向端点表述与规范不变配分函数的 Molien–Weyl 描述在相差一个普适因子下等价；(2) 低温展开中的二次项系数为 $d(d+1)/2$，用于计数 Gaussian 单态；(3) 在连续极限下，该系数分解为 $\\frac{d(d+1)}{2} \\to 0 + (-2d) + \\text{(}\\beta\\text{-通道)}$，其中朴素 Gaussian 项消失，$D$-通道贡献为 $-2d$，$\\beta$-通道保留；(4) 物理鞍点是约束边界鞍点，而非标准临界点；(5) 玩具模型 $V_{\\rm toy}(B) = -\\log\\cosh B$ 精确复现了 $-2d$ 的 $D$-通道贡献；(6) 该 $D$-通道被诠释为与嵌入 $\\mathbb{R}^d$ 中的涌现四维几何相关的 Wishart–Stiefel 熵。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that non-perturbative holonomy effects in the endpoint formulation of matrix quantum mechanics generate finite continuum contributions missed by naive Gaussian analysis. The $D$-channel arises from the singular structure of the holonomy potential and is geometrically interpretable as entropy of an emergent 4D Wishart–Stiefel manifold within $\\mathbb{R}^d$. This suggests that even in the simple $N=2$, large-$d$ Gaussian sector, spacetime geometry can emerge from collective matrix dynamics through constrained boundary saddles and non-polynomial interactions. The toy model provides a solvable framework capturing essential non-Gaussian physics relevant to continuum limits in holographic matrix models.
\n\n
本文表明，在矩阵量子力学的端点表述中，非微扰的整体效应产生了朴素 Gaussian 分析所遗漏的有限连续极限贡献。$D$-通道源于整体势的奇异结构，并可几何地诠释为嵌入 $\\mathbb{R}^d$ 中的涌现四维 Wishart–Stiefel 流形的熵。这表明，即使在简单的 $N=2$、大-$d$ Gaussian 区域，时空几何也能通过约束边界鞍点和非多项式相互作用从矩阵集体动力学中涌现。该玩具模型提供了一个可解框架，捕捉了全息矩阵模型连续极限中关键的非 Gaussian 物理。

Abstract: We study the \(N=2\), large--\(d\) sector of BFSS/BMN-type matrix quantum mechanics on the lattice in the Gaussian regime. We develop a radial endpoint formulation in which the bulk, gauge, and longitudinal degrees of freedom are integrated out, leaving transverse endpoint variables governed by an effective holonomy potential. We show that this planar endpoint formulation is equivalent to the angular Molien--Weyl description of the gauge-projected partition function, up to a universal spectator factor. This relation allows the low-temperature expansion of the endpoint partition function to be obtained from the Molien--Weyl result, whose quadratic coefficient \(d(d+1)/2\) counts Gaussian singlet states above the vacuum.
  We then analyze the continuum limit of the quadratic coefficient and show that it separates into a Gaussian contribution, a \(D\)-channel, and a \(β\)-channel. The naive Gaussian term becomes trivial, while the exact holonomy kernel generates finite continuum contributions through singular dependence on the endpoint Gaussian width and anisotropic coupling.
  We then study the geometry of the holonomy potential and show that its relevant saddle is a constrained boundary saddle on the aligned branch, rather than an unconstrained critical point. The associated transverse expansion captures the local saddle geometry, but any finite polynomial truncation has a trivial continuum limit. Finally, we introduce a non-polynomial toy model based on \(V_{\rm toy}(B)=-\log\cosh B\), which provides a completion of the transverse expansion and reproduces exactly the continuum \(D\)-channel contribution \(-2d\). This prepares the geometric interpretation of the \(D\)-channel as a Wishart--Stiefel entropy associated with an emergent four-dimensional geometry embedded \(\mathbb R^d\) in the endpoint formulation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the desire to simplify the computationally intensive Khovanov-Rozansky homology framework, particularly its matrix factorization formulation, by connecting it to more elementary combinatorial methods like the Kauffman-Khovanov cycle calculus. Demonstrating consistency for $N=2$ provides a crucial check on the validity of this new reduction technique for bipartite links.

动机源于希望简化计算上非常复杂的Khovanov-Rozansky同调框架（尤其是其矩阵因子化表述），通过将其与更初等的组合方法（如Kauffman-Khovanov圈演算）联系起来。在 $N=2$ 情况下验证一致性，为这种针对二分链环的新约化技术的有效性提供了关键检验。

Method: The authors compare the hypercube structures underlying the two computational approaches. They analyze how the full $2^2$-hypercube of the standard Kauffman-Khovanov construction for $N=2$ is reduced or "shrinked" to a smaller 3-hypercube when the link is bipartite, thereby showing that the simplified cycle calculus reproduces the correct Khovanov polynomial in this specific case.
\n\n
作者比较了两种计算方法所依赖的超立方体结构。他们分析了当链环为二分时，标准Kauffman-Khovanov构造中完整的 $2^2$-超立方体如何被约化或“收缩”为一个更小的3-超立方体，从而表明这种简化的圈演算在此特定情形下能复现正确的Khovanov多项式。

Result: The paper successfully demonstrates that the reduction of the Khovanov-Rozansky formalism to a bipartite cycle calculus yields results consistent with the established $N=2$ Khovanov homology. The key result is the explicit description of the shrinking from the $2^2$-hypercube to the 3-hypercube, confirming the validity of the simplified method for this class of links.
\n\n
本文成功证明了将Khovanov-Rozansky形式体系约化为二分圈演算所得结果与已建立的 $N=2$ Khovanov同调一致。核心结果是对从 $2^2$-超立方体到3-超立方体收缩过程的显式描述，确认了该简化方法对这类链环的有效性。

Conclusion: The consistency check for $N=2$ supports the broader applicability of the newly proposed elementary cycle calculus as a viable alternative to the full Khovanov-Rozansky matrix factorization for bipartite links. This paves the way for further generalizations and simpler computations in knot homology theories.
\n\n
对 $N=2$ 的一致性检验支持了新提出的初等圈演算在二分链环情形下作为完整Khovanov-Rozansky矩阵因子化方法的可行替代方案具有更广泛的适用性。这为纽结同调理论中的进一步推广和更简化的计算铺平了道路。

Abstract: Recently, for a limited class for bipartite links, the complicated Khovanov-Rozansky matrix factorization technique was reduced to an analogue of elementary Kauffman-Khovanov cycle calculus for an arbitrary $N$. In this note, we demonstrate the consistency of such reduction with the computation of the bipartite Khovanov polynomials for $N=2$. Namely, we explain how the Kauffman-Khovanov $2^2$-hypercube is shrinked to the bipartite 3-hypercube.

</details>


### [87] [Null Strings Gauged and Reloaded, I: Null Strings Have Carroll-Weyl Gauge Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25817)
*M. M. Sheikh-Jabbari,H. Yavartanoo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper resolves a consistency issue in null string theory by identifying its underlying Carrollian geometric structure, revealing two distinct Carroll-Weyl scaling symmetries (unlike the single Weyl symmetry of tensile strings). The authors construct a new null string action invariant under both Carroll-Weyl scalings and 2D diffeomorphisms; the traditional Isberg-Lindström-Sundborg-Theodoridis (ILST) action emerges after gauge-fixing one of these scalings, with the residual symmetry explaining a previously overlooked partial-gauge symmetry—thus enabling a consistent quantization framework.
\n\n
本文通过识别零弦理论背后的Carroll几何结构，解决了其一致性问题，揭示出两种不同的Carroll-Weyl标度对称性（而普通张力弦世界面仅有一种Weyl对称性）。作者构建了一个在两种Carroll-Weyl标度和二维微分同胚下不变的新零弦作用量；传统的Isberg-Lindström-Sundborg-Theodoridis (ILST) 作用量是在固定其中一个标度后得到的，其剩余对称性恰好解释了先前被忽视的部分规范对称性——从而为零弦的一致量子化铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from a recent observation that the standard ILST action for null strings possesses an unaccounted partial-gauge symmetry, casting doubt on the validity of existing analyses. This inconsistency suggests that the conventional formulation may miss essential geometric structures inherent to Carrollian worldsheets, which arise in the tensionless limit of string theory. The authors aim to clarify the geometric origin of this symmetry and establish a more robust foundation for null string dynamics compatible with quantization.
\n\n
动机源于最近的一项观察：标准的ILST零弦作用量具有一个未被考虑的部分规范对称性，这引发了对现有分析有效性的质疑。这种不一致性表明，传统表述可能忽略了Carroll世界面所固有的基本几何结构——这些结构出现在弦论的无张力极限中。作者旨在澄清该对称性的几何起源，并建立一个与量子化相容的、更稳健的零弦动力学基础。

Method: The authors leverage the intrinsic geometry of Carrollian manifolds, which admit two independent Weyl scaling transformations—termed Carroll-Weyl scalings—unlike Lorentzian worldsheets that support only one. They postulate a new null string action principle requiring invariance under both Carroll-Weyl scalings and 2D diffeomorphisms as local gauge symmetries. By systematically gauge-fixing one of the two Carroll-Weyl symmetries, they recover the ILST action and demonstrate that the leftover gauge freedom corresponds exactly to the previously identified partial-gauge symmetry.
\n\n
作者利用Carroll流形的内蕴几何结构，该结构允许两种独立的Weyl标度变换（称为Carroll-Weyl标度），而Lorentz型世界面仅支持一种。他们提出一个新的零弦作用量原理，要求其在两种Carroll-Weyl标度和二维微分同胚下均具有局域规范对称性。通过系统地固定两个Carroll-Weyl对称性之一，他们复现了ILST作用量，并证明剩余的规范自由度恰好对应于先前发现的部分规范对称性。

Result: The key result is the construction of a new, geometrically motivated null string action invariant under two Carroll-Weyl scalings and 2D diffeomorphisms. The established link shows that the ILST action is a gauge-fixed version of this more symmetric action, and its residual symmetry explains the overlooked partial-gauge invariance. This clarifies the Carroll-geometric origin of the symmetry and resolves the apparent inconsistency in prior formulations.
\n\n
主要结果是构建了一个新的、具有几何动机的零弦作用量，该作用量在两种Carroll-Weyl标度和二维微分同胚下不变。所建立的联系表明，ILST作用量是这一更具对称性的作用量的规范固定形式，其剩余对称性解释了被忽视的部分规范不变性。这澄清了该对称性的Carroll几何起源，并解决了先前表述中的明显不一致性。

Conclusion: The paper concludes that null string theory must be formulated on a Carrollian worldsheet with two independent Carroll-Weyl scaling symmetries to ensure consistency. The traditional ILST action is valid only as a partially gauge-fixed theory, and its hidden symmetry originates from the unfixed Carroll-Weyl redundancy. This refined understanding provides a solid geometric foundation for future efforts toward the consistent quantization of null strings.
\n\n
本文得出结论：为确保一致性，零弦理论必须在具有两种独立Carroll-Weyl标度对称性的Carroll世界面上表述。传统的ILST作用量仅作为部分规范固定理论有效，其隐藏对称性源于未固定的Carroll-Weyl冗余。这一更精细的理解为未来实现零弦一致量子化的努力提供了坚实的几何基础。

Abstract: Null strings, strings with Carrollian worldsheets, are traditionally described by the Isberg-Lindström-Sundborg-Theodoridis (ILST) action, which is obtained via a tensionless limit of standard tensile strings. In a recent work, we observed that the ILST action enjoys an overlooked partial-gauge symmetry whose existence calls into question the consistency of {standard} null strings analysis found in the literature. In this paper, we show that the Carrollian geometry provides us with two Weyl scaling options, in contrast to a single Weyl scaling available for the ordinary tensile string worldsheet. Defining the null string theory by the action that realizes the two Carroll-Weyl scalings as well as the 2D diffeomorphisms as local (gauge) symmetries, we construct the new null string action. We show that the ILST action is obtained after fixing one of the two Carroll-Weyl scalings of the action that we construct, and that the residual part of this symmetry is precisely the overlooked partial-gauge symmetry. We hence clarify the Carroll-geometric origin of the overlooked symmetry and pave the way for a consistent quantization of null strings.

</details>


### [88] [Hard cutoff and gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25827)
*V. Branchina,F. Contino,R. Gandolfo,A. Pernace*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper resolves the apparent conflict between Wilsonian effective field theory with a hard UV cutoff $Λ$ and gauge invariance by constructing a gauge-invariant regularization scheme for scalar and fermionic QED, reproducing the Euler-Heisenberg effective action up to $Λ$-suppressed corrections. This provides a foundation for a new Wilsonian renormalization group approach compatible with gauge symmetry.
\n\n
本文通过为标量和费米子QED构建一种规范不变的正则化方案，解决了Wilson有效场论中硬紫外截断$Λ$与规范不变性之间的表面冲突，在$Λ$压制修正项范围内重现了Euler-Heisenberg有效作用量。这为一种与规范对称性兼容的新Wilsonian重整化群方法奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard implementations of a hard momentum cutoff $Λ$ in gauge theories explicitly break gauge invariance, creating tension with the Wilsonian EFT framework where $Λ$ is interpreted as a physical boundary of validity. This undermines the consistent application of Wilsonian renormalization group (RG) methods to gauge theories. The authors aim to reconcile these two pillars of quantum field theory by developing a cutoff procedure that preserves gauge symmetry at the quantum level.
\n\n
在规范理论中，标准的硬动量截断$Λ$实现会显式破坏规范不变性，这与Wilsonian EFT框架（其中$Λ$被解释为理论有效性的物理边界）产生张力。这阻碍了Wilsonian重整化群（RG）方法在规范理论中的一致应用。作者旨在通过开发一种在量子层面保持规范对称性的截断程序，来调和量子场论的这两个支柱。

Method: The authors introduce a gauge-invariant Wilsonian hard cutoff $Λ$ by modifying the functional integral measure or propagators in a way that respects the background gauge symmetry. They apply this method to compute the one-loop effective action for both scalar and fermionic QED in a constant electromagnetic background field, comparing the result to the standard Euler-Heisenberg action derived via proper-time regularization. The construction is inspired by the spirit of the Wegner-Houghton RG formalism, which integrates out high-momentum modes while attempting to maintain symmetries.
\n\n
作者通过以尊重背景规范对称性的方式修改泛函积分测度或传播子，引入了一种规范不变的Wilsonian硬截断$Λ$。他们将此方法应用于计算标量和费米子QED在恒定电磁背景场中的一圈有效作用量，并将其结果与通过proper-time正则化导出的标准Euler-Heisenberg作用量进行比较。该构造受Wegner-Houghton RG形式的精神启发，后者在积分掉高动量模式的同时尝试保持对称性。

Result: For both scalar and fermionic QED, the gauge-invariant cutoff procedure reproduces the standard Euler-Heisenberg effective Lagrangian, up to additional terms that are suppressed by powers of the cutoff scale $Λ$. These novel correction terms exhibit periodicity in the inverse background field strength ($1/F$) and could become significant when the background field probes energy scales comparable to $Λ$, i.e., when $F \\sim Λ^2$.
\n\n
对于标量和费米子QED，这种规范不变截断程序重现了标准的Euler-Heisenberg有效拉氏量，但附加了被截断能标$Λ$的幂次所压制的额外项。这些新颖的修正项在背景场强度的倒数（$1/F$）中表现出周期性，当背景场探测的能量尺度与$Λ$相当时（即$F \\sim Λ^2$），这些项可能变得显著。

Conclusion: The work demonstrates that it is possible to implement a physical Wilsonian hard UV cutoff $Λ$ in gauge theories without violating gauge invariance at the quantum level. This achievement paves the way for a new formulation of the Wilsonian renormalization group program for gauge theories, one that is more aligned with the original integrating-out philosophy of Wilson and Wegner-Houghton, while maintaining the crucial symmetry principle of gauge invariance.
\n\n
本工作证明了在规范理论中可以在不破坏量子层面规范不变性的情况下实施一个物理的Wilsonian硬紫外截断$Λ$。这一成就为规范理论的Wilsonian重整化群方案开辟了一条新路径，该方案更符合Wilson和Wegner-Houghton原始的“积分掉”哲学，同时保持了规范不变性这一关键对称性原理。

Abstract: According to usual calculations, the use of a hard cutoff $Λ$ in gauge theories leads to a violation of gauge invariance. This seems to generate a tension between gauge theories and the Wilsonian effective field theory (EFT) paradigm, where $Λ$ has the physical meaning of ultimate scale of the theory, the scale above which the latter has to be replaced by its UV completion. In the present work, considering the Euler-Heisenberg correction to the free Maxwell action, we present a way to introduce the Wilsonian hard UV cutoff $Λ$ that preserves gauge invariance at the quantum level. For both scalar and fermionic QED, we recover the well-known Euler-Heisenberg result obtained within proper-time regularization, apart from terms that are generically cutoff-suppressed. These terms, periodic in the inverse background field, might become relevant in regimes where the latter probes scales not much smaller than $Λ$. On the theoretical side, the methods developed in the present work represent a first step towards a new (closer in spirit to the Wegner-Houghton construction) realization of the Wilsonian renormalization group program in gauge theories.

</details>

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper proves that $\mathcal{N}=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory retains its superconformal symmetry at the quantum level by using the $\mathcal{N}=1$ superfield formalism, showing that no higher-order quantum corrections arise and that $\mathcal{N}=1$ computations suffice to ensure full $\mathcal{N}=4$ supersymmetry.

该论文利用$\mathcal{N}=1$超场形式证明了$\mathcal{N}=4$超杨-米尔斯理论在量子层面保持其超共形对称性，表明不存在高阶量子修正，且$\mathcal{N}=1$的计算足以保证完整的$\mathcal{N}=4$超对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to rigorously establish whether the classical superconformal symmetry of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory survives quantization, which is nontrivial due to potential anomalies or radiative corrections that could break the symmetry. Demonstrating this using a minimal $\mathcal{N}=1$ framework simplifies the analysis while still capturing the full symmetry structure.
