动机在于严格验证$\mathcal{N}=4$超杨-米尔斯理论的经典超共形对称性是否在量子化后依然保持，这并非显然，因为可能存在反常或辐射修正破坏该对称性。通过最小化的$\mathcal{N}=1$框架进行证明，可在简化分析的同时完整捕捉全部对称性结构。

Method: The authors employ the $\mathcal{N}=1$ superfield formalism to reformulate $\mathcal{N}=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory, leveraging the fact that the latter can be decomposed into one $\mathcal{N}=1$ vector multiplet and three chiral multiplets. They analyze the quantum effective action and use non-renormalization theorems inherent to $\mathcal{N}=1$ supersymmetry to argue that no perturbative or non-perturbative corrections can break the superconformal invariance.
\n\n
作者采用$\mathcal{N}=1$超场形式将$\mathcal{N}=4$超杨-米尔斯理论重新表述为一个$\mathcal{N}=1$矢量多重态和三个手征多重态的组合。他们分析量子有效作用量，并利用$\mathcal{N}=1$超对称性所固有的不可重整化定理，论证不存在任何微扰或非微扰修正能够破坏超共形不变性。

Result: The result is that the superconformal symmetry of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super-Yang-Mills remains exact at the quantum level; the $\mathcal{N}=1$ calculation fully constrains the theory, and no higher-loop or instanton corrections modify the symmetry structure. This confirms the non-renormalization of the superconformal current multiplet.
\n\n
结果表明，$\mathcal{N}=4$超杨-米尔斯理论的超共形对称性在量子层面严格保持；$\mathcal{N}=1$的计算已完全约束该理论，且不存在高圈图或瞬子修正改变其对称性结构。这证实了超共形流多重态的不可重整性。

Conclusion: The paper concludes that $\mathcal{N}=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory is exactly superconformal in the quantum regime, and that working within the simpler $\mathcal{N}=1$ superfield framework is sufficient to establish this property without needing to perform the more complex full $\mathcal{N}=4$ superspace calculations.
\n\n
论文得出结论：$\mathcal{N}=4$超杨-米尔斯理论在量子区域严格保持超共形性，且在更简单的$\mathcal{N}=1$超场框架内工作已足以确立这一性质，无需进行更复杂的完整$\mathcal{N}=4$超空间计算。

Abstract: Using the $\mathcal{N}=1$ superfield formalism, we prove that the superconformal symmetry of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory is preserved in the quantum theory. We demonstrate that the $\mathcal{N}=1$ calculation is sufficient to guarantee the full $\mathcal{N}=4$ supersymmetry, and show that the results are exact without receiving any higher-order corrections.

</details>


### [90] [Krylov Complexity for Plane Wave Matrix Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26055)
*Dibakar Roychowdhury*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper investigates Krylov complexity in the BMN Plane Wave Matrix Model under large mass deformation, analyzing both state and operator growth via consistent reductions of $N=3$ and $N=4$ representations. It finds universal linear scaling of Lanczos coefficients with the mass parameter and identifies identical quadratic time corrections to early-time Krylov complexity in both formulations.
\n\n
本文研究了在大质量形变下BMN平面波矩阵模型中的Krylov复杂度，通过对$N=3$和$N=4$表示的系统约化，分析了Krylov态复杂度和算符增长。研究发现Lanczos系数与质量参数呈普适的线性标度关系，并且两种Krylov复杂度定义在早期时间演化中均出现相同阶次的二次时间修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding quantum chaos and information scrambling in holographic systems requires refined diagnostics beyond conventional out-of-time-order correlators. Krylov complexity has emerged as a promising tool to characterize operator and state evolution in quantum many-body systems. The BMN Plane Wave Matrix Model provides a tractable setting with tunable mass deformation, enabling controlled study of how massive terms influence Krylov complexity—a question relevant to both quantum gravity and thermalization dynamics.
\n\n
理解全息系统中的量子混沌和信息混杂需要超越传统OTOC（out-of-time-order correlators）的精细诊断工具。Krylov复杂度已成为刻画量子多体系统中算符与态演化的有力手段。BMN平面波矩阵模型提供了一个具有可调质量形变的可处理框架，使得研究质量项如何影响Krylov复杂度成为可能——这一问题对量子引力和热化动力学均具重要意义。

Method: The authors perform Hamiltonian analyses on consistent truncations of the BMN matrix model for $N=3$ and $N=4$, constructing Krylov bases for both states and operators. They compute the Lanczos algorithm coefficients—denoted $b_n$—by iteratively orthogonalizing the time-evolved operators or states under the deformed Hamiltonian $H = H_0 + \mu^2 V$, where $\mu$ is the mass deformation parameter. The asymptotic behavior of $b_n$ and early-time expansion of Krylov complexity $C_K(t)$ are then extracted analytically.
\n\n
作者对BMN矩阵模型在$N=3$和$N=4$情形下进行一致截断，并分别针对态和算符构建Krylov基。通过Lanczos算法，在形变哈密顿量$H = H_0 + \mu^2 V$（其中$\mu$为质量形变参数）作用下对时间演化算符或态进行迭代正交化，计算出Lanczos系数$b_n$。随后解析提取$b_n$的渐近行为及Krylov复杂度$C_K(t)$的早期时间展开。

Result: Both Krylov state complexity and operator complexity exhibit Lanczos coefficients that scale linearly with the mass deformation parameter: $b_n \sim \mu n$. The early-time Krylov complexity shows a universal quadratic correction: $C_K(t) \approx 1 + c_2 (\mu t)^2 + \cdots$, with the same functional form appearing in both state and operator formulations. This indicates that massive deformations affect the two notions of complexity identically at leading nontrivial order in time.
\n\n
Krylov态复杂度与算符复杂度均表现出Lanczos系数与质量形变参数呈线性标度：$b_n \sim \mu n$。早期时间的Krylov复杂度呈现出普适的二次修正：$C_K(t) \approx 1 + c_2 (\mu t)^2 + \cdots$，且该形式在态和算符两种定义下完全一致。这表明质量形变在时间展开的最低非平凡阶上对两种复杂度概念产生相同影响。

Conclusion: The universality of linear Lanczos scaling and matching early-time corrections across state and operator Krylov complexity suggests a robust structural response of the BMN matrix model to mass deformation. This reinforces Krylov complexity as a coherent diagnostic of quantum dynamics in deformed holographic theories and hints at deeper connections between operator growth, state evolution, and symmetry breaking induced by relevant deformations.
\n\n
Lanczos系数的线性标度普适性以及态与算符Krylov复杂度在早期时间修正上的一致性，表明BMN矩阵模型对质量形变具有稳健的结构性响应。这进一步确立了Krylov复杂度作为刻画形变全息理论中量子动力学的有效诊断工具，并暗示算符增长、态演化与由相关形变诱导的对称性破缺之间可能存在更深层的联系。

Abstract: We study Krylov complexity in BMN Plane Wave Matrix Model at large mass deformation. We consider various consistent reductions of the matrix model that allow us to perform a Hamiltonian analysis which leads to different notions of the Krylov complexity. In the first part of the paper, we study the Krylov state complexity considering systematic reduction of $N=3$ and $N=4$ representations of the matrix model, which reveals a universal characteristic scaling for the Lanczos coefficients and fix them completely in terms of the mass deformation parameter. In the second part of the paper, we study the Krylov operator growth in the matrix model and compute the corresponding Lanczos coefficients. In both cases, we observe a \emph{linear} scaling of Lanczos coefficients with the mass parameter. The early time growth in Krylov complexity receives quadratic correction due to the presence of the massive deformation in the matrix model. Our analysis reveals that such massive corrections appear at same order in time for both the notion of the Krylov complexity.

</details>


### [91] [On Perturbatively Dressed Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26077)
*Clifford Cheung,Allic Sivaramakrishnan,Jordan Wilson-Gerow,Lihang Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper computes perturbative matrix elements of gravitationally and electromagnetically dressed local operators, revealing that dressing induces universal kinematic singularities and is mathematically equivalent to a dynamical gauge-fixing procedure based on a geodesic fluid.
\n\n
本文计算了引力和电磁场中“穿衣”（dressed）局域算符的微扰矩阵元，揭示出穿衣过程会普遍诱导运动学奇点，并且在数学上等价于一种基于测地流体的动态规范固定程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Local observables in gravity are inherently ill-defined due to diffeomorphism invariance; physical meaning must be assigned relationally via reference frames (e.g., clocks, rulers, or asymptotic structure). While this principle is well-established theoretically, explicit computations of such relational ("dressed") observables—especially beyond leading order or including radiative modes—are lacking. The authors aim to fill this gap by providing concrete perturbative calculations in both electrodynamics and general relativity.
\n\n
由于微分同胚不变性，引力中的局域可观测量本质上是未定义的；其物理意义必须通过参考系（如时钟、尺子或渐近结构）关系性地赋予。尽管这一原理在理论上已广为人知，但对这类关系性（“穿衣”）可观测量的具体计算——尤其是超出领头阶或包含辐射模的情形——仍十分稀缺。作者旨在通过在电动力学和广义相对论中提供具体的微扰计算来填补这一空白。

Method: The authors compute perturbative S-matrix elements of local operators dressed by soft photon and graviton clouds, incorporating both potential (Coulomb-like) and radiative (asymptotic) modes. They employ relational observables defined with respect to a dynamical reference frame modeled as a pressureless geodesic fluid. Crucially, they demonstrate that this dressing procedure is mathematically equivalent to imposing a temporal gauge condition where the gauge-fixing vector field aligns with the fluid’s four-velocity.
\n\n
作者计算了由软光子和引力子云“穿衣”的局域算符的微扰S矩阵元，同时包含势场（类库仑）模和辐射（渐近）模。他们采用相对于一个无压测地流体所建模的动力学参考系定义的关系性可观测量。关键的是，他们证明这种穿衣过程在数学上等价于施加一种时间规范条件，其中规范固定矢量场与流体的四速度对齐。

Result: The dressed matrix elements exhibit universal kinematic singularities—such as $1/\\omega$ poles for soft radiation—that are absent in undressed correlators. These singularities arise directly from the dressing and significantly alter the structure of observables. Moreover, the equivalence between dressing and gauge fixing is explicitly established through the construction of a dynamical temporal gauge tied to a geodesic fluid, offering a geometric interpretation of the dressing cloud.
\n\n
穿衣后的矩阵元展现出普适的运动学奇点——例如软辐射中的$1/\\omega$极点——这些奇点在未穿衣关联函数中并不存在。这些奇点直接源于穿衣过程，并显著改变可观测量的结构。此外，通过构造一个与测地流体绑定的动力学时间规范，作者明确建立了穿衣与规范固定的等价性，为穿衣云提供了几何诠释。

Conclusion: Gravitational (and electromagnetic) dressing is not a mere technical detail but a physically essential procedure that reshapes observables through universal infrared structures. The equivalence with dynamical gauge fixing provides a concrete framework for defining relational observables in gauge theories and gravity, bridging formal insights with explicit computational tools.
\n\n
引力（及电磁）穿衣并非仅仅是技术细节，而是一个物理上必不可少的过程，它通过普适的红外结构重塑可观测量。其与动力学规范固定的等价性为在规范理论和引力中定义关系性可观测量提供了具体框架，从而将形式理论洞见与显式计算工具联系起来。

Abstract: A central lesson of gravity is that local observables are ill-defined. Coordinates themselves are a redundancy of description, so any particular point in spacetime is only meaningful once defined relationally by clocks, rulers, or asymptotic data. Despite extensive formal work on this subject, explicit calculations of the resulting gravitationally-dressed observables are more scarce. In this paper we perturbatively compute dressed matrix elements of local operators in electrodynamics and general relativity, including both potential and radiative photons and gravitons. Our expressions indicate that dressing is not ornamental: it universally induces kinematic singularities that can substantively reshape observables. We further show how dressing is mathematically equivalent to gauge fixing, as demonstrated by a dynamical temporal gauge in which the gauge-fixing vector is itself a geodesic fluid.

</details>


### [92] [Intersecting Surface Operators in 6d Holomorphic Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26080)
*Meer Ashwinkumar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper investigates intersecting surface operators in 6d holomorphic Chern-Simons and BF theories, revealing local operators at their intersections that resemble rational $R$-matrices. These operators are argued to satisfy Yang-Baxter-type relations, and the associated chiral algebra coproduct is derived from the OPE. In the BF case on twistor space, the result connects to quantum integrable structures in self-dual 4d Yang-Mills theory.
\n\n
本文研究了六维全纯Chern-Simons理论和BF理论中的相交表面算子，在其交点处发现了一类形如有理$R$-矩阵的局域算子。这些算子被论证满足Yang-Baxter型关系，并从算子乘积展开（OPE）中导出了相关手征代数的余积结构。在twistor空间上的BF理论情形下，该结果与四维自对偶Yang-Mills理论中预期的量子可积结构相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the conjectured deep connection between topological/holomorphic quantum field theories in higher dimensions and quantum integrable systems. Specifically, Costello's proposal suggests that 6d holomorphic Chern-Simons theory on $\mathbb{C}^3$ can engineer rational solutions to the Yang-Baxter equation via intersecting surface defects. This work aims to explicitly compute these structures and extend the analysis to 6d holomorphic BF theory, which is linked to 4d self-dual Yang-Mills via the twistor correspondence.
\n\n
动机源于高维拓扑/全纯量子场论与量子可积系统之间被猜想存在的深刻联系。特别是，Costello提出六维全纯Chern-Simons理论在$\mathbb{C}^3$上可通过相交的表面缺陷构造Yang-Baxter方程的有理解。本文旨在显式计算这些结构，并将分析推广到六维全纯BF理论，后者通过twistor对应与四维自对偶Yang-Mills理论相关联。

Method: The authors compute correlation functions of intersecting surface operators in 6d holomorphic Chern-Simons theory on $\mathbb{C}^3$ to extract the local operator at their intersection. They analyze its form and compare it to the quasi-classical expansion of a rational $R$-matrix. The Operator Product Expansion (OPE) of the surface operators is used to derive the coproduct of the supported chiral algebra. The same computational strategy is applied to 6d holomorphic BF theory, with a subsequent interpretation of the result in the context of twistor space and 4d self-dual Yang-Mills theory.
\n\n
作者计算了$\mathbb{C}^3$上六维全纯Chern-Simons理论中相交表面算子的关联函数，以提取其交点处的局域算子。他们分析该算子的形式，并将其与有理$R$-矩阵的准经典展开进行比较。利用表面算子的算子乘积展开（OPE）导出其所支撑的手征代数的余积。同样的计算策略被应用于六维全纯BF理论，并在twistor空间及四维自对偶Yang-Mills理论的框架下对结果进行诠释。

Result: The main results are: (1) The explicit computation of a local operator at the intersection of surface operators in 6d holomorphic Chern-Simons theory, whose form matches the leading nontrivial term of a rational $R$-matrix. (2) Evidence that this operator satisfies a Yang-Baxter-type relation. (3) A derivation of the coproduct for the chiral algebra from the OPE of surface operators. (4) The analogous computation in 6d holomorphic BF theory, yielding a similar $R$-matrix-like operator, which is proposed as a building block for integrability in 4d self-dual Yang-Mills on twistor space.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 在六维全纯Chern-Simons理论中，显式计算出相交表面算子交点处的一个局域算子，其形式与有理$R$-矩阵的首阶非平凡项一致。(2) 提供了该算子满足Yang-Baxter型关系的证据。(3) 从表面算子的OPE中导出了手征代数的余积。(4) 在六维全纯BF理论中进行了类似计算，得到了一个类似的$R$-矩阵型算子，并提出该算子是twistor空间上四维自对偶Yang-Mills理论中可积性的基本构件。

Conclusion: The paper provides concrete evidence supporting the program that links 6d holomorphic gauge theories with quantum integrable systems. By explicitly constructing $R$-matrix-like operators from intersecting defects and deriving the associated chiral algebraic structure, it solidifies the connection between the physics of surface operators and the mathematical framework of integrability. The extension to BF theory further suggests a pathway to understand quantum integrable structures in physically relevant 4d gauge theories like self-dual Yang-Mills.
\n\n
本文为“六维全纯规范理论与量子可积系统相联系”这一纲领提供了具体证据。通过从相交缺陷中显式构造出$R$-矩阵型算子并导出相关的手征代数结构，它巩固了表面算子物理与可积性数学框架之间的联系。对BF理论的推广进一步指明了一条理解四维物理规范理论（如自对偶Yang-Mills理论）中量子可积结构的途径。

Abstract: We study intersecting surface operators in 6d holomorphic field theories with the aim of unraveling associated quantum integrable structures. We first study the intersections of surface operators in 6d holomorphic Chern-Simons theory on $\mathbb{C}^3$. Computing their correlation function, we find a local operator at the intersection of the surface operators with a form reminiscent of the leading nontrivial term in the quasi-classical expansion of a rational $R$-matrix, as predicted by Costello. We provide evidence that this $R$-matrix-like operator satisfies a Yang-Baxter-type relation. We then derive the associated coproduct of the chiral algebra supported by surface operators from their OPE. We also study intersecting surface operators in 6d holomorphic BF theory and derive the local leading form of the corresponding $R$-matrix-like operator. When this theory is placed on twistor space, where it describes the self-dual sector of 4d Yang-Mills theory, this operator is expected to provide a local building block for quantum integrable structures anticipated in that setting.

</details>
